### [1] [Optimal Transport for Network Comparison: A Review with Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27500)
*James Hyun,François G. Meyer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用最优传输理论比较无向无权重图的三种主要距离：Wasserstein、Gromov-Wasserstein和Bures-Wasserstein距离，并探讨了它们的闭式解、传输计划的解释能力以及基于拉普拉斯谱的界，最后在合成网络聚类和真实时间序列网络异常检测中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输在网络比较中是一个新兴研究领域，与标准图度量不同，它不仅计算图之间的不相似性，还提供解释图如何互相变形（morph）的传输计划。本文旨在系统综述三种基于最优传输的图距离方法，填补该领域缺乏统一比较和理论分析的空白。

Method: 本文综述了三种最优传输距离：（1）Wasserstein距离，利用一维节点特征概率分布推导闭式解；（2）Gromov-Wasserstein距离，通过传输计划揭示图扰动后影响距离的关键节点；（3）Bures-Wasserstein距离，推导基于拉普拉斯谱的上下界以避免完整谱分解。此外，在合成网络数据集上进行聚类任务，在真实世界时间序列网络上进行异常检测任务来评估这些距离。

Result: 通过闭式解的分析展示了Wasserstein距离在一维情形下的可计算性；证明了Wasserstein和Gromov-Wasserstein的传输计划能够识别对图扰动后距离变化贡献最大的特定节点；推导的Bures-Wasserstein谱界避免了昂贵的全谱分解；实验评估验证了这些距离在聚类和异常检测任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 最优传输距离不仅提供图之间的定量不相似性度量，还通过传输计划提供可解释的定性洞察。三种距离各有优劣：Wasserstein计算简单但表达能力有限，Gromov-Wasserstein能捕获结构差异且传输计划可解释，Bures-Wasserstein通过谱界实现了计算效率与理论保证的平衡。

Abstract: Network comparison using optimal transport is a growing area of research in network science. Unlike standard graph metrics, optimal transport computes both network dissimilarity and a transport plan that explains how one graph morphs into another. In this paper, we review how optimal transport compares undirected, unweighted graphs using three primary distances: the Wasserstein, Gromov-Wasserstein, and Bures-Wasserstein distances. We examine the closed form of the Wasserstein distance in one dimension via node feature probability distributions, and show how the transport plans of the Wasserstein and Gromov-Wasserstein distances capture which specific nodes influence the distance after graph perturbation. For the Bures-Wasserstein distance, we derive bounds using Laplacian spectra to bypass full spectral decompositions. Finally, we evaluate these distances using a synthetic network dataset for clustering and a real-world time series network for anomaly detection.

</details>


### [2] [Towards a mathematical theory of superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27540)
*Michael I. Ivanitskiy,John Jasper,Emily J. King,Dustin G. Mixon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用框架理论和压缩感知的数学工具，发展了神经网络中"叠加"现象（superposition）的数学理论。通过过一个过完备字典W编码稀疏二元特征向量x，并使用ReLU(W^T W x + b)进行特征恢复。论文在随机支撑和最坏情况支撑两种设定下证明了多个恢复定理，包括近紧致低相干字典的高概率支撑恢复保证、尖锐可计算的最坏情况支撑恢复判据，以及实等角紧框架的精确恢复阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络中的叠加（superposition）现象——即网络用高维激活向量表示远超维度数的特征——在经验上已被广泛观察，但缺乏严格的数学分析基础。作者希望用框架理论和压缩感知的工具为该现象建立严谨的数学理论，从而在理论层面解释和理解神经网络特征表示的机制。

Method: 将特征恢复建模为：稀疏二元向量x通过过完备字典W编码，恢复通过ReLU(W^T W x + b)实现。利用框架理论（如近紧致低相干字典、等角紧框架）和压缩感知技术，分别在随机支撑设定和最坏情况支撑设定下推导支撑恢复定理。对实等角紧框架的情形，通过刻画Gram矩阵中符号分布的创新性理论结果，推导出精确的恢复阈值。

Result: 在随机支撑设定下，为近紧致低相干字典建立了高概率支撑恢复保证，期望稀疏度可达O(d/log n)量级。在最坏情况支撑设定下，给出了尖锐且可计算的支撑恢复判据，并应用于高斯随机矩阵和等角紧框架。对n>d+1的实等角紧框架，以相干性为参数确定了精确的恢复阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作为神经网络中的叠加现象提供了严格的数学基础，建立了从特征编码到恢复的完整理论框架。特别地，对实等角紧框架给出的Gram矩阵符号分布刻画具有独立的框架理论价值，为后续研究提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We develop a mathematical theory of superposition in neural networks using tools from frame theory and compressed sensing. In our model, a sparse binary vector \(x\) of active features is encoded through an overcomplete dictionary \(W\), and feature recovery is performed by applying \(\operatorname{ReLU}(W^\top W x+b)\) with an appropriate bias vector \(b\). We prove several recovery theorems for this model. In the random-support setting, we establish high-probability support recovery for nearly tight, low-coherence dictionaries, with guarantees when the expected sparsity is up to order \(d/\log n\). In the worst-case support setting, we give a sharp and computable criterion for which sparsity levels permit support recovery. We apply this criterion to Gaussian random matrices and equiangular tight frames. For real equiangular tight frames with \(n>d+1\), we determine the exact recovery threshold in terms of the coherence. The proof of this result for real equiangular tight frames relies on a novel characterization---which should be of independent interest to frame theorists---of the distribution of signs in the Gram matrix.

</details>


### [3] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28179)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究风险规避决策问题，其中智能体在系统状态不确定时选择动作，风险通过优化的确定性等价（OCE）指标衡量（涵盖均值-方差风险与CVaR）。作者刻画了已知分布下的最优策略，并证明其对于CVaR退化为基于预测集的解，为共形预测类预测集提供了操作性解释。对于未知分布，提出基于合成似然模型与留出校准数据的数据驱动校准策略，实现OCE风险的高概率控制，并在两个无线波束成形场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际决策中，智能体常面临系统状态不确定性的风险，需要一种通用的风险度量框架来指导决策。OCE指标统一了多种风险准则，但其最优策略的解析形式及未知分布下的数据驱动校准方法尚不明确。

Method: 首先在已知分布下推导OCE风险下最优策略的刻画，并通过理论分析证明CVaR情形下最优策略可简化为预测集形式，从而联系共形预测。随后针对未知分布，提出一种基于合成似然模型和留出校准数据的数据驱动校准策略，构建合成数据集以估计风险并实现高概率的OCE风险控制。

Result: 理论结果建立了OCE-最优策略的解析刻画，并揭示了CVaR情形下最优策略与预测集（共形预测）之间的深层联系。实验在两个无线波束成形场景中验证了所提数据驱动校准方法能够有效控制OCE风险，达到高概率保证。

Conclusion: 该工作为风险规避决策提供了统一的OCE理论框架，架起了鲁棒优化、风险度量和共形预测之间的桥梁，并为未知分布下的风险控制提供了实用的数据驱动方案。

Abstract: We study risk-averse decision making, in which an agent selects actions while being uncertain about the true system state. The risk is measured via optimized certainty equivalent (OCE) metrics, which generalize popular criteria such as mean-variance risk and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR). We characterize the optimal policy under known distributions, and show that it reduces to a prediction set-based solution for the CVaR. This provides an operational interpretation of conformal prediction-type prediction sets. For unknown distributions, we develop a data-driven calibration strategy, based on a synthetic model for the likelihood and held-out calibration data, yielding high-probability control of the OCE risk. The approach is evaluated on two wireless beamforming settings.

</details>


### [4] [I-FLOP: Fast Learning of Order and Parents from Interventional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28245)
*Liuting Chen,Alex Markham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文将FLOP算法从观测数据扩展到干预数据，提出I-FLOP算法。通过调整Hauser和Bühlmann的干预BIC分数以适配FLOP的迭代Cholesky分数更新机制，I-FLOP在样本极限下能恢复与数据生成DAG相同的干预马尔可夫等价类中的DAG，并在真实与模拟干预数据上表现优于现有因果结构学习算法，同时运行时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLOP算法在观测数据上具有速度快、性能好的优势，但尚未扩展到干预数据场景。干预数据包含更丰富的因果信息，能够细化因果方向的识别，因此需要将FLOP的高效性推广到干预数据设置中。

Method: 将FLOP算法从观测数据扩展至干预数据，采用Hauser和Bühlmann (2012)提出的干预BIC评分函数，并对其加以调整以兼容FLOP中基于迭代Cholesky分解的分数更新机制，从而保持FLOP的计算效率优势。

Result: 理论分析表明，在样本量趋于无穷时，I-FLOP能够恢复与数据生成DAG处于同一干预马尔可夫等价类的DAG。实验对比中，I-FLOP在真实和模拟干预数据上，在性能和运行时间两方面均优于现有因果结构学习算法。

Conclusion: I-FLOP成功将FLOP算法的效率优势引入干预数据因果结构学习领域，在保证理论正确性（干预马尔可夫等价类层面的可识别性）的同时，实现了优于现有方法的性能与更短的运行时间。

Abstract: We extend the FLOP (fast learning of order and parents) algorithm recently proposed by Wienöbst et al. (2026) from observational to interventional data. In particular, we use the interventional BIC score of Hauser and Bühlmann (2012), adapting it to be used with the iterative Cholesky-based score updates that are partly responsible for FLOP's speed. We show that, in the sample limit, I-FLOP recovers a DAG in the same interventional Markov equivalence class as the data-generating DAG. We compare I-FLOP to existing causal structure learning algorithms on real and simulated interventional data, where it performs favorably in terms of both performance and run time.

</details>


### [5] [Localizing Global Discrepancies: Marginal Contributions and Contextual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28375)
*Tommaso dorigo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过条件/边际贡献将观测值定位到全局失拟（goodness-of-fit）统计量中的框架，将重采样诊断与数据估值联系到投影理论和事件级异常检测。对称统计量下固定大小替换等价于中心化条件定位；U-统计量的加性分数等于第一阶Hoeffding/Hájek贡献。该方法还带来更高效的估计器，在LHC奥林匹克异常检测基准上达到0.9993的相关性。此外，论文探讨了上下文是否包含超出事件自身特征的信息，在共享潜在变量的玩具模型中证明了跨事件依赖可保留分类信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局拟合优度和失拟统计量能判断样本是否偏离参考分布，但无法识别是哪些观测驱动了这种偏离。作者希望解决这一"定位问题"（localization problem），为每个观测分配其在随机统计上下文中的条件或边际贡献，以精确找出异常驱动观测。

Method: （1）提出在每个观测的条件分布下分配条件或边际贡献的框架，将重采样诊断与数据估值统一到投影理论中。（2）对对称统计量证明固定大小替换等价于中心化条件定位；对U-统计量证明加性分数对应第一阶Hoeffding/Hájek贡献；对平滑分布泛函连接到影响函数（influence function）；对无偏已知背景MMD精确退化为MMD witness。（3）提出匹配上下文减法（matched-context subtraction）和成对MMD事件包含项作为更高效的定位估计器。（4）通过共享潜在变量玩具模型区分上下文的两种作用：高效定位全局失拟 vs 提供额外类别信息。

Result: 在LHC奥林匹克异常检测基准上，成对估计器以预测的1/(Rm^2)标度收敛到直接经验MMD witness；在m=1000、R=5×10^6时达到0.9993的相关性和基本相同的AUC。在共享潜在变量玩具模型中，单事件信号和背景分布完全相同（孤立事件AUC=0.5），但跨事件依赖保留下来的判别信息可被集成估计恢复，而独立潜在变量对照无法恢复。

Conclusion: 本文建立的定位框架统一了重采样诊断、数据估值与投影理论/事件级异常检测，提供了高效且理论上可扩展的估计器。同时揭示了上下文（context）的两重角色：一是高效定位全局失拟的功能性角色，二是当备择假设包含共享结构时提供额外类别信息的信息性角色。这为异常检测中"事件孤立vs上下文联合"的范式选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Global goodness-of-fit and discrepancy statistics can establish that a sample departs from a reference distribution without identifying which observations drive the departure. We develop a framework for this localization problem by assigning to each observation its conditional or marginal contribution across random statistical contexts. This connects resampling diagnostics and data valuation to projection theory and event-level anomaly detection. For symmetric statistics, fixed-size replacement is exactly equivalent to centered conditional localization. For U-statistics, the addition score equals the first Hoeffding/Hájek contribution; for smooth distributional functionals it is related at leading order to the influence function; and for unbiased known-background MMD it reduces exactly to the MMD witness.
  This viewpoint also yields more efficient estimators. Matched-context subtraction removes fluctuations unrelated to the observation, while for pairwise MMD the event-containing terms give a simple localizer. On the LHC Olympics anomaly-detection benchmark, the pair estimator converges to the direct empirical MMD witness with the predicted 1/(Rm^2) scaling, where m is batch size and R the number of batches. At m=1000 and R=5x106 it reaches correlation 0.9993 with essentially identical AUC.
  We also ask when context contains information beyond an event's own features. In a shared-latent toy model, the full single-event signal and background distributions are identical by construction, forcing isolated-event AUC=0.5. Discriminating information survives only in cross-event dependence induced by the shared latent parameter; the ensemble recovers this information, whereas an independent-latent control does not. This separates two roles of context: efficient localization of a global discrepancy and genuinely additional class information when the alternative contains shared structure.

</details>


### [6] [Learning between the peaks: sharp asymptotics for kernel ridge regression under power-law anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28564)
*Lorenzo Rizzi,Arie Wortsman Zurich,Bruno Loureiro*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了各向异性高斯数据下核岭回归的核谱与泛化误差，在多项式高维区间n=Θ(d^κ)中导出了渐近精确表达式，揭示了弱各向异性（0<α<1）与强各向异性（α>1）下学习曲线的不同行为，以及目标函数与数据主方向的对齐程度如何决定偏差转变和方差特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核学习理论大多假设各向同性（isotropic）输入分布，而真实数据通常具有各向异性结构，其协方差以幂律衰减。理解输入几何（协方差谱衰减指数α）如何影响核谱与泛化误差，是弥合理论有效性与实际数据差距的关键。

Method: 在多项式内积核与各向异性高斯数据设定下，利用多项式高维区间n=Θ(d^κ)的渐近分析，推导核谱与泛化误差的精确渐近表达式，分别处理弱各向异性（0<α<1）与强各向异性（α>1）情形，并专门分析单指标目标下的对齐效应。

Result: 弱各向异性下方差仍在整数样本复杂度κ∈N处出现峰值但随α增强而衰减；目标与主方向强对齐时偏差在分数样本复杂度处发生转换。强各向异性下有效维数恒定，方差在无脊插值下平台化或在固定脊惩罚下以显式速率消失；偏差转变由目标衰减率决定，低于阈值时学习是突变的，高于阈值时偏差以恢复经典源与容量速率的幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 各向异性的输入协方差几何从根本上重塑核特征与泛化性质，目标与数据主方向的相对对齐程度决定了各向异性对学习的影响方式，这为理解实际高维数据下核方法的性能提供了更精确的理论框架。

Abstract: We study kernel ridge regression under anisotropic Gaussian data, where the input covariance decays as a power law with exponent $α\geq 0$ for polynomial inner-product kernels. We derive asymptotically sharp expressions for the kernel spectrum and the generalization error in the polynomial high-dimensional regime $n=Θ(d^κ)$, revealing how anisotropy reshapes the learning curves. For weak anisotropy ($0<α<1$), the problem remains effectively high-dimensional and retains some features of the isotropic case, while departing from it in others: the variance still peaks at integer sample complexities $κ\in\mathbb{N}$, but these peaks are progressively damped as $α$ grows; meanwhile, for targets strongly aligned with the data's principal directions, the bias drops at fractional sample complexities, decoupling the bias transitions from the interpolation peaks. For strong anisotropy ($α> 1$), the effective dimension of the problem is constant, and the variance stops depending on sample size altogether, plateauing under ridgeless interpolation or vanishing at an explicit rate under fixed ridge penalty. The bias undergoes a sharp transition governed by the target's decay rate: below a threshold, learning is abrupt rather than gradual; above it, the bias decays as a power law that recovers the classical source and capacity rates. We finally specialize these results to single-index targets, showing how the alignment of the index with the data's principal directions determines the effect of anisotropy on learning. Together, our results clarify how the input geometry shapes the kernel features and fundamentally impacts its generalization properties.

</details>

### [7] [Adaptive workforce exploration in complex productivity landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27656)
*Mateus F. B. Granha,Igor V. G. de Oliveira,André L. M. Vilela,Chao Wang,Paulo R. A. Campos*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于NK模型（适应度地形/崎岖景观概念），研究组织劳动力分工的动态演化。模型包含两类员工——专职某单一任务的专家型（specialists）和每个时间步随机选择任务的通才型（generalists），通过群体选择算法揭示了非线性自适应动力学的涌现，探讨任务依赖产生的生产力景观崎岖度如何影响组织优化分工和满足市场需求的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业化与任务分配是提升组织效率和创新的核心机制，但任务间的相互依赖如何影响劳动力分工的演化尚不清晰。作者希望借鉴进化生物学中广泛应用的NK模型框架，将任务依赖关系引入组织分工研究，以理解组织如何在复杂任务环境中动态优化劳动配置。

Method: 以NK模型为理论基础构建组织劳动力分工模型，模型中包含两类员工：专家型（固定承担单一任务）和通才型（每个时间步随机选择任务）。通过调节任务相互依赖程度来塑造生产力景观的崎岖度，并采用群体选择算法（group selection algorithms）模拟组织的适应性和优化过程。

Conclusion: 公司可通过理解任务间依赖关系所塑造的生产力景观特征，动态调整专家与通才的配置比例和策略，以更好地适应市场需求并促进创新。该框架为组织管理中的劳动力分工决策提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Specialization and task allocation enhance efficiency and innovation across diverse systems, from biological organisms to socioeconomic institutions. The evolution of task distribution and its influence on organizational productivity encapsulate the dynamics between task dependencies and adaptive strategies. We explore the organizational division of labor, inspired by the NK model of rugged landscapes, which is widely applied in evolutionary biology, and incorporate interdependencies among the attributes of technical experts within an organization. Our model considers two types of employees characterized by their task allocation strategies: specialists, who are permanently assigned to a single task, and generalists, who stochastically select a task at each time step. We investigate how the ruggedness of the productivity landscape, shaped by task interdependency, affects the organization's capacity to optimize labor division and meet market demands. Using group selection algorithms, we reveal the emergence of nonlinear adaptive dynamics, providing insights into how companies can adapt their strategies to meet market demands and foster innovation.

It is shown that a He-3 bound state localized at the perimeter of sufficiently small (a few tens of atoms) clusters exists not just on Cs, as previously reported, but on all alkali substrates.

TL;DR: 本文研究幺正多体系统动力学中截断传播子的主特征值（Ruelle-Pollicott共振），利用CMV矩阵（单位圆上的正交多项式理论）获得精确结果，并发现两种弛豫相：第一相弛豫源于局域算符演化为非局域算符，第二相因大算符回流而弛豫更快，且该相中RP共振与截断传播子特征值不一致，而是隐藏在病态特征值环内。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用CMV矩阵（幺正传播子的正则形式）作为数学框架，结合单位圆上的正交多项式理论。对最简单的单边移位加杂质的CMV类，通过三种独立方法（截断传播子、解析延拓预解式、rigged Hilbert空间方法）交叉验证获得闭式结果；对更现实的渐近移位动力学CMV矩阵，依据正交多项式理论识别两种相并分析其弛豫机制。

Result: (1) 对最简单CMV类，三种求解RP共振的方法结果一致；(2) 识别出两种相：第一相弛豫源于局域算符演化为渐增非局域算符且回流可忽略；第二相因大算符回流贡献而弛豫更快；(3) 第二相中RP共振不再等于截断传播子的特征值，而是隐藏在病态特征值环内部。

Conclusion: CMV矩阵为研究幺正多体系统RP共振提供了精确的计算框架；揭示了RP共振与截断传播子特征值在部分参数区间的偏差，表明求解RP共振需要更精细的数学处理，并通过回流机制解释了加速弛豫的物理图像。

Abstract: Leading eigenvalues of the truncated propagator, known as Ruelle-Pollicott (RP) resonances, are an elegant way of addressing the dynamics of unitary many-body systems. We study unitary propagators in their canonical form, known in the mathematical literature as the CMV matrices, and obtain a number of exact results for RP resonances and the associated norm-diverging eigenvectors. For the simplest CMV class describing a unilateral shift with an impurity, motivated by operator dynamics in dual-unitary circuits, we obtain closed-form results and in particular show that the three independent ways of obtaining RP resonances -- the truncated propagator, analytic continuation of the resolvent, and the rigged Hilbert space approach -- all give the same results. In more realistic CMV matrices, in which shift-like operator dynamics characteristic of chaotic systems is only asymptotic, we rely on the rich theory of orthogonal polynomials on the unit circle and identify two phases. In the first phase, relaxation occurs due to local operators effectively evolving into increasingly nonlocal ones with negligible backflow. Especially interesting is the second phase, which, surprisingly, exhibits faster relaxation because of contributions from the backflow of large operators. Additionally, in the second phase, RP resonances are not equal to the eigenvalues of the truncated propagator, instead, they are ``hidden'' within a ring of ill-conditioned eigenvalues.

</details>

Conclusion: 面向特定任务设计的热力学计算机在热力学运行方面保留相当大的自由度，表明其运行机制具有灵活性，不必牺牲精度或效率。

Abstract: Thermodynamic computers are stochastic physical devices designed to perform calculations at the thermal energy scale. Their operation is constrained by the equations of stochastic thermodynamics, among which are a set of bounds, known as speed limits, that relate a thermodynamic computer's run time to its computational progress and the heat it dissipates. Using the Wasserstein speed limit we assess the thermodynamic efficiency of a simulation model of a thermodynamic computer trained to perform a standard machine-learning classification task. On this task the thermodynamic computer is as capable as a simple multilayer perceptron. We show that different inference protocols allow the computer to operate within 40\% of the thermodynamic limit of efficiency without loss of accuracy, or to perform inference increasingly rapidly at fixed accuracy and thermodynamic efficiency. These results indicate that a thermodynamic computer designed for a particular task retains considerable freedom in its thermodynamic operation.

Abstract: Can quenched disorder generate ordering in driven systems? Using a recently proposed hydrodynamic model, we show that sufficiently long-ranged quenched disorder can induce long-range order in two-dimensional (2D) nonreciprocal XY systems, even when the clean system exhibits only short-range order at finite noise. Active surfaces tangentially advected by quenched velocities, governed by the same hydrodynamic equation, become statistically flat with super- or subdiffusive relaxation. In three dimensions (3D), quenched disorder combined with nonreciprocity produces a novel transition between strong-coupling and asymptotically noninteracting regimes, supporting either long- or short-range order. In both 2D and 3D, the exponents are nonuniversal, which vary continuously with the degree of transversality of the quenched disorder. The degree of transversality of the quenched disorder can be tuned to induce transitions in the model for fixed disorder and noise variances.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文通过强化学习智能体实验发现，群体的聚集行为可以不依赖直接的社会奖励（如同类对齐或接近），而是作为个体寻找可更新目标的纯个体奖励的副产品涌现出来。随着视觉范围的增加，智能体从环境调谐的个体搜索过渡到尺度无关的集体搜索，这一转变与空间聚集的起始同步发生。一个最小分析首次通过模型重现了该转变，揭示了间接、资源驱动的奖励是涌现集体现象的通用路径。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 使用强化学习智能体进行模拟实验：初始执行随机游走的觅食者被赋予纯个体奖励（找到可补充的目标），它们只能感知同类（conspecifies）而永远看不见目标本身。通过变化视觉范围（visual range）观察行为转变。此外构建了一个最小的解析首次通过模型（first-passage model）来解释和重现个体搜索策略向集体搜索策略的转变。

Result: 发现随着视觉范围增大，智能体经历一个急剧的交叉（sharp crossover）：从环境调谐的个体搜索转变为尺度无关的集体搜索，该交叉与空间聚集的起始时间点吻合。聚集相作为最优觅食的副产品出现，不需要任何直接的分组奖励。解析模型成功重现了这一过渡。

Conclusion: 研究识别出间接的、资源驱动的奖励机制是涌现集体现象的一种普适路径。集体行为可以仅由个体追求资源最优化的连带效应产生，无需进化或设计层面的直接社交目标，这为理解生物群体行为涌现提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Collective behaviour in living systems is usually modelled as the outcome of a \emph{direct} social drive: agents are rewarded, or hard-wired, to align with or approach their neighbours. Here we show that aggregation can instead emerge from an \emph{indirect} objective. We let reinforcement learning foragers, initially performing a random walk, optimize their dynamics from a purely individual reward for finding replenishable targets, while perceiving only their conspecifics and never the targets themselves. As the visual range grows, the agents undergo a sharp crossover from an environment-tuned individual search to a scale-agnostic collective one, and this crossover coincides with the onset of spatial aggregation. Thus a collective phase arises as a by-product of optimal foraging, without any direct reward for grouping. A minimal analytical first-passage model reproduces the transition as a crossover between the two search strategies. Our results identify indirect, resource-driven reward as a generic route to emergent collective phenomena.

Abstract: We generalize a one-dimensional random sequential adsorption model (RSA) of charged unit segments in which the deposition position follows a beta kernel determined by the charges bounding the available gap and those of the incoming particle. The interaction parameter \(0\leqλ<1\) interpolates between uniform car parking and strongly localized deposition. We derive a four component generating function equation that provides exact recursions for the complete occupation statistics, although only the mean and variance are analyzed in detail here. Exact finite-shell solutions serve as benchmarks for the Gauss--Jacobi quadrature and Monte Carlo simulations. We prove that, although boundary charges affect finite size behavior, the four boundary states have the same asymptotic mean and variance densities, consequently, we prove that the jammed density is self-averaging. Numerical results show that charge selectivity increases the coverage, especially for mixtures dominated by opposite endpoint charges, which also exhibit strongly reduced fluctuations near \(λ=1\). The model provides a tractable connection between uniform RSA, dynamically generated disorder, and interaction-driven deposition.

Abstract: We investigate the action of the affine Yangian $Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1)$ on the singular cohomology of the moduli space of stable sheaves $\mathcal{M}$ on a smooth projective surface $S$. Through an intersection-theoretic analysis of nested moduli spaces, we obtain a proof of the representation $$Y_{t_1,t_2}(\widehat{\mathfrak{gl}}_1) \curvearrowright H_{\mathcal{M}}.$$ This intersection-theoretic approach extends classical constructions to the full Yangian structure and circumvents the $K$-theoretic machinery traditionally required to establish this action.

  (5) For any $k\in\{d+1,...,n\}$ and for any projection $π\colon \mathbb{C}^{n}\to \mathbb{C}^{k}$ such that $π^{-1}(0)\cap C_{\mathbb{C}}(X,\infty)=\{0\}$ and $Y=π(X)$, $\mathcal{H}^{2d}(Z(Y,\infty))<\mathcal{H}^{2d}(\mathbb{C}P^{d})$.

Abstract: Perturbed plane waves are fundamental objects in scattering theory on Euclidean space and asymptotically Euclidean spaces. These are specified using a two-faced compactification $X\hookleftarrow \mathbb{R}^d$, with one face for each asymptotic regime. We prove full polyhomogeneity. Abstract: We study a two-species model for tissue growth in which both populations are transported by an external drift and by a velocity potential determined through Brinkman's law. The pressure is generated by a stiff constitutive relation depending on the total density. Our main result establishes the incompressible limit as the stiffness exponent tends to infinity, in arbitrary space dimension and for merely integrable initial data. The limit system consists of the two balance laws coupled to Brinkman's equation, the hard-congestion constraint $0\leq n_\infty\leq 1$, the graph relation $p_\infty(1-n_\infty)=0$, and the corresponding complementarity relation. A key point of the analysis is a new $L^2$-based compactness theory that avoids both uniform $L^\infty$-bounds on the pressure and the kinetic reformulation used in earlier approaches. We first construct weak solutions for bounded data and then remove the boundedness assumption by means of a weighted compactness argument inspired by Bresch--Jabin. We also prove an Aubin--Lions--Simon type lemma based on oscillation control, yielding time continuity of the constructed solutions. Finally, a refined dissipation estimate for the Bresch--Jabin compactness functional gives strong compactness of the pressure in the stiff limit and implies a regularising effect: the limiting pressure is bounded even when the approximating pressures are only integrable.

Abstract: We study the bifurcation of two-dimensional Darcy-Bénard convection (DBC) in a rectangular domain, a canonical model for thermal convection in porous media with applications in geophysics and engineering. The momentum equation lacks advection and viscous dissipation, being regularized solely by a linear Darcy damping term. As a result, the linearized operator generates a semigroup that is neither analytic nor compact and the nonlinear term fails to be Lipschitz. The system is thus placed outside the scope of the standard center-manifold theorem.
  To overcome these obstructions, we develop a center-manifold reduction adapted to DBC system. Our main result is a constructive proof of the existence of the center manifold function h and local attractivity of the center manifold-the exponential convergence of small solutions toward it-without relying on analyticity of the full linear semigroup and Lipschitz nonlinearity. We circumvent these difficulties by exploiting the partially dissipative structure: the temperature equation is governed by the Laplacian, which generates an analytic semigroup and provides the smoothing needed to compensate for the lack of regularity in the velocity and the absence of global Lipschitz bounds. Through carefully designed inequalities, we establish both the construction of the center manifold function h and the exponential convergence of nearby solutions.
  Explicit approximations for the center manifold are derived in two scenarios-one simple eigenvalue and two distinct eigenvalues-yielding reduced systems of ordinary differential equations whose analysis determines the bifurcation type. Numerical simulations are presented to corroborate the theoretical results.

Abstract: We establish a weighted Wasserstein spectral gap for the three-dimensional damped cubic wave equation with genuinely finite rank Brownian forcing. Under a saturation condition, the gap holds with respect to the negative phase topology $\mathcal E_s=H^{-s}\times H^{-1-s}$ for every $0<s<1/2$, from which we deduce unique ergodicity and exponential mixing in the energy topology. Sharp geometric characterizations of the saturation condition are also obtained.
  The method we develop is a stable--compact asymptotic coupling mechanism for hypoelliptic dissipative SPDEs beyond the parabolic setting. Instead of relying on positive time smoothing or asymptotic gradient estimates, it reduces the infinite-dimensional obstruction to contraction to a compact defect in a weaker coupling topology. Dense Malliavin range then permits this defect to be compensated by a finite dimensional Cameron--Martin shift, producing a finite distance contraction on bounded Lyapunov cores. Together with a separate high energy contraction from dissipation, this yields a global weighted Wasserstein spectral gap.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 首先通过小参数$ε$下的适定性分析建立任意大时间区间的解的存在唯一性；随后利用两尺度均匀化方法，以经验测度描述夹杂物的空间分布和尺寸，引入$2$-Wasserstein距离控制经验测度的收敛，并区分临界情形（$α=3$）和非临界情形（$α∈(1,3)$）分别推导均匀化极限。

Result: 建立了任意大时间区间上的适定性；获得了定量均匀化极限，经验测度的演化由连续性方程刻画且收敛在$2$-Wasserstein距离下受控。临界情形$α=3$下Stokes系统收敛为Brinkman系统，边界层效应修改反应-扩散-平流方程的微观交换律；非临界情形$α∈(1,3)$下极限为Darcy型且交换律不变，所有结果带显式误差估计。

Conclusion: 夹杂物半径尺度$α$的取值决定了均匀化极限的定性差异：临界尺度$α=3$产生Brinkman型流动和修正的交换律，而非临界尺度保留Darcy型流动和原始交换律。文章给出了完全定量的收敛估计，为动态演化夹杂介质的均匀化分析提供了完整框架。

Abstract: We study a coupled Stokes-reaction-diffusion-advection system in a three dimensional domain perforated by a large number of evolving spherical inclusions with radii of order $\varepsilon^α$ for $α\in(1,3]$. Their centers are separated on the order of $\varepsilon$ but are not assumed to form a periodic lattice. The inclusions grow or shrink through an interfacial adsorption-desorption mechanism, so that the evolution of the geometry is coupled to the Stokes-reaction-diffusion-advection system.
  We first establish well-posedness on arbitrary large time intervals for sufficiently small $\varepsilon$. We then derive quantitative homogenization limits in terms of the limiting empirical measure describing the spatial distribution and size of the inclusions. Its evolution is governed by a continuity equation in the radius variable, and the convergence of the empirical measures is controlled in the $2$-Wasserstein distance.
  In the critical case, $α=3$, the Stokes system converges to a Brinkman system, while non-vanishing concentration boundary layers modify the microscopic exchange law for the reaction-diffusion-advection equation. For $α\in(1,3)$, the limit is of Darcy type and the original exchange law is retained. The homogenization results are quantitative and provide explicit error estimates.

</details>

*Ehsan Abedi,Zhenhao Li,Timo Schultz*

TL;DR: 本文在一般度量空间上引入了基于测度值导子（measure-valued derivations）的连续性方程概念，用以刻画概率测度空间中关于1-Wasserstein距离绝对连续乃至有界变差（BV）的曲线，并在\mathbb{R}^n上与Almi-Rossi-Savaré提出的一致性方程（奇异性通量）保持一致，最终给出了BV曲线的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续性方程通常需要光滑性或余切结构，难以统一描述度量空间上概率测度曲线的正则性（AC与BV）。作者旨在建立一种统一的框架，使度量空间中BV型概率测度曲线也能被连续性方程表征。

Method: 引入测度值导子的概念并发展其基本理论；基于该导子构造度量空间上的连续性方程，将其通量视作测度值导子（有限质量）；在\mathbb{R}^n上将该框架与Almi-Rossi-Savaré的奇异性通量连续性方程建立一致性，包括最小解的概念；通过扩展作者此前在BV曲线上的概率表示结果（Calc.Var.(2024)63:16），从概率表示构造出测度值导子（或通量测度）。

Result: 证明了在（扩展的）1-Wasserstein度量下，概率测度空间中的BV曲线恰好等价于满足以有限质量测度值导子为通量的连续性方程的曲线；同时建立了该框架与\mathbb{R}^n上已知理论（含最小解概念）的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种统一且完备的度量空间上概率测度BV曲线的刻画框架，将连续性方程、测度值导子与概率表示理论有机融合，并在欧氏空间中与已有理论互洽。

Abstract: We introduce a notion of continuity equation on metric spaces that is capable of describing curves of probability measures which are absolutely continuous, and more generally of bounded variation (BV), with respect to the 1-Wasserstein distance. This continuity equation is based on a notion of measure-valued derivations, whose basic theory is also developed in this paper. On $\mathbb{R}^n$, our formulation is consistent with the continuity equation with singular flux introduced by Almi--Rossi--Savaré (arXiv:2506.15333), including the corresponding notion of minimal solutions. In this work, we characterize BV-curves in the space of probability measures equipped with the (extended) 1-Wasserstein distance as those curves satisfying the continuity equation with a measure-valued derivation of finite mass. To this aim, we extend our previous work (Calc.Var.(2024)63:16) on probabilistic representations on BV-curves and construct from them measure-valued derivations (resp. flux measures) on geodesic metric spaces (resp. on $\mathbb{R}^n$).

</details>

### [54] [Marginal Coverage Credit Reduces Redundant Exploration in Parallel State-Entropy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27507)
*Junhao Cao,Hongyi Xia,Jianian Wu,Xiaopeng Yi,Lixia Huang,Ping Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCC-PGPSE方法，通过leave-one-policy-out覆盖度和状态所有者归属来估计每个策略的边际覆盖贡献，并将非负辅助内在奖励按贡献重新分配，以促进并行策略间的互补覆盖。在受控环境、七个公开离散状态基准及Room/Maze设置中，该方法在归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度上均优于Entropy基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGPSE虽然通过训练多个独立参数化策略来扩大状态空间覆盖，但其池化的团队熵指标只能衡量集体探索效果，无法识别哪些策略贡献了非冗余覆盖，导致策略间可能产生重复访问，缺乏互补性。

Method: 提出MCC-PGPSE，结合leave-one-policy-out（逐策略剔除）覆盖度与state-owner（状态所有者）专门化归属来估计每个策略的独立覆盖贡献，并据此将非负辅助内在奖励按贡献重新分配，保持奖励总量不变。这种分配机制旨在抑制冗余访问并促进互补覆盖。

Result: 在所有测试设置中，MCC-PGPSE相较于Entropy基线在归一化团队状态熵和状态支持度上均取得正向最终窗口增益。受控任务对比和固定公开套件的聚合结果具有统计显著性，五种子原始协议对比结果方向一致但未达显著。消融实验表明大部分增益来自leave-one-policy-out覆盖机制，而非非均匀权重、错误信用分配或神经新颖性本身。

Conclusion: 贡献条件化的辅助奖励分配是提升离散状态空间中并行策略互补覆盖的一种可解释且有效的方法，为并行智能体协调探索提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Policy Gradient for Parallel State Entropy maximization (PGPSE) expands state-space coverage by training independently parameterized policies in replicated copies of the same environment. However, its pooled team-entropy score measures only collective exploration and cannot identify policies that contribute non-redundant coverage. We introduce Marginal Coverage Credit for PGPSE (MCC-PGPSE), which combines leave-one-policy-out coverage with state-owner specialization to estimate policy-specific credit. MCC-PGPSE preserves PGPSE's pooled objective and redistributes non-negative auxiliary intrinsic rewards according to these credits without changing their total mass. This redistribution is designed to discourage redundant visitation and promote complementary coverage. We evaluated MCC-PGPSE in controlled environments, seven public discrete-state benchmarks, and representative Room and Maze settings from the original PGPSE protocol. Across all tested settings, MCC-PGPSE produced positive final window gains in normalized team state entropy and state support over the Entropy baseline. Controlled-task comparisons and the fixed-suite public aggregate were significant, whereas five-seed original-protocol comparisons were directionally consistent. Ablations and credit alignment controls indicate that most gains arise from leave-one-policy-out coverage rather than non-uniform weighting, mismatched credit, or neural novelty alone. These results support contribution-conditioned auxiliary reward allocation as an interpretable approach to improving complementary coverage among parallel policies in discrete state spaces.

</details>


### [55] [Quantization-Triggered Backdoors in Language Models: Cross-Quantizer Transferability and the Validation--Deployment Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27512)
*Jacopo Dardini,Claudio Stanzione,Giordano Colò,Giuseppe Fenza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量化行为等价类（QBECs）概念，形式化证明源精度模型的安全认证无法外推到量化部署后的行为等价性，并构建三阶段对抗微调框架，在满足源精度检查的模型中植入仅在INT8或4-bit压缩时激活的恶意后门。实验覆盖多语言编码器-解码器翻译模型与立场分类器，翻译模型在量化后从0%友敌翻转升至85.02%反转，立场分类器出现ΔBias=0.33的意识形态偏移，并发现后门持续性取决于量化方案与架构而非标称位宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前后训练量化被视作语义中立的部署优化，但量化是参数空间上的多对一映射，源精度下的认证无法保证部署配置中行为等价，形成结构性的验证-部署鸿沟。作者试图从理论上证明这一鸿沟并展示其可利用性，以警示源精度审计不足以保障边缘AI的可信性。

Method: (1) 形式化定义量化行为等价类（QBECs），并证明QBEC成员关系不蕴含行为等价性；(2) 设计三阶段对抗微调框架，将潜在恶意载荷嵌入模型，使其通过源精度检查但在量化后激活；(3) 在多语言编码器-解码器序列到序列模型（区别于以往仅覆盖decoder-only因果LM）上评估；(4) 在两个实操场景（战术机器翻译、政治内容分析）中进行测试；(5) 跨量化器迁移性分析，考察不同量化方案与架构下的攻击持久性。

Result: 后门翻译模型在修复的FP16下友敌腐败为零，但在量化后达到最高85.02%的反转率；配对立场分类器在压缩后测得ΔBias=0.33的意识形态偏移。跨量化器分析表明攻击持续性随量化方案与模型架构变化，而非仅由名义位宽决定。

Conclusion: 源精度审计本身无法排除量化触发的恶意行为，行为认证必须覆盖最终部署配置，这是实现可信边缘AI的必要条件。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is often treated as a semantically neutral optimization for edge deployment of Large Language Models. When a full-precision source checkpoint is evaluated and quantization is applied downstream without equivalent re-evaluation, this workflow creates a structural validation--deployment gap: because quantization is a many-to-one mapping over parameter space, source-precision certification does not guarantee behavioral equivalence in the deployed configuration. We formalize this gap through Quantization Behavioral Equivalence Classes (QBECs) and prove that QBEC membership does not imply behavioral equivalence, providing a theoretical basis for quantization-triggered backdoor attacks. Building on a three-stage adversarial fine-tuning framework, we embed latent malicious payloads into models that satisfy the source-precision checks used in our evaluation, yet activate targeted adversarial behavior upon INT8 or 4-bit compression. We evaluate this threat in two operationally motivated scenarios, tactical machine translation and political content analysis, extending prior work from decoder-only causal LMs to multilingual encoder-decoder sequence-to-sequence models. Results show that backdoored translation models move from zero measured friend--foe corruption at repaired FP16 to up to 85.02% inversion after quantization, and that a paired stance classifier measures an ideological shift of up to $Δ\mathrm{Bias}=0.33$ upon compression. A cross-quantizer transferability analysis further shows that attack persistence varies across quantization schemes and model architectures, rather than being determined by nominal bit-width alone. These findings demonstrate that source-precision auditing alone does not rule out quantization-triggered behavior and that the final deployed configuration must be included in behavioral certification for trustworthy edge AI.

</details>


### [56] [DAMP: Decay-Aware Mixed-Precision Recurrent-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27513)
*Tao Zhang,Jianchao Tan,Pingwei Sun,Yanqi Yu,Zixu Jiang,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAMP是首个针对Gated DeltaNet (GDN)和Kimi Delta Attention (KDA)语言模型中循环状态的后训练量化方法。它发现均匀量化在这些循环状态上表现差，并观察到量化误差能量集中在少数通道、状态通道的相对衰减强度在提示和任务间保持稳定。DAMP利用量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性在离线校准中识别高风险通道，以更高精度存储这些通道，其余用INT8。在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上，9.9 bits/state精度接近FP32基线，减少69.1%存储，加速循环状态更新核最高2.01倍，降低全模型TPOT最多10.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN和KDA等模型用固定大小的循环状态替代KV缓存以减少推理内存，但这些循环状态以FP32存储仍占用大量GPU显存，且更新过程受内存带宽限制，显著拖慢解码延迟。现有研究缺乏对循环状态量化的探索，而简单的均匀量化在复杂推理任务上精度损失严重，因此需要一种能兼顾存储压缩和精度保持的循环状态量化方案。

Method: DAMP首先通过校准分析发现两个关键洞察：(1) 量化误差能量集中于少量通道(子集)；(2) 状态通道的相对衰减强度在不同提示和任务间保持稳定。基于此，DAMP在离线校准阶段同时利用量化误差能量和基于衰减的持久性来识别高风险通道，对这些通道以更高精度(FP32或更高位宽)存储，其余通道量化为INT8，从而在压缩存储的同时保持精度。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B和Kimi-Linear-48B上，跨越数学推理、通用推理和代码生成六个基准的评估显示：DAMP在9.9 bits/state下平均精度接近FP32基线；减少循环状态存储69.1%；循环状态更新核加速最高2.01倍；全模型TPOT(单Token处理时间)降低最多10.9%。

Conclusion: DAMP证明了基于通道敏感性和衰减持久性识别的混合精度量化方案可以有效压缩GDN/KDA模型的循环状态存储，在几乎不损失推理精度的前提下显著降低存储开销并加速解码，为混合线性注意力架构的高效推理部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Softmax attention stores key and value vectors for every preceding token, causing inference memory to grow with sequence length. Recent language models incorporating Gated DeltaNet (GDN) or Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) reduce this cost by replacing the KV cache in most layers with fixed-size recurrent states. However, these recurrent states are commonly stored in FP32 and consume substantial GPU memory; their updates are memory-bandwidth bound and contribute significantly to decoding latency. To our knowledge, we are the first to study post-training quantization of recurrent states in GDN and KDA based language models. We find that uniform quantization provides a poor accuracy--storage trade-off: INT8 and FP8 already degrade accuracy on complex reasoning tasks, while INT4 and NVFP4 reduce it to near zero. We further find that most quantization-error energy is concentrated in a small subset of channels and that the relative decay strength of state channels remains stable across prompts and tasks. Motivated by these findings, DAMP uses both quantization-error energy and decay-based persistence to identify high-risk channels during offline calibration. It stores these channels at higher precision and the remainder in INT8. We evaluate DAMP on Qwen3.6-35B and Kimi-Linear-48B across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, general reasoning, and code generation. At 9.9 bits per state value, DAMP maintains average accuracy close to the FP32 baseline. DAMP reduces recurrent-state storage by 69.1%, accelerates the recurrent-state update kernel by up to 2.01x, and lowers full-model TPOT by up to 10.9%.

</details>


### [57] [A Deeper Analysis of Block-Sparse Featurizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27515)
*Alexandru-Iulius Jerpelea,Amith Ananthram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了块稀疏特征化器（BSF）的优缺点，发现其仍存在特征分裂和组合等经典SAE失败模式，并提出架构改进（如Tournament Top-K选择规则）以及将块范式扩展到跨编码器（crosscoder）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSF作为SAE的变体，其原子单元是块方向而非单一方向，专为视觉中常出现的低维流形特征设计。但BSF仍受经典SAE失败模式困扰，需要系统研究其优缺点并加以改进。

Method: 分析BSF相比SAE的优缺点，诊断其失败模式（特征分裂、组合等），提出多种架构改动，包括Tournament Top-K选择规则以减少特征分裂，并将块范式扩展到crosscoder架构。

Result: 提出的Tournament Top-K选择规则显著减少了特征分裂现象；块范式成功扩展到crosscoder，增强了跨编码器场景的能力。

Conclusion: 通过架构改进，BSF的性能得到提升，在保留块稀疏范式优势的同时缓解了特征分裂和组合等经典问题。

Abstract: The recently introduced block-sparse featurizer (BSF; Fel et al., 2026) is similar to a sparse autoencoder (SAE), but its atomic unit is a small subspace (a block of directions) rather than a single direction. It is designed for features that live on low-dimensional manifolds, which are especially frequent in vision. This work studies the BSF's strengths and weaknesses, finding how it still somewhat suffers from classic SAE failure modes, like feature splitting and composition. We propose several architectural changes to the BSF, including a Tournament Top-K selection rule that significantly reduces feature splitting, and we also extend the block paradigm to the crosscoder.

</details>


### [58] [When Muon Meets Task Interference: A Spectral Perspective on Continual Learning and Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27518)
*Shangge Liu,Yuehan Yin,Yinghuan Shi,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示持续学习（CL）和模型合并（MM）中的两个核心难题——灾难性遗忘和权重解纠缠误差——实际上是同一现象"任务干扰"的两种表现，并将其形式化为逐层的Frobenius内积。理论分析表明优化器通过控制权重更新的谱范数来调节任务干扰，并发现Muon优化器天然具备这一控制机制，在CL和MM基准上均显著优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习和模型合并在文献中通常被视为两个独立问题，分别用不同方法缓解，而基础优化器引入的几何性质往往被当作实现细节忽略。作者认为这两个困难本质上是同一现象——一个任务的有用参数更新会改变模型在另一个任务上的输出——应当统一形式化，并揭示优化器在其中扮演的核心角色。

Method: (1) 将CL和MM的困难统一形式化为"任务干扰"，简化为逐层Frobenius内积 <ΔW, J(x)>_F；(2) 理论上推导出上界，将权重更新的谱范数 ||ΔW||_2 分离为优化器可控制的干扰因子；(3) 进行逐模式（per-mode）分析，证明该上界能追踪经验干扰的主导部分；(4) 识别Muon优化器通过构造性设计调节谱范数，收紧CL和MM的干扰界。

Result: 用Muon替换AdamW后：在8任务模型合并基准上、跨3个CLIP骨干上精度最高提升+5.02点；在持续学习方面，Muon在10个类增量协议、3个任务增量协议以及11任务MTIL基准上均取得一致的正向收益。

Conclusion: CL和MM的困难是同一"任务干扰"现象的不同实例，优化器（特别是Muon）通过控制谱范数成为调节任务干扰的原则性手段。Muon是一种与现有解决方案互补的、以优化器为中心的途径，理论分析得到实验充分验证。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) and model merging (MM) both aim to obtain a single model that performs well across multiple tasks, challenged respectively by catastrophic forgetting and weight-disentanglement error. In the literature, these difficulties are merely treated separately and mitigated through a variety of solutions, while the geometry induced by the base optimizer is treated as an implementation detail. In this work, we show that the two difficulties are in fact two instances of the same phenomenon: a parameter update useful for one task shifts the model's outputs on another. We formalize this shared phenomenon as \textit{task interference} and reduce it to a common layer-wise Frobenius inner product $\langle ΔW_\ell, J_\ell(x)\rangle_F$. This quantity, in turn, is utilized to expose the role of the optimizer. We theoretically derive an upper bound that isolates the spectral norm $\|ΔW_\ell\|_2$ as an optimizer-controllable factor of task interference, and a per-mode analysis shows that this bound tracks the dominant part of the empirical interference. Specifically, we then identify the recent Muon optimizer as a mechanism that regulates this factor by construction. Our work reveals that its elegant control on spectral norm tightens the interference bound for both CL and MM, positioning Muon as a principled optimizer-centric approach complementary to existing solutions. Our theoretcal analysis is well validated by experimental results. Replacing the AdamW optimizer with Muon improves accuracy by up to +5.02 points on the eight-task model-merging benchmark across three CLIP backbones. For continual learning, Muon also delivers uniformly positive gains across ten class-incremental protocols, three task-incremental protocols, and the 11-task MTIL benchmark.

</details>


### [59] [Dandelion: A Spherical Flower for Neural Simulation of Planetary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27521)
*Till Muser,Giovanni Abati,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dandelion，一种球面版本的warp-based神经PDE求解器，通过在球面切平面上预测位移并沿大圆传输特征来实现空间混合，完全不使用卷积，而是在球谐域中进行层级池化。作者还发布了包含多种具有挑战性的原生球面PDE数据集的基准套件，实验表明Dandelion在所有数据集上表现最佳或次佳，且在高分辨率下优势更加明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多动力学过程都在球面上展开，但现有的科学机器学习架构默认基于欧几里得空间。将欧几里得架构应用于经纬网格会导致问题：笛卡尔卷积在高纬度产生畸变、2D FFT在傅里叶神经算子中错误假设双重周期性、ViT中的笛卡尔位置编码扭曲球面测地距离。因此需要原生球面的架构。

Method: Dandelion是Flower（一种warp-based神经PDE求解器）的球面版本。其每层预测切平面位移，并沿大圆路径传输特征；通过完全在球谐域中实现层级（hierarchical）池化来构建U-Net-like结构；不使用任何卷积，仅通过球面坐标变换（warp）实现空间混合。

Result: Dandelion在所有基准数据集上均取得最佳或次佳性能。与非warp基线相比，性能差距随分辨率增大而扩大：在256×512分辨率下，Dandelion和Flower2D在单步预测和滚动（rollout）任务中均占据前两名。

Conclusion: warp-based方法（如Dandelion）是处理球面PDE问题的有效范式，且其相对于传统卷积或谱方法基线的优势在高分辨率下更为显著。作者发布的球面PDE基准套件填补了现有数据集的空白，为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Many dynamical processes unfold on the sphere but the default scientific machine learning architectures are Euclidean. Applying these architectures on a regular lat-lon grid causes problems: Cartesian convolutions become distorted at high latitude; 2D FFTs in Fourier neural operators incorrectly assume double periodicity; Cartesian positional encodings in ViTs distort spherical geodesic distances. Recent work moves towards natively spherical primitives, including spherical convolutions (e.g., DeepSphere or DISCO), Spherical Fourier Neural Operators (SFNOs), and geodesic attention. Here we propose Dandelion, a spherical version of Flower, a warp-based neural PDE solver. Layers of Dandelion predict a tangent-plane displacement and transport features along great circles. We obtain a U-Net-like structure by implementing hierarchical pooling entirely in the spherical-harmonic domain. There are thus no convolutions: spatial mixing is achieved only through spherical coordinate changes, or warps. To compare Dandelion with existing spherical architectures, we release an evolving benchmark suite of challenging, natively-spherical PDE datasets including a modified Galewsky jet, anomalous chained turbulence, Cahn-Hilliard decomposition, spherical Riemann shocks, Held-Suarez dry atmospheric transport and global ocean dynamics. This new benchmark fills the gap in existing spherical datasets which are either too small and stylized, or much too large (ERA5) for model iteration. Dandelion is best or second-best on every dataset, and the gap to non-warp baselines widens with resolution: at $256\times 512$, Dandelion and Flower2D occupy the top two slots in both single-step prediction and rollout.

</details>


### [60] [Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27634)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CARSANN（曲率感知半径收缩的自适应最近邻分类）框架，通过估计局部流形曲率来动态调整每个邻居的空间支持范围，而非常规k-NN在全局使用固定邻域基数。在70多个OpenML数据集上验证了其有效性，平均平衡准确率从0.6506提升至0.7528，并通过统计检验确认改善显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统k-NN在整个特征空间中强制使用相同的邻域基数（k值），这对局部几何结构变化剧烈的流形数据而言是不充分的假设。现有自适应方法仅修改邻居数量或局部度量，但没有显式地调整局部证据的空间范围。作者希望利用局部流形曲率作为几何控制变量，自适应地调整每个邻域的空间支持范围。

Method: CARSANN首先使用TwoNN方法估计内在维度，并通过主成分分析（PCA）构建内在表示。随后基于形状算子（shape operator）公式估计局部平均曲率，曲率控制邻域尺度：高曲率区域被施加更强的半径收缩，近似平坦区域保留更广泛的空间支持。该方法不同于只修改邻居数量或局部度量的方法，而是显式调整局部证据的空间范围。

Result: 在70多个OpenML真实数据集上的实验表明，CARSANN一致性地优于标准k-NN，并与现有自适应最近邻方法具有竞争力。在受控比较中（相同基础邻域大小下），CARSANN在45个数据集中有40个取得了更高的平衡准确率，平均平衡准确率从0.6506提升至0.7528。该优势在固定k=5的k-NN比较中依然保持。Friedman和Nemenyi检验确认改进具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 局部流形曲率可以作为一种有效的几何控制变量来适应邻域支持范围，为基于基数（cardinality）的最近邻自适应方法提供了互补的范式。

Abstract: Nearest neighbor classification relies fundamentally on how locality is defined, yet conventional $k$-NN imposes the same neighborhood cardinality throughout the feature space. This assumption can be inadequate for data whose local geometry varies substantially across the underlying manifold. We introduce Curvature-Aware Radius Shrinkage for Adaptive Nearest Neighbor Classification (CARSANN), a geometry-driven framework that adapts the spatial support of each neighborhood according to local geometric complexity. CARSANN first estimates intrinsic dimensionality using TwoNN and constructs an intrinsic representation through principal component analysis. Local mean curvature is then estimated using a shape-operator-based formulation and controls neighborhood scale: highly curved regions receive stronger radius shrinkage, whereas approximately flat regions retain broader spatial support. Unlike methods that modify only the number of neighbors or the local metric, CARSANN explicitly adapts the spatial extent of local evidence. Experiments on more than 70 real-world OpenML datasets show that CARSANN consistently improves upon standard $k$-NN and is competitive with adaptive nearest-neighbor methods. In a controlled comparison using the same base neighborhood size, CARSANN achieves higher balanced accuracy on 40 of 45 datasets, increasing mean balanced accuracy from 0.6506 to 0.7528. The advantage also persists against $k$-NN with fixed $k=5$. Friedman and Nemenyi tests confirm that the improvements are statistically significant. These results indicate that local manifold curvature can serve as an effective geometric control variable for adapting neighborhood support, providing a complementary paradigm to cardinality-based nearest-neighbor adaptation.

</details>


### [61] [Self-Explainable Multi-Label Graph Neural Network for Correlated Evidence Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27574)
*Yingqi Feng,Yufei Tang,Min Shi,Xingquan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端自可解释的多标签图神经网络（SEMGNN），在统一的框架中同时进行多标签节点分类和边缘级解释生成，利用标签间相关性提升预测性能和解释质量，并在多个真实多标签网络上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数多标签图学习方法不具备训练时的解释能力；后验图解释器虽已存在，但未在多标签图学习中显式建模标签依赖的证据共享，尤其在标签对呈弱相关或负相关时，后验方法难以刻画不同标签之间的证据共享与分离关系。

Method: SEMGNN在一个统一框架和训练目标中联合学习一个预测器和一个稀疏边掩码解释器。利用标签-标签相关性来改进多标签节点分类并增强各个标签的解释，使得同一节点的不同标签可以由不同但连贯的结构和/或相关证据支持。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的多标签网络（涵盖社交网络、娱乐和生命科学领域）上的实验与对比表明，SEMGNN取得了具有竞争力或更优的预测性能，同时提供了更忠实、更紧凑的标签条件解释。

Conclusion: SEMGNN作为首个端到端自可解释的多标签图神经网络，成功解决了多标签学习中标签相关证据共享与分离的问题，在预测精度和解释质量上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multi-label graph learning intends to capture the intrinsic complexity of real-world applications, where one sample is often related to multiple groups or consists of multiple objects. To date, a handful of multi-label graph learning methods exist, but none of them integrate training-time interpretation capability. While post-hoc graph explainers have been developed, they do not explicitly model label-dependent evidence sharing in multi-label graph learners, especially when label pairs are weakly or negatively associated. As a result, post-hoc approaches may miss how evidence should be shared or separated across different labels. This paper advances a new end-to-end self-explainable multi-label graph neural network (SEMGNN), which aims to simultaneously classify multi-labeled nodes and identify edges significantly contributing to each target node w.r.t. predicted labels. Different from post-hoc methods, SEMGNN jointly learns a predictor and a sparse edge-mask explainer within a unified framework and training objective. Label-label correlations are used to improve multi-label node classification and enhance individual label explanations, so that different labels of a node can be supported by distinct yet coherent structural and/or correlated evidence. Experiments and comparisons on synthetic and real-world multi-label networks, in social networking, entertainment, and life sciences, show that SEMGNN achieves competitive or improved predictive performance while providing more faithful and compact label-conditioned explanations.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Pairwise Graphs in Science: Hypergraph Adaptive Wavelet Operators for Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27883)
*Rajat Sarkar,Venkataramana Runkana,Souvik Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO是一种将物理域提升为超图并在谱小波域中学习的神经算子。它通过Chebyshev多项式小波滤波器避免显式超图拉普拉斯特征分解，以线性稀疏矩阵成本获得局部谱核，并通过可训练的二元小波尺度正则化实现适应不同PDE的多尺度谱覆盖。在2D/3D结构化与非结构化网格基准上，HALO在频域、Transformer、DeepONet、状态空间和基于图的基线上取得最佳或接近最佳的精度，并能在具有数十万点的工业气动网格上与最强固定离散化Transformer持平或更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在学习物理系统解算子时空变且需自回归稳定性的问题上面临挑战；许多方法依赖规则网格，难以处理真实模拟中的非结构化网格或点云；图神经算子虽能处理这些域，但只能捕捉成对边关系，无法直接建模网格单元、局部邻域或守恒体积之间的群体耦合。

Method: HALO将物理域提升为超图结构，并在超图的谱小波域中学习。它利用Chebyshev多项式构造小波滤波器，避免显式超图拉普拉斯的特征分解，以线性稀疏矩阵运算成本实现局部化谱核。可训练的二元小波尺度通过正则化趋向紧框架覆盖，使频率响应能自适应每种PDE，同时保持稳定的多尺度谱覆盖。

Result: 在2D和3D的结构化与非结构化离散化基准测试中，HALO在与频率法、Transformer、DeepONet、状态空间法和基于图的方法等基线对比中取得最佳或接近最佳精度，并维持稳定的多步自回归滚动。在工业气动网格（数十万点）上，其性能与最强固定离散化Transformer相当或更优，同时保持分辨率等变性。

Conclusion: HALO通过超图与小波谱学习有效解决了非结构化网格上神经算子难以捕捉群体耦合和保持自回归稳定性的问题，为复杂几何上的物理系统建模提供了兼具精度、稳定性和分辨率等变性的通用方案。

Abstract: Physical systems are often modeled by solution operators that map input fields, parameters, geometries, or past states to steady or future physical states. Learning these maps is difficult, especially for time-dependent systems that must assimilate history and remain stable under autoregressive rollout. Many neural operators work best on regular, structured grids, while realistic simulations often require unstructured meshes or point clouds to resolve complex geometries; in such settings, grid-centric representations can lose accuracy. Graph neural operators handle these domains through message passing or spectral graph filtering, but pairwise edges do not directly capture group-wise couplings among mesh cells, local neighborhoods, or conservation volumes. We introduce the Hypergraph Adaptive waveLet Operator (HALO), which lifts the domain to a hypergraph and learns in its spectral wavelet domain. HALO avoids explicit hypergraph-Laplacian eigendecomposition through Chebyshev polynomial wavelet filters, giving localized spectral kernels at linear sparse-matrix cost. Its trainable dyadic wavelet scales are regularized toward tight-frame coverage, allowing the frequency response to adapt to each PDE while encouraging stable multi-scale spectral coverage. Across 2D and 3D benchmarks on structured and unstructured discretizations, HALO achieves best or near-best accuracy among frequency-, transformer-, DeepONet-, state-space-, and graph-based baselines and sustains stable multi-step rollouts. The same model scales to industrial aerodynamic geometries: on meshes of a few hundred thousand points it is on par with, or better than, the strongest fixed-discretization transformers, while remaining resolution-equivariant.

</details>


### [63] [Fast Weight Attention for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27763)
*Yifan Zhang,Steve Ta,Jasper Zhang,Jichen Feng,Shuzhen Li,Yongxin Zhang,Yifeng Liu,Huizhuo Yuan,Mengdi Wang,Quanquan Gu,Andrew Chi-Chih Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环快速权重记忆（recurrent fast-weight memories）和选择性状态空间模型中的状态更新规则，将其视为在线学习问题。在read-after-write自回归语义下，作者推导了平方误差回归和负内积目标的归一化一阶更新规则，提出Falcon系列模型（包括标量NLMS、逐列扩展和滑动窗口小批量变体及其内积对应版本），并提供了循环、掩码并行和块并行三种实现形式及数值稳定的正衰减重归一化方法。实验表明这些变体在语言建模中具有竞争力并提升了变长数字加法的长度外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环状态空间模型将不断扩展的上下文压缩为固定大小的循环状态，其状态转移本质上是在线学习规则。然而现有研究对该规则在自回归语义下的精确目标函数缺乏系统分析，特别是读后写（read-after-write）场景中，前缀对齐的样本对与常见的同一步关联样本对在优化目标上存在差异，这一区别值得深入探究。

Method: 作者首先明确在prefix-prediction目标下，局部快速记忆样本应为前缀对齐对$(φ(k_{t-1}),v_t)$而非同一步关联$(φ(k_t),v_t)$。随后针对平方误差回归和负内积两种目标函数，推导归一化一阶在线更新规则。据此提出Falcon-1/2/3分别对应标量NLMS、逐列扩展和滑动窗口小批量更新，Falcon-1A/2A/3A为其内积变体。方法还包含三种并行形式（循环、掩码并行、块并行）以及稳定正衰减重归一化技术。

Result: 代表性Falcon变体在语言建模任务上保持了竞争力，并在变长数字加法任务上展现出改进的长度外推能力。框架成功分离并解析了循环序列模型中时间对齐、可塑性、遗忘和有界复述（bounded rehearsal）等不同机制。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解循环快速权重记忆和状态空间模型中的在线学习规则提供了统一的理论框架，明确了不同时间对齐语义对优化目标的实质影响，并通过Falcon系列模型验证了理论推导的有效性，为设计更好的循环序列模型提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Recurrent fast-weight memories and selective state-space models compress an expanding context into a fixed-size recurrent state, making the state transition an online learning rule. We study this rule under read-after-write autoregressive semantics. For the prefix-prediction objective considered here, the local fast-memory example revealed at step $t$ is the prefix-aligned pair $(\mathbf{x}_t,\mathbf{y}_t)=(φ(\mathbf{k}_{t-1}),\mathbf{v}_t)$. The common same-step association $(φ(\mathbf{k}_t),\mathbf{v}_t)$ remains causal, but optimizes a different internal objective. We derive normalized first-order updates for squared-error regression and negative inner-product objectives. The regression family comprises Falcon-1 (a scalar NLMS update), Falcon-2 (its per-column extension), and Falcon-3 (a sliding-window mini-batch update); Falcon-1A/Falcon-2A/Falcon-3A are the corresponding inner-product variants. We provide recurrent, masked-parallel, and chunk-parallel forms, together with numerically stable positive-decay renormalization. Representative variants remain competitive in language modeling and improve length extrapolation on variable-digit addition. This framework separates temporal alignment, plasticity, forgetting, and bounded rehearsal in recurrent sequence models.

</details>


### [64] [Euclidean Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28425)
*Nathanael Bosch,Niklas Frederik Schmitz,Michael F. Herbst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO），通过将谱核参数化为物理波矢的连续函数，解决了标准FNO因离散傅里叶模式权重与物理波矢绑定而无法跨周期域泛化的问题，并在热方程和材料科学交换关联势学习任务上验证了其跨网格分辨率和跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准FNO虽然对网格分辨率具有不变性，但其离散谱权重按整数傅里叶模式索引，对应于物理波矢。当应用到不同周期域时，相同的训练权重在不同波矢上起作用，导致FNO静默地表示不同的算子，使其无法适用于需要跨域迁移的任务。

Method: 作者提出欧几里得傅里叶神经算子（EFNO），核心创新是将谱核参数化为物理波矢的连续函数，而非离散的整数模索引。这使得EFNO能够跨不同形状和大小的周期域学习一致作用的算子。

Result: 在简单热方程以及学习不同晶体结构的交换关联势这一实际材料科学任务上，EFNO展现出对未见网格尺寸和未见域的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: EFNO作为一种域无关的FNO替代方案，通过连续化谱核参数化，成功解决了FNO在跨域迁移场景下的局限性，为函数空间映射学习提供了更具普适性的框架。

Abstract: Fourier neural operators (FNOs) provide an efficient framework for learning mappings between function spaces as they are, by construction, independent of the grid resolution at which they are trained and evaluated. However, FNOs are not independent of the periodic domain they are applied to: their discrete spectral weights are indexed by integer Fourier mode numbers, which correspond to physical wavevectors. When applied to a different domain, the same trained weights act at different wavevectors, and the FNO silently represents a different operator. This makes FNOs unsuitable for tasks where transfer across domains is crucial. We propose Euclidean Fourier neural operators~(EFNOs) as a domain-independent alternative to FNOs. By parameterizing the spectral kernel as a continuous function of the physical wavevector, the EFNO can learn operators that act consistently across periodic domains of varying shape and size. We evaluate the EFNO on a simple heat equation and on a practically relevant materials science task of learning exchange-correlation potentials across different crystal structures, and demonstrate that the EFNO is able to generalize to unseen grid sizes and domains.

</details>


### [65] [More Data Cannot Break a Symmetry: Identifiability by Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27651)
*Jing Xu,Christopher Kanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于等距群退化分析的先验诊断方法，用于在数据收集前评估无监督表征对齐（URSA）的可识别性。作者指出最便宜的非常规重标记（近重复换位）作为退化诊断会给出错误排序，并证明在颜色域中退化是结构性的：即使重启预算增加64倍，对称设计仍无法对齐，而非对称设计总能恢复。判别表征模型与恢复对应关系本质上不相关（r=-0.02），仅用该诊断选择九种颜色即可将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%。该风险普遍存在于规则设计与候选几何等距群相遇之处（如均匀间隔的方向、音调、运动方向），且检查仅需一次函数调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督表征对齐仅从几何结构恢复刺激间对应关系，但刺激几何的自同构群从根本上限制了任何对齐方法的可识别性。现有的退化诊断（最便宜的非恒等重标记）因密集采样产生近重复而失效，会错误排序已发表的设计方案。作者旨在将已知的不变性转化为设计阶段的诊断工具和干预手段，在数据收集前就能识别和规避对齐退化的风险。

Method: 作者利用颜色域中候选几何具有封闭解的特性，系统比较了对称与非对称颜色设计在对齐中的表现，施加64倍重启预算检验结构失效性。通过计算3,000个子集上判别模型与恢复对应的相关性验证目标非关联性，并提出仅依赖几何诊断（不参考任何学习表征）来选择刺激子集的设计方法，在固定模型、层数、N和求解器的条件下测试诊断的有效性和普适性。

Result: 对称颜色设计即使增加64倍重启预算仍无法对齐，而非对称设计总能恢复；判别表征模型与恢复对应之间的相关性为r=-0.02，表明两者本质上不相关。仅通过诊断选择九种颜色，在模型、层数、N和求解器全部固定时，将灾难性对齐失败率从75%降至2%，并使93个模型表征全部偏离退化点。该检查在数据收集前仅需一次函数调用即可完成。

Conclusion: 无监督表征对齐的退化风险源于规则设计与其候选几何的等距群相遇，这是结构性的而非计算资源问题。所提诊断可作为设计阶段的标准检查，适用于均匀间隔方向、音调、运动方向等多种场景，以极低成本预判并避免对齐失败。

Abstract: Unsupervised representational alignment recovers a stimulus-by-stimulus correspondence from geometry alone, but the automorphism group of the stimulus geometry bounds what any such alignment can identify, before data exist. The obvious diagnostic for this degeneracy, the cheapest non-identity relabelling, ranks two published designs in the wrong order, because dense sampling creates near-duplicates whose transposition is nearly free. We turn this known invariance (Demetci et al., 2024) into a design-time diagnostic and intervention. In colour, where candidate geometries have closed form, we show that the failure is structural: sixty-four times the restart budget leaves a symmetric design unmoved while an asymmetric set at the same N recovers every time. Discriminating representational models and recovering a correspondence are essentially uncorrelated objectives (r = -0.02 over 3,000 subsets). Choosing nine colours by this diagnostic alone, without consulting any learned representation, moves all 93 model representations away from the degenerate point and cuts catastrophic alignment failures from 75% to 2% with the models, the layers, N and the solver all held fixed. The same risk arises wherever a regular design meets its candidate geometry's isometry group, including evenly spaced orientations, tones, or motion directions, and the check costs one function call before data collection.

</details>


### [66] [Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting for Forecast Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28116)
*Prasen R. Nuthanakaluva,Nava K. Gaddam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出广义Gibbs系综加权（GGEW）框架，将预测模型视为专家，通过Gibbs风格指数变换的归一化预测损失分配集成权重，并结合数值稳定化、多样性感知评分修正和UCB式在线超参数自适应。在M4竞赛数据及多个外部数据集上的实验表明，该自适应加权方法在多种基准设置下具有竞争力，但不宣称普遍优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单聚合规则（如均值、中位数、截尾均值、逆损失加权和指数加权）虽常作为强基线，但其相对性能会因数据集、预测视界、部署设置和基预测器分歧程度的不同而变化。需要一个能够自适应调整权重分配策略的概率框架，以在不同场景下稳健地提升预测组合性能。

Method: GGEW框架将预测模型视为专家，采用Gibbs风格指数变换的归一化预测损失来分配集成权重。通过数值稳定化、多样性感知评分修正（Directional Gibbs-NCL和Symmetric Gibbs-NCL变体）以及在线Local-UCB bandit机制自适应学习率、多样性强度和Gibbs变体选择，而无需在每个预测步评估完整超参数网格。

Result: 在M4竞赛官方提交数据和Monash Traffic Hourly、Electricity Hourly、Solar Weekly外部滚动起点部署实验上，GGEW展现出竞争力，但其相对性能因数据集、预测视界、部署协议和预测分歧组别而异。

Conclusion: 本文贡献不是宣称通用优势，而是提供一个框架和实证研究，推动对自适应Gibbs风格预测组合何时有效的进一步探究。Gibbs风格自适应加权是预测组合工具箱中有用且具竞争力的工具。

Abstract: Forecast combination is a reliable way to improve predictive performance when several forecasting models are available. Simple aggregation rules such as the mean, median, trimmed mean, inverse-loss weighting, and exponential weighting are often strong baselines, but their relative performance can vary across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment settings, and levels of disagreement among base forecasters. We develop Generalized Gibbs Ensemble Weighting (GGEW), a probabilistic framework that treats forecasting models as experts and assigns ensemble weights using a Gibbs-style exponential transformation of normalized predictive loss. The framework extends this basic weighting rule through numerical stabilization, diversity-aware score corrections, and online hyperparameter adaptation. GGEW produces a family of related methods, including Stable Gibbs weighting, Directional Gibbs-NCL, and Symmetric Gibbs-NCL. These variants share one core algorithm and differ only in the score used inside the exponential weighting rule. For sequential deployment, we adopt a UCB-style bandit mechanism, called online Local-UCB, to adapt the learning rate, diversity strength, and Gibbs variant without evaluating the full hyperparameter grid at every prediction step. We evaluate GGEW on official M4 competition forecast submissions and external rolling-origin deployment experiments using Monash Traffic Hourly, Electricity Hourly, and Solar Weekly datasets. Results suggest that Gibbs-style adaptive weighting is a useful and competitive tool across several benchmark settings, although its relative performance varies across datasets, forecast horizons, deployment protocols, and forecast disagreement groups. The contribution is not a universal dominance claim, but a framework and empirical study motivating further investigation of when adaptive Gibbs-style forecast combination is useful.

</details>


### [67] [Unsupervised Continual Learning with Growing Self-Organizing Maps and Synthetic Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27662)
*Pujan Thapa,Alexander Ororbia,Travis Desell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生长自组织映射（GSOM）的生成式持续学习方法，通过引入学习到的分布统计量（均值、方差、协方差）和编码器-解码器模型，实现无范例（exemplar-free）的类别增量学习。每个GSOM单元维护自身的分布统计信息并用于生成合成样本进行回放，完全无需存储原始数据，且在训练过程中不依赖任务边界或类别标签，是一种完全无监督的方法。实验表明该方法在多个基准上达到了与有监督记忆基线相当的性能，并优于无记忆方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习面临的核心挑战是灾难性遗忘，现有基于记忆回放的方法通常需要存储原始样本，带来隐私和存储开销问题；同时许多方法依赖任务边界或类别标签等监督信息，限制了其适用性。本文旨在提出一种无需存储原始数据、无需监督信号的持续学习框架，以更灵活和可扩展的方式解决类别增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Method: 本文方法基于生长自组织映射（GSOM）构建生成式持续学习框架，核心包括：（1）每个GSOM单元维护自身的均值、方差和协方差分布统计量，形成"分布统计记忆"；（2）利用这些统计量通过从分布中采样生成合成样本用于回放，实现无范例回放；（3）在编码器-解码器配置中，生成样本通过祖先采样解码回输入空间用于后续训练；（4）整个过程完全无监督，不依赖任务边界或类别标签。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在性能上与有监督的、基于记忆（存储原始数据）的持续学习方法相当，且始终优于无记忆方法。尤其在具有挑战性的单类增量学习场景中，框架匹配或超越了现有基线。此外，作者还提供了TinyImageNet和MiniImageNet单类增量学习的基线结果，为未来研究提供参考。

Conclusion: 本文证明了无监督、自适应的、基于拓扑结构的神经形式统计回放方法在持续学习中的有效性，为持续学习提供了一种可扩展、灵活的解决方案。该工作表明分布统计记忆可以在无需存储原始数据和无需监督信号的情况下达到接近监督方法的效果，为未来持续学习研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: This work presents a generative continual learning framework based on growing self-organizing maps (GSOMs) that are augmented with learned distributional statistics as well as encoder-decoder models for class-incremental learning. The proposed approach enables exemplar-free replay using distributional statistical memory, which eliminates the need to store raw data. Each GSOM unit maintains its own mean, variance, and covariance estimates, which are subsequently used to generate synthetic samples for replay; in encoder-decoder configurations, these samples are then decoded back into the input space (via ancestral sampling) for subsequent training. Our method is fully unsupervised, as it does not rely on explicit task boundaries or class labels during training. Results across multiple benchmarks show that the proposed approach achieves performance competitive even with supervised state-of-the-art memory-based methods while consistently outperforming memory-free approaches. In several settings, our framework matches or exceeds existing baselines, particularly in challenging single-class incremental scenarios. We also provide baseline results for single-class incremental TinyImageNet and MiniImageNet, offering a useful reference for future work. This work highlights the effectiveness of an unsupervised, adaptive, topology-driven neural form of statistical replay as a scalable, flexible approach to continual learning.

</details>


### [68] [Performative Privacy: When Differential Privacy Maximizes Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28198)
*Uddalak Mukherjee,Edwige Cyffers,Yann Chevaleyre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将"保护隐私可提升长期效用"这一动机形式化，提出"表现型隐私"(performative privacy)框架，结合差分隐私与表现型学习，证明在数据泄露会降低未来参与的反馈循环足够强时，有限隐私预算的估计器在长期均值估计中优于非私有估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护学习常以"保护用户数据能维持信任并提高长期参与度"为动机，但这一主张从未被形式化。作者希望将隐私保护与表现型学习(performative learning)结合，为隐私机制在长期效用上的优势提供理论依据。

Method: 引入"表现型隐私"概念，其中数据泄露会减少未来参与。构建一个简单模型：智能体重复贡献数据用于均值估计，泄露时可能离开系统；隐私通过差分隐私机制实现，从而产生估计噪声与未来参与之间的权衡。通过理论动力学分析和数值实验进行研究。

Result: 研究表明，当泄露-参与反馈循环足够强时，有限隐私预算在长期均值估计中优于非私有估计。这是首个证据表明差分隐私不仅作为保护机制，而且在长期效用视角下也可能是最优的。

Conclusion: 差分隐私在考虑数据泄露对用户参与度反馈影响的情况下，可以在长期效用上超越非私有方法，填补了隐私保护学习长期效用动机缺乏形式化的空白。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving learning is often motivated by the idea that protecting users' data can preserve trust and thus participation, improving utility in the long term. However, this claim has not been formalized so far. In parallel, performative learning provides a framework for studying learning systems whose deployment affects the data they later observe. In this work, we bring these two perspectives together and introduce \emph{performative privacy}, where data leakage reduces future participation. We study a simple model where agents repeatedly contribute data for mean estimation but may leave the system when their data is leaked. Privacy is implemented through differentially private mechanisms, creating a trade-off between estimation noise and future participation. We show, through a theoretical study of the dynamics and numerical experiments, that a finite privacy budget can outperform non-private estimation in the long term when the feedback loop between leakage and participation is sufficiently strong. This provides first evidence that differential privacy can be optimal not only as a protection mechanism, but also from the perspective of long-term utility.

</details>


### [69] [SegBench-GC: Testing Segmentation Invariance in Multi-Step Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27678)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SegBench-GC，一个专注于离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中段分割不变性的受控压力测试基准。研究发现，管理中人为设置的轨迹分割边界（非真实终止）会显著影响多步离线GCRL算法的性能。在固定的状态转移、源轨迹、目标采样和优化设置下仅改变人工备份边界及其是否保留延续价值，结果表明：当将人为切割视为吸收态（naive处理）时，性能急剧下降（PointMaze成功率从50.5%降至19.1%，Puzzle-4x5从47.2%降至0.27%）；而保留延续价值的CVT处理则部分缓解了退化。该工作提供了受控基准、故障隔离以及跨学习器的证据，证明行政性分割会实质性改变多步离线GCRL的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线GCRL方法通常依赖轨迹结构进行未来目标采样和多步目标计算，但实际收集的日志轨迹可能因管理性原因（非环境终止）被分割成多段。这种人工分割可能导致多步目标计算错误，严重影响算法性能。作者旨在通过受控实验明确隔离并量化人工分割边界对离线GCRL的影响，为社区提供系统性的故障诊断基准。

Method: 作者构建了SegBench-GC基准：在保持状态转移、源轨迹、目标采样、优化设置和评估完全一致的前提下，仅改变人工备份边界的放置方式（三种分割实现）以及这些边界是否保留延续价值（CVT vs naive吸收处理）。CVT（延续有效目标）作为对照方法：在人工切割处停止奖励累积，但目标从存储的后继状态进行自举。实验在PointMaze中进行了35,000次人工切割的匹配计数研究，并在Puzzle-4x5上使用独立的已发布n=25步基线（来自Decoupled Q-Chunking代码库）进行交叉验证。此外还使用目标级诊断验证分析目标差异到数值精度，以及学习评论家诊断检验乐观偏差。

Result: 在PointMaze中，最终50集每任务成功率：未切割为50.5%，CVT为39.1%，naive处理（将切割视为吸收态）仅为19.1%。跨三种分割实现，naive的平均成功率在4.8%到31.9%之间波动，显示出对分割位置的极端敏感性。在Puzzle-4x5上，独立n-step基线同样失败：未切割47.2%，CVT 58.5%，naive仅0.27%。目标级诊断验证了分析目标差异到数值精度；学习评论家诊断显示naive处理下出现大幅乐观偏移，而CVT与未切割评论家近似对齐。

Conclusion: 人工分割边界（无论放置位置如何）若被当作真实终止处理（naive），会严重损害多步离线GCRL的性能；而保留延续价值的CVT处理提供了近似不变的分割一致性对照。CVT使用的是标准延续自举而非新的Bellman规则，因此本文的核心贡献不在于提出新算法，而在于提供受控基准、故障隔离方法，以及跨学习器（不同算法架构）的证据，表明行政性分割会实质性改变多步离线GCRL的结果。这项工作为后续研究如何使离线GCRL方法对轨迹分割具有鲁棒性提供了重要基线。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) often uses trajectory structure for future-goal sampling and multi-step targets, yet logged trajectories may be partitioned for administrative reasons that do not correspond to termination. We introduce SegBench-GC, a controlled stress test of segmentation invariance that holds transitions, source trajectories, goal sampling, optimization settings, and evaluation fixed while varying only artificial backup boundaries and whether those boundaries retain continuation value. Continuation-valid targets (CVT) provide the segmentation-consistent control: reward accumulation stops at an artificial cut, but the target bootstraps from its stored successor. In a matched-count PointMaze study with 35,000 artificial cuts, three segmentation realizations, and three optimization seeds, final 50-episode-per-task success is 50.5% uncut, 39.1% with CVT, and 19.1% when the same cuts are treated as absorbing; across segmentation realizations, naive mean success ranges from 4.8% to 31.9%. An independent published n-step baseline (n=25) from the Decoupled Q-Chunking codebase shows the same failure on Puzzle-4x5: 47.2% uncut, 58.5% CVT, and 0.27% naive across three optimization seeds. A target-level diagnostic verifies the analytic target difference to numerical precision, and learned-critic diagnostics show a large optimistic shift under naive handling while CVT remains approximately aligned with the uncut critic. CVT applies standard continuation bootstrapping rather than a new Bellman rule; the contribution is the controlled benchmark, failure isolation, and cross-learner evidence that administrative segmentation can materially change multi-step offline GCRL.

</details>


### [70] [SafeStep: An Interactive Demonstration of Semantic Communication for Pedestrian Safety Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27688)
*Christian McDowell,Andrea Panebianco,Jeremiah Yang,Sirin Chakraborty,Samuel Chamoun,Travis Ross,Yin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeStep是一个基于浏览器的交互式语义通信平台，用于实时行人安全监控。它从四个实时交通摄像头提取行人信息，通过语义通信收发器在AWGN信道传输，并渲染用户特定的位置、轨迹和风险标签。平台允许独立选择收发器、SNR、码长和AoI，比较了Meta-VIB与五种基线收发器的性能，Meta-VIB以416万参数实现高达92.1%的平均任务损失降低，支持高达20个用户维持5帧/s，100个用户时帧率降至约1帧/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义通信系统缺乏真实世界的实时演示平台，尤其是无法让AoI诱导的下游性能退化在实时监控应用中直接可观测。SafeStep旨在填补这一空白，提供一个可交互的浏览器平台来实时演示语义通信性能。

Method: SafeStep构建了一个完整的端到端语义通信平台：从四个实时交通摄像头提取行人信息，经由语义通信收发器在AWGN信道传输，并在浏览器中渲染行人位置、轨迹和风险标签。平台支持独立配置收发器、SNR、codelength和AoI参数，并将Meta-VIB（416万参数的紧凑神经模型）与五种基线收发器进行对比。Meta-VIB无需在线重训练即可泛化到不同SNR、codelength和AoI配置。

Result: Meta-VIB相比基线收发器实现了高达92.1%的平均任务损失降低。在高端GPU服务器上，集成并发访问工作负载可在20个用户下维持5帧/s的目标帧率；在100个用户各自请求不同配置时，无请求失败且平均应用响应时间低于1秒，但每浏览器平均帧率降至约1帧/s。

Conclusion: SafeStep是首个使AoI诱导的下游退化在实时监控应用（如行人安全监控）中直接可观测的实时语义通信平台，为语义通信系统的实际部署和性能评估提供了重要的实验验证工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop SafeStep, an interactive browser-based semantic communication platform for live pedestrian safety monitoring. SafeStep extracts pedestrian information from four live traffic-camera feeds, transmits it through a semantic communication transceiver over an Additive White Gaussian Noise (AWGN) channel, and renders user-specific positions, trajectories, and risk labels. The platform allows to independently select the transceiver, Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR), codelength, and Age of Information (AoI), and demonstrates the transceiver performance of the selected configuration through live pedestrian safety monitoring to each browser. SafeStep compares a recently proposed semantic communication design called Meta-VIB with five baseline transceivers. Meta-VIB uses a compact neural model with only $4.16$ million parameters to generalize across varying SNR, codelength, and AoI values without online retraining. Experimental results show that Meta-VIB achieves mean task-loss reductions of up to $92.1\%$. On one high-end GPU server, the integrated concurrent-access workload maintains the target $5$ frames/s through $20$ users. At $100$ users, each requesting a distinct configuration, SafeStep records no request failures and a mean application response time below $1$ s, but its mean per-browser frame rate falls to approximately $1$ frame/s. To our knowledge, SafeStep is the first real-time semantic communication platform to make AoI-induced downstream degradation directly observable in live monitoring applications.

</details>


### [71] [RiskBlend: A Multi-Signal Framework for Test Input Prioritization in Machine Learning Regression Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27704)
*Madhusudan Srinivasan,Namith Nishal Raphae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了 RiskBlend，一个分类器无关的测试输入优先级排序框架，通过结合四种互补的风险信号（历史失败模式、预测位移、决策边界位移和邻域变化），并使用验证学习的 APFD 平方权重进行融合。在 80 个数据集-分类器-场景组合中均取得最高平均 APFD，比最强基线最多提升 0.32 APFD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习分类器重训练后，之前正确分类的输入可能被新版本错误分类，产生回归缺陷。由于验证预测结果需要人工标注、专家审查或昂贵模拟，成本很高。现有测试输入优先级排序方法主要依赖单一模型的置信度分数，未能利用模型版本之间预测、决策边界和局部邻域的变化信息。

Method: 提出 RiskBlend 框架，融合四种互补风险信号：(1) 历史失败模式；(2) 预测位移；(3) 决策边界位移；(4) 邻域变化。使用验证集学习的 APFD 平方权重组合这些信号，且为分类器无关的通用方法。

Result: 在 4 个数据集、5 种分类器、4 种回归更新场景和 15 个随机种子（共 1200 个实验配置）上，RiskBlend 在所有 80 个数据集-分类器-场景组合中均取得最高平均 APFD，较最强基线提升最多 0.32 APFD。基于置信度的方法仅在稀疏分类特征的线性分类器上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 跨版本行为信号为机器学习系统的回归缺陷优先级排序提供了重要的互补信息。基于置信度的方法的竞争力局限与特征空间几何有关，验证了综合利用多种跨版本信号的优越性。

Abstract: When machine learning classifiers are retrained, inputs correctly classified by the previous model version may be misclassified by the updated version, creating regression faults that are costly to detect because verifying predictions against ground truth may require human annotation, expert review, or expensive simulation rather than inexpensive model inference. Test input prioritization addresses this problem by ranking inputs so that a limited verification budget reveals as many regression faults as possible. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single-model confidence scores and do not exploit how predictions, decision boundaries, and local neighborhoods change between model versions. We propose RiskBlend, a classifier-agnostic prioritization framework that combines four complementary risk signals: historical failure patterns, prediction shift, decision-boundary shift, and neighborhood change. These signals are combined using validation-learned APFD-squared weighting. Across four datasets, five classifiers, four regression-update scenarios, and 15 random seeds, totaling 1,200 experimental configurations, RiskBlend achieves the highest average APFD in all 80 dataset-classifier-scenario combinations, with improvements of up to 0.32 APFD over the strongest baseline. Confidence-based methods remain competitive primarily for linear classifiers on sparse categorical features, which we attribute to feature-space geometry. The results show that cross-version behavioral signals provide important complementary information for prioritizing regression faults in machine learning systems.

</details>


### [72] [DART-FL: Burst-Aware Multitask Federated Learning under Dynamic Inference Demand at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27713)
*Yiming Xie,Pinrui Yu,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Ningfang Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART-FL是一个SLO感知、需求驱动的多任务联邦学习框架，它联合调整推理与训练的资源分配以及任务级训练重点，以在资源受限的边缘设备上同时满足推理服务目标并提升高频任务的训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能系统需要在资源受限的设备上同时进行模型训练和在线推理，而推理需求随时间波动。这带来两个耦合挑战：(1) 必须保留足够的计算资源用于推理以满足SLO；(2) 剩余训练容量应适配任务特定需求，使高频请求的任务能更早获得改进。

Method: DART-FL在每个调度间隔内，利用推理积压和服务容量画像确定推理所需的最小资源分配，然后将剩余训练容量通过队列感知的DPP（determinantal point process）启发式调度器分配到各任务，并将任务分配映射为动态损失权重。客户端训练共享骨干网络和任务特定头部，通过FedAvg聚合完整的多任务模型。

Result: 在Stanford Cars和Oxford Flowers 102数据集上，使用合成和真实Alibaba trace衍生的工作负载进行评估。结果表明DART-FL能动态适应时变的推理-训练资源分配，将高频需求任务的学习进度向峰值时段偏移，在任务被频繁请求时提升模型准确率，同时保持相当的长周期多任务性能。

Conclusion: DART-FL通过联合优化推理-训练资源划分和任务级训练重点，有效解决了边缘场景下推理SLO保障与需求驱动训练之间的耦合问题，在保持整体多任务性能的同时提升高频任务在需求高峰期内的模型质量。

Abstract: Edge intelligence systems increasingly require model training and online inference to coexist on resource-constrained devices, while inference demand can vary substantially across tasks over time. This creates two coupled challenges: sufficient computation must be reserved for inference to maintain service-level objectives (SLOs), while the remaining training capacity should adapt to task-specific demand so that frequently requested tasks can improve earlier during training.
  We propose an SLO-aware, demand-driven multitask federated learning framework (DART-FL) that jointly adapts the inference-training resource split and task-level training emphasis. At each scheduling interval, DART-FL uses the inference backlog and profiled service capacity to determine the minimum resource allocation required for inference. The remaining training capacity is then distributed across tasks using a queue-aware DPP-inspired scheduler, and the resulting task allocations are mapped to dynamic loss weights. This allows tasks experiencing higher inference demand to receive greater training emphasis in earlier communication rounds. Clients train a shared backbone with task-specific heads, and the complete multitask model is aggregated through FedAvg.
  We evaluate DART-FL using Stanford Cars and Oxford Flowers 102 under both synthetic and real Alibaba trace-derived workloads. Results show that DART-FL dynamically adapts the inference-training resource split to time-varying inference demand and shifts the learning progress of high-demand tasks toward their burst periods, improving model accuracy when those tasks are frequently requested while maintaining comparable long-term multitask performance.

</details>
