Conclusion: SinkSLOT通过稀疏化策略和更优的先验耦合同时解决了Sinkhorn的效率瓶颈和参考测度质量问题，在大规模EOT应用中兼具计算高效性和理论保证，代码已开源。

Abstract: Entropic optimal transport (EOT) has been shown to offer a computationally tractable approximation to exact optimal transport. However, the standard Sinkhorn-Knopp algorithm has two main limitations. First, given discrete measures with $N$ points, each iteration requires $O(N^2)$ operations, which restricts its use on large-scale datasets (e.g. $N\geq10^4$). Second, it uses the independent coupling as a reference measure for regularisation. This assigns mass to high-cost transport edges at moderate regularisation strengths. We propose SinkSLOT, which addresses both limitations by putting forth the expected sliced lifted transport plan as a natural way to sparsify the Gibbs kernel with a non-independent prior coupling. We prove that: 1) SinkSLOT converges; 2) with $L$ slices, each resulting sparse Sinkhorn iteration costs $O(LN)$; and 3) the resulting objective is a divergence requiring no debiasing. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks show that SinkSLOT delivers substantial speedups over state-of-the-art dense and sparse EOT methods. We also demonstrate the applicability of the proposed divergence in a gradient flow experiment. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/cai4cai/SinkSLOT.

</details>


### [107] [Residual-Guided Randomized Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28267)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer,Mohd. Arshad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种残差引导的贪心构造方法（residual guided procedure），用于改进随机神经网络中隐藏层特征的构建质量。该方法通过逐步生成候选随机单元、基于闭式残差减少准则评分、选择最优单元并闭式重拟合输出权重，从而保证训练目标单调递减。在71个UCI基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确率、稳定性和排名性能上均优于基线随机神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机神经网络（如随机向量函数链接网络、极限学习机等）通过固定输入到隐藏层的随机参数并闭式求解输出权重来实现快速训练，但其性能高度依赖于单次随机初始化隐单元的质量。这种单次、任务无关的特征构建方式往往导致特征冗余和模型容量利用率不足。

Method: 提出残差引导的贪心特征选择流程，每阶段包含四个步骤：(i) 生成一批随机候选隐单元；(ii) 根据每个候选单元在岭正则化目标函数中产生的精确闭式残差减少量进行评分；(iii) 选择得分最高的k个单元；(iv) 使用包含直接输入连接的标准设计矩阵闭式重拟合输出层。该方法模型无关，仅候选单元的生成依赖于具体架构，而评分-选择-重拟合循环在各模型间共享，且训练目标保证单调递减。

Result: 在UCI仓库的71个基准数据集上（涵盖二分类和多分类任务），残差引导的随机神经网络在准确率、稳定性和整体排名性能方面持续超越基线随机神经网络模型。

Conclusion: 通过将特征构建过程从单次随机采样转变为残差指导的贪心迭代过程，可以有效提升随机神经网络的表示质量和模型容量利用率，同时保持闭式求解的训练效率。该方法简单、通用且具有理论上的单调收敛保证。

Abstract: Randomized neural networks enable fast and analytically tractable training by fixing the input to hidden layer parameters at random and learning the output weights in closed form; however, their performance critically depends on a single uninformed draw of hidden units. This one shot and task uninformed feature construction often leads to redundant representations and suboptimal utilization of model capacity. To address this limitation, we propose a simple and broadly applicable residual guided procedure that greedily constructs the hidden layer using a closed form residual decrease criterion. At each stage, we (i) generate a pool of random candidate units, (ii) score each candidate by the exact reduction it induces in the ridge regularized objective, (iii) select the top k units, and (iv) refit the readout in closed form using the standard design with direct input links. This procedure yields a progressive training process with a guaranteed monotonic decrease of the training objective. The method is model agnostic: only the candidate generation is architecture specific, while the scoring selection refitting loop is shared across models. Extensive experiments on 71 benchmark datasets from the UCI repository, covering both binary and multiclass classification tasks, demonstrate that the proposed residual-guided models consistently outperform their baseline counterparts in terms of accuracy, stability, and overall ranking performance.

</details>


### [108] [Learning to Transfer Across Modes: Towards Unified Urban Mobility Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28273)
*Yixuan Zhao,Man Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TransMod是一个统一的城市场景多模式交通需求预测框架，通过构建共享的区域级空间表示来对齐不同空间粒度的移动模式，实现从数据丰富的源模式向数据稀缺的目标模式的知识迁移，从而提升异构出行模式下的联合预测性能，并在有限目标数据条件下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通系统中多种出行模式共存且相互依赖，需求动态具有相关性。然而，跨模式联合预测面临两大挑战：(1)模式间存在显著的空间异质性；(2)新兴模式的历史数据极为有限。现有方法大多针对单一出行模式设计，且隐式假设源系统与目标系统具有兼容的空间结构，严重限制了多模式场景下的适用性。

Method: TransMod提出一个统一框架：首先构建共享的区域级（zone-level）空间表示，将具有不同空间粒度的移动系统对齐到公共空间中，以缓解结构失配和分布偏移；在此基础上，该框架从数据丰富的源模式学习可迁移的时空模式，并将其适配到数据稀缺的目标模式，从而降低对目标域大量历史数据的依赖。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，TransMod一致性地优于现有方法，并在有限目标数据条件下提供了稳健的预测性能。

Conclusion: TransMod有效解决了多模式交通需求预测中的空间异构性和数据稀缺性问题，通过统一的共享空间表示与跨模式知识迁移机制，为多模态城市场景下的需求预测提供了一种通用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Urban transportation systems consist of multiple mobility modes that coexist within the same city and exhibit complex interdependencies, leading to correlated demand dynamics across modes. However, forecasting demand jointly across different modes remains challenging due to substantial heterogeneity in space and the limited availability of historical data for emerging modes. Existing forecasting methods are largely developed for individual mobility modes and implicitly assume compatible spatial structures between source and target systems, which severely restricts their applicability in multi-modal settings. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{TransMod}, a unified framework for urban mobility demand forecasting that enables effective knowledge transfer across heterogeneous mobility modes. TransMod constructs a shared zone-level spatial representation that aligns mobility systems with different spatial granularities into a common space, thereby reducing structural mismatch and distributional shift. Built on this unified representation, TransMod further learns transferable spatio-temporal patterns from data-rich source modes and adapts them to data-scarce target modes, alleviating the dependence on extensive target-domain histories. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TransMod consistently outperforms existing approaches and provides robust forecasting performance under limited target data.

</details>


### [109] [An algebraic proof of Colombo's difference-power determinant conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28274)
*Kun Li,Li Tie,Peng Wang,Zihan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Colombo在1928年提出的关于差分幂矩阵非奇异性的猜想，即对任意偶数n≥2、坐标两两不同的实向量λ，矩阵A_d(λ)=[(λ_r-λ_s)^d]在任意d≥n-1时均非奇异。该猜想此前对偶数d已被证明，本文补全了所有奇数d≥n+1的情形，从而完成了整个猜想，并得到rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}的完整秩公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Colombo于1928年证明了det A_{n-1}(λ)≠0以及0≤d<n-1时rank A_d(λ)=d+1，并猜想对一切d≥n-1都有det A_d(λ)≠0。此前已有结果涵盖了偶数d的情形，剩余的未解情形是超临界奇数指数d≥n+1，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明采用反证法：假设存在核向量，将其转化为一个实的二元型（binary form），该二元型具有超过其实Waring长度所允许的射影实线性因式个数（含重数），从而导出矛盾，证明非奇异性。

Result: 完整证明了Colombo猜想，即对一切d≥n-1（包括所有超临界奇数指数），det A_d(λ)≠0；并由此推出rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}对所有d∈ℕ成立。

Conclusion: 该结果彻底解决了Colombo猜想，将差分幂矩阵的秩结构完全刻画为rank A_d(λ)=min{n,d+1}，并为距离幂矩阵与差分幂矩阵的理论提供了完整的非奇异性与秩结论。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ be even, let $λ=(λ_1,\ldots,λ_n)\in\mathbb{R}^n$ have pairwise distinct coordinates, and define the difference-power matrix \[ A_d(λ) := \bigl[(λ_r-λ_s)^d\bigr]_{r,s=1}^n, \qquad d\in\mathbb{N}. \] In 1928, Colombo proved that $\det A_{n-1}(λ)\ne0$---and hence $\det A_{n-1}(λ)>0$---and that $\operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=d+1$ for $0\le d<n-1$. He conjectured that \[ \det A_d(λ)\ne0 \qquad\text{for every } d\ge n-1. \] For even $d$, the conjectured nonsingularity follows from previously published results on distance-power matrices. The remaining open cases were therefore the supercritical odd exponents $d\ge n+1$. We prove nonsingularity for all these odd exponents, thereby completing Colombo's conjecture. Consequently, \[ \operatorname{rank} A_d(λ)=\min\{n,d+1\} \qquad(d\in\mathbb{N}). \] Our proof converts a hypothetical kernel vector into a real binary form having more projective real linear factors, counted with multiplicity, than its real Waring length permits.

</details>


### [110] [Parser States Already Know: Structure-Conditioned KV Persistence for Structured Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28276)
*Linze Wu,Xinrui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PASK（Parser-Aware Structural KV Persistence），一种将解析器推导出的语法结构转化为KV缓存持久化决策的方法，用于结构化生成场景（JSON、SQL、函数调用）。PASK通过任务错误敏感性和注意力输出失真度来制定按层分组KV保留策略，在8个BFCL子类别上平均超越最强压缩基线17.39个百分点，并实现最高2.2倍吞吐量提升和3.3倍TPOT降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化生成中单个字段错误会导致下游操作失败，约束解码已能追踪解析器状态以强制格式有效性，但现有KV压缩方法忽略了这些解析器转换所暴露的架构关键性结构信号（如必填字段、参数、结构边界）。作者旨在利用任务相关的结构性信号来指导KV压缩，而非仅依赖模型侧KV灵敏度。

Method: PASK将解析器推导的结构信息转化为层组特定的KV持久化决策。核心包括两点：(1) 使用任务误差敏感度设置最小保护下限（protection floors）；(2) 使用注意力输出失真度分配剩余KV容量。通过离线校准阶段将信号编译为持久化策略，在线推理时仅需轻量级的结构条件查询。

Result: 在目标总KV预算为0.33时，PASK在Qwen3-4B上跨越8个BFCL非Live和Live子类别，平均比最强压缩基线高出17.39个百分点。端到端服务中，PASK实现最高2.2倍吞吐量提升、3.3倍TPOT降低，同时峰值GPU内存仅为Full KV的0.53倍。

Conclusion: PASK证明了将解析器结构信号纳入KV压缩决策可以显著改善结构化生成中的压缩效率与任务保真度，弥合了模型侧KV敏感性与任务级结构化风险之间的不匹配，为约束生成场景中的高效KV压缩提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Structured generation underpins large language model (LLM) agents that produce JSON, SQL, and function calls, where a single wrong field can cause the downstream action to fail. Constrained decoding already tracks parser transitions to enforce formal validity, and these transitions expose how generated tokens participate in schema-critical decisions such as required fields, arguments, and structural boundaries under the active grammar. Existing KV compression largely leaves this task-relevant structural signal unused. We introduce PASK (Parser-Aware Structural KV Persistence), which turns parser-derived structure into layer-group-specific KV persistence decisions. PASK addresses the mismatch between model-side KV sensitivity and task-level structured risk by using task-error sensitivity to set minimum protection floors and attention-output distortion to allocate residual KV capacity. An offline calibration stage compiles these signals into a persistence policy, leaving only lightweight structure-conditioned lookup online. At a targe total KV budget of 0.33, PASK outperforms the strongest compressed baseline by 17.39 percentage points on average across eight BFCL non-live and Live subcategories on Qwen3-4B. In end-to-end serving, PASK achieves up to 2.2x higher throughput and 3.3x lower TPOT, while using 0.53x the peak GPU memory of Full KV.

</details>


### [111] [VISTA: Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28306)
*Zewen Ding,Zezhong Wu,Zhou Tao,Shida Wang,Shizhuo Hou,YongXiang Hua,Haoyu Cao,Linli Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA（Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation），一种改进的on-policy自蒸馏（OPSD）方法。标准OPSD将特权教师分布视为固定目标，只更新学生模型，但教师分布未必与学生推理对齐。VISTA在保持标准OPSD学生更新的同时，利用结果验证的rollouts将教师向学生分布自适应调整，并在每个已验证rollout内限制在教师-学生KL散度最大的top-k位置进行适配。VISTA复用了OPSD的rollout和损失函数，无需额外采样或独立奖励目标。在AIME24、AIME25和HMMT25基准上，使用Qwen3模型（1.7B、4B、8B），VISTA在所有规模下均达到最高Avg@12，相比OPSD分别提升0.6、0.7和2.1个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准OPSD将教师分布作为固定目标并仅更新学生，但特权条件的教师并不总能提供与问题-only推理最匹配的目标，这种单向监督会在教师分布与学生有效推理不对齐时误导学生。

Method: VISTA在保持标准OPSD学生更新的同时，利用outcome-verified rollouts来将教师向学生分布自适应调整。在每个已验证的rollout中，进一步将适配限制在教师-学生KL散度最大的top-k位置。VISTA完全复用OPSD的rollout和损失函数，不引入额外采样或独立奖励目标。

Result: 在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25基准上，Qwen3模型的1.7B、4B、8B规模下，VISTA均达到最高Avg@12，相比OPSD分别提升0.6、0.7和2.1个点。

Conclusion: 结果验证了从outcome-verified rollouts获得的学生监督的价值，并表明学生到教师的自适应（student-to-teacher adaptation）是OPSD的一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves reasoning by training a problem-only student on its own rollouts using dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher that also sees a reference solution. However, standard OPSD treats the teacher distribution as a fixed target along the student's rollout and updates only the student %, although -- even though privileged conditioning does not guarantee that the teacher always provides the most appropriate target for problem-only reasoning. This one-way supervision can therefore misdirect the student when the teacher distribution is misaligned with valid student reasoning. We therefore introduce Verifier-Informed Student-to-Teacher Adaptation (VISTA), which preserves the standard OPSD student update while using outcome-verified rollouts to adapt the teacher toward the student distribution. Within each verified rollout, VISTA further restricts this adaptation to the top-$k$ positions with the largest teacher--student KL divergence. Notably, VISTA reuses the rollout and loss function from standard OPSD, introducing no additional sampling or separate reward objective. Across AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25 with Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B, VISTA achieves the highest Avg@12 at every scale, improving over OPSD by $0.6$, $0.7$, and $2.1$ points, respectively. These results demonstrate the value of student supervision from outcome-verified rollouts and highlight student-to-teacher adaptation as a promising direction for OPSD.

</details>


### [112] [Deriving Scaling Laws for OpenEuroLLM Models: Learning Rate, Batch Size and Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28308)
*Niccolò Ajroldi,Diana Alexandra Onutu,Haider Al-Tahan,Jörg Franke,Sampo Pyysalo,Jenia Jitsev,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了预训练稠密大语言模型时学习率和批次大小的缩放行为，包括联合最优缩放、随模型容量和数据规模的变化、Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度下学习率退火的收益，以及最优超参数在稳定与衰减阶段的迁移性。文章还评估了建模损失与模型容量及数据集规模关系的缩放形式，并开源了全部预训练运行数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在预训练大语言模型时，如何选择最优的学习率和批次大小以及它们如何随模型规模和数据量缩放，尚缺乏系统性的理解和量化模型。需要为未来的OpenEuroLLM模型开发建立缩放基线。

Method: 在英文占主导的语料上对稠密大语言模型进行大量预训练实验。采用Warmup-Stable-Decay学习率调度，分别研究联合最优的（学习率、批次大小）缩放以及二者的边际演化规律。评估学习率退火在不同超参数、模型和数据预算下的收益，检验超参数在稳定与衰减两阶段之间的迁移性。采用显式建模模型容量与数据规模交互的缩放形式来刻画损失依赖关系。

Result: 提出的缩放形式能够有效刻画欠训练（undertraining）与过训练（overtraining）两种状态下的损失行为。确定学习率与批次大小随模型规模和数据量的缩放规律，并解析了学习率退火的收益及其超参数迁移特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为OpenEuroLLM模型系列建立了首个缩放基线与流程，并开源了全部预训练运行数据，为后续大规模模型训练的超参数选择提供了可复现的指导。

Abstract: We study the scaling behavior of learning rate and batch size in pretraining dense large language models on English-prevalent corpora. Beyond scaling \textit{jointly optimal} learning rates and batch sizes, we investigate their \textit{marginal} evolution with model capacity and data scale and develop a model that captures these relationships. As we employ a Warmup-Stable-Decay learning rate schedule, we further investigate the gains from learning rate annealing over a broad range of hyperparameters settings, models and data budgets, and whether the optimal learning rate and batch size \textit{transfer} between the stable and decay phases. Finally, we characterize the dependence of loss on model capacity and dataset size, evaluating recently proposed scaling forms that explicitly model their interaction. We find these approaches particularly effective at capturing both undertraining and overtraining regimes across our experiments. This study establishes a first baseline and scaling procedure for the development of future OpenEuroLLM models. We open-source the complete collection of pretraining runs used in this study.

</details>


### [113] [SymboLLM-FE: LLM-Accelerated Symbolic Regression for Automated Feature Engineering on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28408)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Zi-Yi Jia,Zhi Zhou,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SymboLLM-FE框架，将符号回归与大型语言模型结合用于自动特征工程，解决传统AutoFE可解释性差和基于LLM的AutoFE迭代成本高、易产生偏差和幻觉的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AutoFE依赖盲目的数学变换导致特征可解释性差，而基于LLM的AutoFE需要昂贵的多轮迭代才能生成高效用特征，且存在偏差和幻觉风险。现有方法无法同时兼顾可解释性、模型性能与运行效率。

Method: 首先通过符号回归提取与目标变量强相关的数学表达式公式以增强模型性能，然后利用具有丰富先验知识的LLM对这些公式进行精炼，确保其可解释性。采用统计先验引导的LLM精炼机制，仅需单次数字级的LLM调用。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集和四个Kaggle竞赛中的实证结果表明，SymboLLM-FE优于现有的AutoFE方法，同时解决了可解释性差和迭代次数多两大挑战。

Conclusion: 符号回归与LLM的结合能够有效弥补传统AutoFE和LLM-based AutoFE各自的不足，在特征工程中同时实现高性能、高可解释性和低迭代成本。

Abstract: Tabular data, as a core data format in machine learning, often lacks the discriminative power needed for high-performance modeling due to insufficient feature informativeness. Automated Feature Engineering (AutoFE) overcomes this by automating feature generation and selection, ensuring both model performance and operational efficiency. However, traditional AutoFE often yield features with poor interpretability because they rely on blind mathematical transformations, while large language models (LLM)-based AutoFE faces challenges in requiring costly multi-round iterations to generate high-utility features to effectively enhance model performance, compounded by inherent risks of bias and hallucination. In this paper, we combine symbolic regression with LLMs for feature engineering (SymboLLM-FE) to solve these challenges. We extract mathematically expressive formulas strongly correlated with the target via symbolic regression, which can enhance model performance, then refine them by LLMs with rich prior knowledge to ensure interpretability. Empirical results on six real-world datasets and four Kaggle competitions demonstrate that SymboLLM-FE outperforms existing AutoFE. SymboLLM-FE also addresses the dual challenges of poor interpretability and numerous iterations by employing a statistical prior-grounded LLM refinement mechanism and single-digit LLM calls.

</details>


### [114] [Curvature-Conditioned Multiscale Momentum with Sphere Constraints for LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28442)
*Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Mingze Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种曲率条件的多尺度动量方法（配合球面约束），仅沿平坦方向应用，以加速LLM预训练。该方法将慢衰减动量（降噪）与快衰减动量（快速曲率适应）结合，并通过球面约束防止参数膨胀，在多种架构和模型规模（0.12B–2.3B参数）上显著加速Muon优化器，并有理论验证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练占据LLM训练的大部分计算成本，但噪声主导的梯度和病态（ill-conditioned）损失景观带来严峻挑战。现有自适应优化器（如AdamW、Muon）依赖梯度归一化，对病态曲率的缓解有限，沿平坦方向（小特征值特征方向）的进展——这主导了最终损失下降——仍然相对缓慢。

Method: 提出曲率条件的多尺度动量方法：仅沿平坦方向应用一种双分量动量，包含慢衰减分量（用于降噪）和快衰减分量（用于快速曲率适应），两者互补。同时引入球面约束技术，防止朴素组合导致的参数膨胀和有效学习率过快衰减。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多样化架构（密集、MoE）和模型规模（0.12B–2.3B参数）上显著加速Muon优化器。理论上验证了加速效果，并深入分析了平坦方向多尺度动量背后的设计原理。

Conclusion: 该方法通过沿平坦方向应用多尺度动量并结合球面约束，有效改善了LLM预训练中的病态曲率问题，实现了稳定加速，兼具理论保障和实证支撑。

Abstract: Pretraining accounts for a large fraction of the total computational cost in LLM training. However, noise-dominant gradients and the highly ill-conditioned loss landscape bring severe challenges. Although modern adaptive optimizers such as AdamW and Muon have achieved great success in large-scale pretraining, their reliance on gradient normalization offers limited mitigation of the ill-conditioned curvature. The progress along flat directions (eigen-directions of small eigenvalues), which dominates the final loss reduction, remains relatively slow. To enhance training dynamics along flat directions, we propose a curvature-conditioned multiscale momentum method with sphere constraints, delivering steady acceleration in LLM pretraining. This multiscale momentum, applied only along flat directions, pairs a slow-decay component for noise reduction with a fast-decay component for rapid curvature adaptation, harnessing their complementary strengths. Crucially, we employ a sphere constraint technique to prevent parameter inflation and excessively rapid effective learning rate decay that would otherwise arise from a naive combination. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method significantly accelerates Muon across diverse architectures (dense, MoE) and model sizes (0.12B--2.3B parameters). Theoretically, we verify the acceleration effect and provide insight into the design principles underlying the flat-direction multiscale momentum.

</details>


### [115] [How Proper Scoring Rules Shape LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28482)
*Benjamin Turtel,Paul Wilczewski,Kris Skotheim,Ville A. Satopää,Philip E. Tetlock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种严格适当评分规则（proper scoring rules）作为训练目标对LLM预测器性能与行为的影响。尽管这些规则理论上都激励诚实概率报告，但训练出的模型在校准度、概率使用及偏差-信息-噪声构成上存在差异，而聚合准确度和判别力差异较小。Brier训练的模型取得最低Brier分数和最高AUC-ROC，log训练的模型取得最高log分数和最低校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究默认适当评分规则作为训练目标在理论上等价，但实践中是否能产生可互换的模型效果尚不清楚。作者希望通过系统性比较来揭示奖励函数选择对LLM预测器的影响是否仅限于聚合指标，还是会深入影响模型的行为结构和误差构成。

Method: 作者以已解决的真实世界事件的二元预测为任务，将五种适当评分规则分别作为训练目标训练LLM预测器，并从校准度、概率使用、偏差、信息量和噪声等多个维度对模型进行系统比较，同时评估聚合准确度与判别力。

Result: 不同评分规则训练的模型在聚合准确度和判别力上差异较小，但校准度、概率使用及偏差-信息-噪声的结构性特征差异显著。Brier-trained模型在Brier分数和AUC-ROC上最优；log-trained模型在log分数和校准误差上最优。即使聚合表现相近的模型，其误差结构（偏差、信息、噪声的组合）也不同。由于每个条件仅使用单一随机种子，部分差异可能源于训练随机性。

Conclusion: 严格适当评分规则作为训练目标在实践中并不需要表现可互换。奖励函数的选择不仅影响LLM的预测表现，还决定了其预测误差如何结构化分布。因此，在LLM预测器的训练中，评分规则的选择应被视为一个可能对模型行为产生深远影响的关键设计决策。

Abstract: This paper evaluates how reward function choice shapes the performance and behavior of LLM forecasters. We compare five proper scoring rules as training objectives for binary forecasts of resolved real-world events. Although the rules share the same theoretical incentive for truthful probability reporting, the resulting models differ in calibration, probability use, and estimated profiles of bias, information, and noise, with smaller differences in aggregate accuracy and discrimination. The Brier-trained model has the lowest observed Brier score and highest AUC-ROC, while the log-trained model has the highest observed log score and lowest calibration error. Models with similar aggregate performance also reach that performance through different combinations of bias, information, and noise. Proper scoring rules therefore need not behave interchangeably as training objectives. Reward choice may shape not only how well an LLM forecasts, but how its forecasting errors are structured. Each condition uses a single seed, so some differences may reflect training stochasticity.

</details>


### [116] [REPLICANT: Learning Policies for Evading and Hardening Malware Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28499)
*Shae McFadden,Ilias Tsingenopoulos,Mario D'Onghia,Alexander Herzog,Myles Foley,Chris Hicks,Lorenzo Cavallaro,Fabio Pierazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Replicant，一个基于深度强化学习的框架，用于在严格的仅标签黑盒威胁模型下学习可重复使用的恶意软件逃避策略。该策略可以跨样本、检测器和特征空间迁移，在七个Android恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间上实现了78.8%的平均攻击成功率，优于现有最先进方法20.9%-39.2%，同时用于对抗训练时能产生泛化能力更强的检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估基于机器学习的恶意软件检测在实际场景中的鲁棒性需要针对高能力对手进行测试，但现有最先进的攻击方法往往假设对手拥有特权信息（如训练数据、特征空间或置信度分数），无法有效建模真实世界中的攻击者。因此需要一种在严格标签仅（label-only）黑盒威胁模型下进行逃避攻击的框架。

Method: Replicant采用深度强化学习框架，学习一个可重复使用的策略来指导两个关键决策：如何修改恶意软件样本以及何时查询目标检测器。该策略可跨样本、检测器及特征空间迁移，在严格的仅标签黑盒威胁模型下运作。

Result: 在七个Android恶意软件检测器和三个特征空间上，Replicant达到78.8%的平均攻击成功率，相对最先进方法提升了20.9%-39.2%，是最强且最具查询效率的方法。此外，用于对抗训练时，Replicant产生的检测器具有更可泛化的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 学习逃避任务不仅能带来更强的攻击性能，更重要的是为加固恶意软件检测器提供了更好的信号。Replicant证明了在严格黑盒设置下进行强化学习方法学习的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: To determine the real-world effectiveness of machine learning based malware detection, it is vital to evaluate its robustness against highly capable adversaries. However, state-of-the-art attacks do not effectively model realistic adversaries, as they often assume access to privileged information such as the training data, feature space, or confidence scores of the target. In this work, we present Replicant, a deep reinforcement learning framework that learns the realistic task of evasion under a strict label-only black-box threat model. Replicant learns a reusable policy on how to modify a malware sample and when to query the target, which transfers across samples, detectors, and feature spaces. Across seven Android malware detectors and three feature spaces, Replicant is the strongest and most query-efficient approach achieving a mean attack success rate of 78.8%, a relative improvement of 20.9%-39.2% over the state-of-the-art. Furthermore, when used for adversarial training, Replicant also outperforms the state-of-the art by producing detectors with more generalizable robustness. With Replicant we demonstrate that learning the task of evasion not only results in stronger attack performance but, crucially, provides a better signal for hardening malware detectors.

</details>


### [117] [An Enclosed Mode Is a Gauge Choice: Topology Relative to Reach in Certified Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28541)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究采样门（sampling gate）验证下的代码世界模型（code world model）的精确性与错误代价边界。当遗漏区域为环形冻结模式（annular freeze mode）时，认证模型在可达查询集上精确、在不可达区域（gauge）上任意错误。通过最小环形仪器证明最坏情况（错误拓扑的实心圆盘伪影）无法被任何采样门证伪且位级无害；并用LLM跨三个模型家族实验，发现通道宽度γ将伪影驱动通过三个区域：不可证伪且无害、可证伪且昂贵、立即证伪。三个原则组织实证结果：危险本质上是相对可达性的拓扑；修复受参数和传感器限制；缓解必须匹配错误的维度与方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索被采样门接受（certified）的代码世界模型在遗漏环形冻结模式时的认知边界与错误代价。核心问题：认证模型能知道什么、错误可能造成多大代价，以及如何度量和缓解这些错误。

Method: 引入门商（gate quotient）使问题精确化：带确定性接受的模型在可达查询集上被精确确定，可达之外是规范（gauge）。在最小环形仪器上证明极值情况，并通过三个模型家族的LLM合成实验，测量单一旋钮（通道宽度γ）如何驱动同一伪影穿越三个区域。采用持续同调（persistent homology）摘要作为传感器，对比点栅栏、维度匹配的持续栅栏和双自由度证书三种缓解策略。

Result: （1）可达通道使盲模型的利用代价从1.09骤降至~0（γ~0.1处的拐点），而隐藏通道保持全强度代价1.12。（2）无模型家族能从外部证据恢复该区域；从内部模型能提出正确拓扑但无法确定参数，且提出的拓扑跟随引导的持续同调摘要的错误β₁而非真实值。（3）点栅栏对一维边界无效，维度匹配的持续栅栏将利用代价从0.999降至0.058（两课瞬态），双自由度证书对称地消除发明模式失败（1.769降至0.029）。在n维中，壳体使误识别几乎必然而危险仍完全可利用：两轴独立。

Conclusion: 认证模型的可靠性边界本质上是拓扑相对于可达性的：可用的通道会刺破盲模型的盲区，而隐藏通道保持其错误全强度。修复受参数与传感器的双重限制，缓解策略必须与错误的维度方向和维度匹配。n维下误识别概率与可利用性两者独立，构成一个重要的安全设计考量。

Abstract: A code world model accepted by a sampling gate can be exactly right on everything the gate can see and arbitrarily wrong beyond it. We characterize what a certified model can know, and what its errors can cost, when the omission is an annular freeze mode enclosing an unreachable interior. The gate quotient makes the question precise: acceptance-with-certainty determines the model exactly on the reachable query set; beyond reach is gauge. On a minimal ring instrument we prove the extreme case (a wrong-topology filled-disc artifact unfalsifiable by any sampling gate and bitwise harmless at play) and measure, with LLM synthesis across three model families, how one knob (a channel of width gamma) walks the same artifact through three regimes: unfalsifiable-and-harmless, falsifiable-and-costly, and instantly falsified. Three principles organize the empirics. First, danger is topology relative to reach: a channel the planner can use collapses the blind model's exploitation (play cost 1.09 to ~0 over a knee at gamma ~ 0.1), while a hidden channel with the same first Betti number keeps it at full strength (1.12). Second, repair is parameter-bound and sensor-bound: no family recovers the region from outside evidence; from inside, models pose the right topology but cannot pin its parameters, and the posed topology tracks the guiding persistent-homology summary's wrong beta_1 (a sensor with a measured geometric resolution limit), not the truth. Third, mitigation must match the error's dimension and direction: point fences fail against the one-dimensional boundary, a dimension-matched persisted fence collapses exploitation to a two-lesson transient (0.999 to 0.058), and the dual freedom certificate collapses the invented-mode failure symmetrically (1.769 to 0.029). In n dimensions the shell makes misidentification near-certain while the danger stays fully exploitable: the two axes are independent.

</details>


### [118] [DARTS: Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28547)
*Aaryan Ajay Sharma,Sai Nishanth Padala,Seganrasan Subramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTS提出了一种针对解码器模型（LLM）的表示偏差校正方法。相比编码器模型，解码器模型中表示偏差因因果注意力掩码而跨token位置累积，且不同token位置的重要性不同（高熵位置更关键）。DARTS采用熵加权L1损失和高精度每位置加性偏差，在Llama-2-7B上对代码生成、数学推理和指令遵循三个任务都显著优于标准外科手术（surgery）方法，仅增加0.1%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可将多个任务特化的微调LLM合并为多任务模型而无需额外训练，但合并模型存在表示偏差（系统漂移）。已有工作(Yang et al., 2024a)在编码器视觉模型中研究并缓解该偏差，但解码器模型因自回归特性而未被研究。本文指出了解码器中存在的两个编码器没有的挑战（位置依赖偏差累积和token位置重要性不均），需要专门的解决方案。

Method: DARTS（Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery）方法包含两个核心设计：(1) 熵加权L1损失，在高熵（决策关键）位置加大校正权重，因为那里的错误对生成质量影响最大；(2) 每位置加性偏差（per-position additive bias），用以捕获位置依赖的误差，同时避免过参数化。该校正模块轻量，仅占总参数的0.1%。

Result: 在三个领域（代码生成HumanEval、数学推理GSM8K、指令遵循AlpacaEval）上使用Llama-2-7B模型进行广泛评估，DARTS显著优于标准的surgery方法，同时仅增加可忽略的参数（总参数的0.1%）。

Conclusion: DARTS有效解决了解码器模型合并中的表示偏差问题，通过熵加权损失和位置依赖校正，在不增加大量参数的情况下显著提升了合并模型的多任务生成质量，填补了该领域对解码器模型研究的空白。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific fine-tuned LLMs into a single multi-task model without additional training. However, merged models are known to suffer from representation bias: systematic drift between the merged model's hidden states and those of each individual source model. Prior work (Yang et al., 2024a) study and mitigate this bias for encoder-based vision models using a lightweight correction module trained with L1 loss. However, such bias is not studied for decoder models due to their autoregressive nature. We analyze the problem of representation bias in decoder models, and show two challenges absent in encoders: (1) the causal attention mask causes bias to accumulate across token positions, requiring position-dependent correction; and (2) not all token positions are equally important, i.e., high-entropy (decision-critical) positions matter far more than low-entropy ones. To address these challenges, we propose Decoder-Aware Representation Tuning via Surgery (DARTS). DARTS employs a novel entropy-weighted L1 loss to upweight correction at high-entropy positions where errors most affect generation quality, and a per-position additive bias that captures position-dependent error without overparameterization. We perform extensive evaluation on three domains: code generation (HumanEval), mathematical reasoning (GSM8K), and instruction following (AlpacaEval) on Llama-2-7B models, and show DARTS achieves significant improvement over the standard surgery approach while adding negligible parameters ($0.1\%$ of total parameters).

</details>


### [119] [Advancing Interaction-Sensitive Feature Selection: Novel Relief-Based Algorithms, Expanded Comparisons, and Recommendations for Biomedical Data Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28552)
*Kia Kazemi-Nia,Harsh Bandhey,Philip J. Freda,Ryan J. Urbanowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重构并扩展了scikit-rebate Python包，加入了5种新的Relief-based算法（RBA）变体，并在多种模拟基因组数据上进行基准比较。结果显示MultiSWRFDB*在检测2-way和3-way交互方面表现最佳，重构后算法运行时间降低10-35倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维生物医学数据建模前需要可靠的特征选择以减少计算开销并提高模型性能。但传统过滤式方法难以检测特征交互，而包装式或嵌入式方法计算成本过高。Relief-based算法（RBA）作为过滤方法对特征交互敏感且避免了这些限制，因此需要优化现有实现并提出新变体。

Method: （1）重构、优化并扩展scikit-rebate Python包，包含现有和新提出的RBA变体（如SWRF*、mu-Relief及5个新RBA变体）；（2）在不同样本量、特征数、遗传力和关联类型（主效应与交互）的模拟基因组数据集上进行严格的RBA基准比较，评估预测特征排序和运行时间。

Result: 除mu-Relief外，所有RBA均能有效检测噪声数据中的2-way交互。采用'远'评分策略的RBA最擅长检测2-way交互（MultiSWRFDB*表现最佳），但对主效应不敏感。SWRF、MultiSWRF、MultiSURF和MultiSWRFDB在主效应和2-way交互数据上表现最佳，MultiSWRFDB在考虑3-way交互时最优。代码重构使运行时间降低10-35倍。

Conclusion: 新引入的RBA变体（尤其是MultiSWRFDB）在检测主效应和上位性交互方面表现优异，能同时保留主效应和2-way上位交互信号，为下游建模保留了预测信号，是对scikit-rebate包的重要增强。

Abstract: As a precursor to high-dimensional biomedical data modeling, reliable feature selection can reduce computational expense, improve modeling performance, and yield simpler, more interpretable models. However, most filter-based feature selection methods struggle to detect feature interactions, while wrapper or embedded feature selection methods are computationally expensive. Relief-based algorithms (RBAs) are filter methods that are sensitive to feature interactions while mitigating these other limitations. This study (1) refactors, optimizes, and expands the scikit-rebate Python package with existing and newly proposed RBA variants and (2) conducts rigorous RBA benchmark comparisons across diverse genomic simulations. We expand scikit-rebate to include SWRF*, mu-Relief, and 5 novel RBA variants implementing alternative strategies for neighbor selection and feature scoring. All RBAs were evaluated to compare predictive feature ranking and runtime across simulated genomic datasets varying in sample size, number of features, heritability, and underlying association type (e.g. main effects and interactions). All RBAs, except mu-Relief, were proficient in detecting 2-way interactions in noisy data. RBAs utilizing 'far' scoring were best at detecting 2-way interactions - with MultiSWRFDB* top-performing - but were far less sensitive to main effects. SWRF, MultiSWRF, MultiSURF, and MultiSWRFDB yielded top performance across main effect and 2-way interaction datasets with MultiSWRFDB performing best when also considering 3-way interactions. Refactoring of scikit-rebate resulted in 10 to 35-fold reductions in RBA runtimes. The newly introduced RBAs were among the strongest performing, and by robustly retaining both main effects and 2-way epistatic interactions, these algorithms preserve predictive signals for downstream modeling.

</details>


### [120] [Blog: Survey of Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28557)
*Ruoran Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2025-2026年神经网络优化器设计空间的扩展，从传统的Adam变体扩展到沿时间估计、更新几何、视界管理、表示与系统四个独立轴线的系统化设计框架，并得出矩阵感知方法是真实进步但不存在与上下文无关的AdamW替代品的经验结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络优化领域已不再仅是Adam变体的迭代更替，设计空间已从坐标扩展到矩阵和层、从固定训练视界扩展到时变策略、从数学更新规则扩展到需兼容分片与低精度计算的状态表示。需要一个系统化的组织框架来整合这些分散的进展。

Method: 该综述沿着四个基本独立的轴线组织近年优化器与训练优化方法：时间估计（如Shampoo和SOAP的历史矩阵统计）、更新几何（如Muon的谱归一化）、视界管理（如schedule-free训练和小批次修正）、表示与系统（如内存高效优化器与量化优化器状态），并涵盖自适应/混合矩阵方法和记忆高效优化器。

Result: 核心经验结论并非乐观的：矩阵感知方法代表真正的进步，但不存在与上下文无关的AdamW替代品。优化器排名取决于模型规模、数据与参数比、批大小、调度、参数划分、调参预算以及目标指标（tokens、FLOPs、墙钟时间或内存）。

Conclusion: 实际结论是应采用组合式的优化器设计视角，并建立更严格的优化器声明评估协议，认识到没有单一优化器能在所有上下文中普遍胜出。

Abstract: Neural-network optimization in 2025-2026 is no longer well described as a succession of new Adam variants. The design space has expanded from coordinates to matrices and layers, from fixed training horizons to policies over time, and from mathematical update rules to state representations that must survive sharding and low-precision computation. This survey organizes recent optimizers and training optimization methods along four largely independent axes: temporal estimation, update geometry, horizon management, and representation and systems. It connects the spectral normalization of Muon, the historical matrix statistics of Shampoo and SOAP, adaptive and hybrid matrix methods, memory-efficient optimizers, schedule-free training, small-batch corrections, and quantized optimizer states. The central empirical conclusion is deliberately non-triumphal: matrix-aware methods represent a genuine advance, but there is no context-independent replacement for AdamW. Rankings change with model scale, data-to-parameter ratio, batch size, schedule, parameter partition, tuning budget, and whether the target metric is tokens, FLOPs, wall-clock time, or memory. The practical consequence is a compositional view of optimizer design and a stricter protocol for evaluating optimizer claims.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Vectorized Symmetric and Fermionic Tensor Network Implementations for GPU-Accelerated Variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27861)
*Si-Jing Du,Johnnie Gray,Ao Chen,Garnet Kin-Lic Chan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对张量网络变分蒙特卡洛（TN-VMC）算法未能充分利用GPU加速的问题，提出了面向GPU的向量化对称张量网络VMC框架，通过开发"扁平化"张量网络形式主义实现块稀疏张量的批量计算，在二维Fermi-Hubbard模型中实现了相较于单核CPU高达300倍的GPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于张量网络的变分蒙特卡洛方法在强关联自旋和费米子系统的基态计算中已达到有竞争力的精度，但现有TN-VMC算法在形式化上未能充分利用GPU并行加速能力。GPU加速的关键在于对张量网络振幅和操作进行向量化评估，即对结构相同的计算进行批量处理以大幅提升GPU利用率，这是现有算法所缺失的核心环节。

Method: 本文的关键技术贡献是实现张量网络计算的向量化，通过对具有阿贝尔对称性的张量网络（包括费米子张量网络）开发一种"扁平化"张量网络形式主义，用于块稀疏张量的表示与收缩。基于这一形式主义，构建了GPU自适应的对称TN-VMC工作流，将张量网络计算进行批量（batched）处理，确保结构相同的计算能够被高效地并行化。

Result: 在二维Fermi-Hubbard模型中，采用费米子张量网络变分波函数，实现了相较于单核CPU实现最高300倍的GPU加速。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了TN-VMC在GPU加速方面的关键空白，证明了通过向量化和批量计算可以大幅提升对称张量网络VMC的计算效率，为大规模强关联体系的变分计算提供了实用的高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) calculations based on tensor networks (TN) have recently achieved competitive accuracy in ground-state calculations of strongly correlated spin and fermionic systems. However, existing tensor network VMC (TN-VMC) algorithms have not been formulated in a manner that can fully utilize GPU acceleration. We tackle the key missing ingredient, namely, the vectorized evaluation of tensor network amplitudes and tensor network operations. This ensures high GPU utilization by batching over computations with identical structure. In particular, we show how to achieve vectorization for the practically relevant case of abelian symmetric tensor networks (which includes fermionic tensor networks) by developing a ``flat'' tensor network formalism for block-sparse tensor representation and contraction. Using this, we construct a GPU-adapted symmetric TN-VMC workflow with batched tensor network computation. In the two-dimensional Fermi--Hubbard model, we demonstrate a GPU speedup of up to $300 \times$ over single core CPU implementations, for fermionic TN variational wavefunctions.

</details>


### [122] [Higher-Order Topological Phase in the Two-Dimensional Type-IV Magnet MgCr$_2$O$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27918)
*Xiaorong Zou,Hyeon Suk Shin,Yanmei Zang,Ying Dai,Chengwang Niu,Chang-Jong Kang,Chang Woo Myung*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了二维IV型磁性材料MgCr₂O₄具有本征的高阶拓扑绝缘相，包含C₃z保护的角态和非平凡转动拓扑不变量χ⁽³⁾={-2,4}，量化分数角电荷为4e/3。自旋-轨道耦合打破自旋简并性（[C₂||M_z]）同时保留保护高阶拓扑的C₃z对称性，使自旋劈裂与非平凡拓扑共存。该拓扑相在宽范围双轴应变下不关闭带隙、不发生拓扑相变，表现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IV型二维磁性是一种新分类的共线磁相，具有非相对论自旋简并和自旋-轨道耦合诱导的动量依赖自旋劈裂，扩展了共线磁体的对称性分类，为非常规拓扑量子态提供了新机遇。作者旨在建立IV型磁系统与高阶拓扑之间的联系，探索对称性工程化的磁性拓扑量子态。

Method: 以二维IV型磁性材料MgCr₂O₄为研究对象。通过对称性分析识别其C₃z旋转保护的高阶拓扑相，计算转动拓扑不变量χ⁽³⁾和分数角电荷，并考察自旋-轨道耦合对自旋简并性对称性[C₂||M_z]与拓扑保护对称性的不同影响，以及双轴应变下的拓扑相稳定性。

Result: MgCr₂O₄是本征高阶拓扑绝缘体，具有C₃z保护的角态、转动拓扑不变量χ⁽³⁾={-2,4}和量化分数角电荷4e/3。自旋-轨道耦合可打破自旋简并性对称性但保留拓扑保护的C₃z对称性，实现自旋劈裂与拓扑共存。宽范围双轴应变下拓扑相保持完整，无带隙关闭或拓扑相变。

Conclusion: 该工作直接建立了IV型磁性系统与高阶拓扑之间的联系，证明自旋劈裂与非平凡拓扑可共存，且拓扑态对外部扰动（应变）具有鲁棒性，为对称性工程化的磁性拓扑量子态提供了新路径。

Abstract: Type-IV two-dimensional (2D) magnetism-a newly classified collinear magnetic phase featuring nonrelativistic spin degeneracy and spin-orbit-coupling-induced momentum-dependent spin splitting-extends the symmetry classification of collinear magnets, opening new opportunities for unconventional topological quantum states. Here, we reveal that the recently proposed two-dimensional type-IV 2D magnet MgCr$_2$O$_4$ hosts an intrinsic higher-order topological insulating phase, featuring $\mathcal{C}_{3z}$-protected corner states and a nontrivial rotational topological invariant of $χ^{(3)}$ = $\{-2,4\}$ with a quantized fractional corner charge of $4e/3$. Spin-orbit coupling breaks the spin-degeneracy-enforcing symmetry $[C_{2}||M_z]$ while preserving the crystalline $\mathcal{C}_{3z}$ rotational symmetry that protects the higher-order topological phase, thereby enabling spin splitting to coexist with the nontrivial topology. Furthermore, the higher-order topological phase remains intact throughout a wide range of biaxial strains without band-gap closing and topological phase transition, demonstrating the robustness of the symmetry-protected topological state against external perturbations. Our work establishes a direct connection between type-IV magnetic system and higher-order topology, providing a new route for symmetry-engineered magnetic topological quantum states.

</details>


### [123] [Accelerated S-NFC for Million-Chaff RCS Computation Using Low-Rank Compression of Concatenated Block Rows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27936)
*Dong-Yeop Na,Somyeong Lee,Chung Hyun Lee*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种加速S-NFC（通过忽略远场耦合实现稀疏化）的方法，通过将接收箔条单元保留的非对角交互块拼接并进行共享接收侧基的联合低秩分解，同时保留精确自交互和复用预计算的LU分解。在一百万箔条羽流测试中，端到端速度提升6.92倍，耦合存储仅占6.60%，复远场误差为0.253%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-NFC已能通过仅保留显著局部电磁交互来实现大规模箔条云全波雷达截面分析，但保留耦合的存储和矩阵-向量乘法成本仍较高，且广义共轭残差求解器迭代次数较多。本文旨在进一步加速S-NFC算法。

Method: 将每个接收箔条单元保留的离对角交互块拼接起来，应用共享接收侧基的联合低秩分解；保留精确的自交互，并利用重复箔条模板复用预计算的自交互块LU分解。压缩后的公式减少了保留耦合的存储和矩阵-向量乘法成本，并减少了广义共轭残差求解器的迭代次数。

Result: 数值测试表明：在稀疏状态下低秩近似的复杂远场误差低于1%；在足够大的平均间距下存在实际的纯自交互极限。对于一百万箔条羽流，压缩S-NFC比未压缩版本实现6.92倍端到端加速，仅存储6.60%的保留耦合，复远场误差为0.253%。

Conclusion: 所提出的联合低秩压缩方法能够显著提升S-NFC的计算效率与存储效率，同时保持较高的远场精度，适用于超大规模箔条云的快速全波RCS分析。

Abstract: Sparsification via neglecting far-field coupling (S-NFC) enables fast full-wave radar-cross-section analysis of large-scale chaff clouds by retaining only significant local electromagnetic interactions. This letter further accelerates S-NFC by concatenating the retained off-diagonal interaction blocks associated with each receiving chaff element and applying a joint low-rank factorization with a shared receiving-side basis. Exact self interactions are preserved, while repeated chaff templates reuse precomputed lower--upper factorizations of the self-interaction blocks. The compressed formulation reduces retained-coupling storage and matrix--vector multiplication cost and also decreases the number of iterations required by the generalized conjugate residual solver. Numerical tests with 100,000 chaff elements demonstrate sub-$1\%$ complex-far-field error for low-rank approximations in sparse regimes and identify a practical self-only limit at sufficiently large mean spacing. For a one-million-chaff plume, the proposed compressed S-NFC achieves a $6.92\times$ end-to-end speedup over uncompressed S-NFC while storing only $6.60\%$ of the retained coupling, with a complex-far-field error of $0.253\%$.

</details>


### [124] [A Synthetic Iterative Scheme for Non-Gray Phonon Boltzmann Transport Equation with Dual Relaxation Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28101)
*Dingtao Shen,Jia Liu,Wei Su*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文为稳态线性化非灰Callaway声子玻尔兹曼输运方程开发了一种通用的合成迭代方案（GSIS），通过能量与准动量平衡定律闭合的Chapman-Enskog本构关系构造合成近似，将动力学扫掠与混合不连续伽辽金离散耦合，克服了传统迭代方案在大特征长度下收敛缓慢的问题，并在流体动力学与扩散极限下分别退化为Guyer-Krumhansl类方程和傅里叶导热定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迭代方案（CIS）在求解非灰Callaway方程时，由于碰撞源迭代在大特征长度材料中收敛极慢，严重制约了计算效率。已有的合成加速方案要么只适用于非灰单弛豫模型，要么只适用于灰双弛豫模型，缺乏针对模态分辨的双弛豫输运问题的专用加速框架，亟需一种通用的高效迭代方法。

Method: 提出通用合成迭代方案（GSIS）：利用精确的能量和准动量平衡定律，结合一阶Chapman-Enskog本构关系闭合，构造法向过程伪温度和声子漂移速度的合成近似；阻性过程伪温度由二者导出。每步迭代耦合迎风节点不连续伽辽金动力学扫掠与混合不连续伽辽金合成方程求解，实现高阶空间离散。

Result: 通过分支和频率分辨的傅里叶分析揭示了CIS收敛退化的机理，并证明GSIS的收缩因子在研究石墨材料中始终远离1（即有界收敛）。渐近分析表明GSIS在流体动力学极限下退化为Guyer-Krumhansl类方程的一致离散，在扩散极限下退化为傅里叶导热定律。

Conclusion: GSIS为模态分辨的非灰双弛豫声子输运问题提供了一种可靠的加速求解框架，兼具结构一致的极限行为和稳定的迭代收敛性，有效弥合了现有加速方案在非灰双弛豫模型上的空白。

Abstract: Solving the non-gray Callaway phonon Boltzmann transport equation allows a dual-relaxation-time approximation of separate normal and resistive scatterings and resolving the mode-dependent spectrum. The conventional iterative scheme (CIS) for deterministic solutions avoids a monolithic phase-space inversion, but its collision-source iteration can become prohibitively slow at a large characteristic length of a material. Existing synthetic acceleration schemes address either non-gray single-relaxation models or gray dual-relaxation models, leaving mode-resolved dual-relaxation transport without a dedicated acceleration framework. We develop a general synthetic iterative scheme (GSIS) for the stationary, linearized, non-gray Callaway equation, where synthetic approximations for the normal-process pseudo-temperature and phonon drift velocity are provided by exact energy and quasi-momentum balance laws closed with first-order Chapman-Enskog constitutive relations and non-equilibrium terms evaluated from the kinetic solution. The resistive-process pseudo-temperature is retrieved from the two quantities. Each iteration couples an upwind nodal discontinuous Galerkin kinetic sweep and a hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin solution of the synthetic equations to achieve high-order spatial discretization. A branch- and frequency-resolved Fourier analysis identifies the deterioration of CIS and shows that the GSIS contraction factor remains bounded away from unity for the considered graphene material. Asymptotic analysis indicates that GSIS reduces to a consistent discretization of a Guyer-Krumhansl-like equation and Fourier's law of heat conduction in the hydrodynamic and diffusive limits, respectively.

</details>


### [125] [Editorial: Promoting Green Computing in High Energy Physics and Astrophysics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28193)
*Vasiliki A. Mitsou,Andreas Redelbach,Eleni Vardoulaki*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨并推动高能物理与天体物理学领域的绿色计算策略，旨在评估当前能源消耗模式、识别资源优化机会，并通过跨学科合作促进可持续性发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理和天体物理学领域通常涉及大规模计算需求，能源消耗巨大。研究旨在应对日益增长的能源需求，探索绿色计算策略以实现可持续发展，同时通过跨学科合作寻找协同效应。

Method: 通过系统性评估当前能源消耗模式，分析现有计算技术的能效利用机会，识别高能物理与天体物理项目之间的潜在协同作用，并推动跨学科合作以促进绿色计算实践。

Result: 论文主要提出了该研究主题的核心问题框架：当前领域能源需求状况、现有技术的资源利用效率提升途径，以及高能物理与天体物理项目间的绿色计算协同可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究主题确立了绿色计算在高能物理和天体物理中的重要性，为后续研究提供了明确的研究方向和关键问题，强调通过资源优化与跨学科合作实现计算领域的可持续发展。

Abstract: This research topic aims to explore and promote strategies for green computing in high-energy physics and astrophysics. The primary objectives include assessing current energy consumption patterns, identifying opportunities for resource optimization, and fostering cross-disciplinary collaborations to enhance sustainability. Key questions to be addressed include: What are the current energy demands in these fields? How can existing technologies be leveraged for more efficient resource usage? What synergies can be identified between high-energy and astrophysics projects to promote green computing?

</details>


### [126] [Real-Time Monitoring of MHD Liquid Metal Flows with Shallow Recurrent Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28366)
*Claudio Scardino,Stefano Riva,Carolina Introini,Matteo Lo Verso,Eric Cervi,Antonio Cammi,Laura Savoldi*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了一种基于数据驱动的降阶模型框架——浅层循环解码器（SHRED）结合主成分分析（PCA），用于将稀疏温度测量映射到磁流体动力学（MHD）系统的全热工水力状态。在DEMO聚变堆增殖包层代表性三维域中，对磁场方向和强度进行双参数分析，SHRED在温度、压力和速度场上的平均相对误差约为5%，跨越0.075 T至0.300 T磁场范围和5至30度倾角，即使面对完全未见过的参数场景仍能保持精度，表明其适用于聚变设施实时监测与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚变堆液态金属包层的磁流体动力学状态估计对实时监测至关重要，但由于多物理场性质使得高保真仿真在实时应用中计算成本过高，亟需一种高效且精确的状态估计算法。

Method: 采用SHRED（Shallow Recurrent Decoder）与PCA相结合的数据驱动降阶模型框架，利用循环神经网络架构将稀疏温度传感器数据映射到完整热工水力系统状态，并在包含双圆柱水冷系统的三维DEMO增殖包层域中进行双参数（磁场方向与强度）系统性分析。

Result: SHRED重建在温度、压力和速度场上的平均相对误差约为5%，该精度在弱磁场（0.075 T）到强磁场（0.300 T）以及倾角5至30度范围内均能保持。误差仅略高于低秩截断给定的误差下限，且能捕捉从混沌到层流化的非线性流动转变，包括30度倾角下不对称侧层结构的形成。

Conclusion: SHRED被确立为一种可靠的、适用于复杂真实工程应用的状态估计器，能够处理完全未见过的参数场景，验证了其作为适合实时设施在线监测与控制的实时状态估计技术的有效性。

Abstract: State estimation in magnetohydrodynamic flows is critical for real-time monitoring of liquid metal blankets in tokamak fusion reactors. Due to the multiphysics nature of these phenomena, high-fidelity simulations are computationally prohibitive for real-time applications. This work investigates a data- driven Reduced Order Model framework: the Shallow Recurrent Decoder (SHRED) coupled with Principal Component Analysis, to map sparse temperature measurements to the full thermo-hydraulic system's state. The major contribution of this work lies in the two-parameter analysis of a fully three-dimensional domain representative of the DEMO breeding blanket configuration. Here, the flow is subjected to an external magnetic field varying in direction and intensity and is hindered by two cylinders acting as a water-cooling system, which impose a temperature boundary condition on their surfaces. This double-parametric magnetic variation induces nonlinear transitions in the flow dynamics, ranging from chaotic behavior at low magnetic field intensities to laminarized regimes at high intensities, characterized by the formation of asymmetric side layers at an inclination angle of 30 degrees. SHRED reconstruction maintains a mean relative error of approximately 5% for the temperature, pressure, and velocity fields. This accuracy is maintained across both weak and strong magnetic fields, ranging from 0.075 T to 0.300 T, and for inclination angles from 5 to 30 degrees, reflecting its dominant toroidal component. These errors are only slightly larger than the lower error bound dictated by low-rank truncation. The results establish SHRED as a reliable state estimator for complex and realistic engineering applications involving completely unseen parametric scenarios and validate it as an accurate real-time state estimation technique suitable for online monitoring and control of real facilities.

</details>


### [127] [Modelling platelet dynamics in blood flow: an unresolved DEM approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28483)
*Francesca Zucchelli,Carmine Porcaro,Mahdi Saeedipour,Bastien Chopard,Jonas Latt*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非解析的介观尺度CFD-DEM血小板动力学模型，将刚性扁球血小板与可变形红细胞耦合，在OpenFOAM-LIGGGHTS开源平台上实现。模型经过圆柱微血管中验证后，用于表征血小板边缘化，发现血小板扩散系数随血管尺寸和剪切速率增大、对Ht不敏感，CFL随剪切速率增厚随Ht变薄，且扁球形状使血小板边缘化更快、扩散系数比球形高近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有血流计算模型存在两难：全解析的基于细胞的方法能忠实再现单个细胞动力学但在血管尺度计算代价过高；连续介质模型可扩展但对粒子运动"失明"。需要一种介于两者之间的可扩展且保留粒子动力学的介观尺度模型。

Method: 采用非解析的CFD-DEM方法：血小板建模为刚性扁球粒子，受方向依赖的曳力、升力和水动力力矩闭合；红细胞采用已有的非解析可变形描述；基于开源OpenFOAM-LIGGGHTS耦合框架实现。模型先在直径100-200μm圆柱微血管中对墙剪切率150-1650s^-1及Ht=10-20%条件进行验证，再用于量化血小板扩散系数和CFL（细胞自由层）的形成及其对参数依赖。

Result: 模型成功复现血小板边缘化现象。血小板扩散系数随血管尺寸和剪切率增大，对Ht不敏感；CFL随剪切率增厚、随Ht变薄。形状敏感性显著：扁球血小板边缘化更快、扩散系数比球形替代物高近一个数量级，两者达到相似的稳态分布但时间路径明显不同。

Conclusion: 即使在介观尺度，该模型仍能捕捉血小板形状驱动的动力学，且计算成本远低于全解析方法，向可扩展的全血模型迈进一步。

Abstract: Computational models of blood flow are caught between fully resolved cell-based methods, which faithfully reproduce the dynamics of individual cells but are computationally prohibitive at vessel scale, and continuum models, scalable yet blind to particle motion. We present an unresolved, mesoscale computational fluid dynamics-discrete element method (CFD-DEM) model of platelet dynamics that bridges this gap and, coupled with an existing unresolved description of deformable red blood cells, moves closer to a scalable model of whole blood. Within this framework, implemented on the open source OpenFOAM-LIGGGHTS coupling, platelets are represented as rigid oblate particles advanced by orientation dependent drag, lift and hydrodynamic torque closures. The model is first validated against resolved simulations and experiments in cylindrical microvessels of diameter $100-200\,μm$, across wall shear rates $\dotγ=150-1650\,s^{-1}$ and hematocrit Ht $=10-20\%$ then used to characterize platelet margination. The model quantifies diffusion coefficient, CFL formation and their dependence on shear rate, Ht and channel size. In particular, platelet diffusivity grows with vessel size and shear rate, while remaining insensitive to Ht, whereas CFL thickens with shear rate and thins with Ht. We further show that even at this mesoscale level, the model remains sensitive to platelet shape: oblate platelets marginate faster and diffuse nearly an order of magnitude more than their spherical surrogates, reaching a comparable steady state distribution but along markedly different temporal paths. Together these results demonstrate that shape driven platelet dynamics can be recovered at a fraction of the cost of fully resolved methods.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

### [128] [An Adaptive Augmented Lagrangian Method for Deterministic and Stochastic Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27676)
*Tianzhu Liu,Michael J. O'Neill*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非精确增广拉格朗日算法，用于求解非线性非凸优化问题。该算法采用自适应惩罚参数更新和完整的对偶步长，在确定性、随机目标函数以及随机约束等多种场景下均达到已知最优的最坏情况复杂度（至多差对数因子）。在CUTEst测试问题上的实验以及机器学习中的随机约束优化问题数值实验均验证了该方法的实用性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来具有最坏情况复杂度保证的增广拉格朗日方法通常采用非自适应的惩罚参数和较短的对偶步长，这在实践中可能限制算法性能。本文旨在设计一种更具实用性的非精确增广拉格朗日算法，既能保持最优的复杂度保证，又能在实际求解中展现更好的收敛表现。

Method: 提出一种非精确增广拉格朗日算法，核心特点包括：(1) 自适应地更新惩罚参数；(2) 使用完整的对偶步长（full dual stepsizes）。作者在三种问题设定下分析了该方法的复杂度：函数与约束均确定性、函数随机但约束确定性、函数与约束均随机。

Result: 在三种设定下，该方法的最坏情况复杂度均与已知增广拉格朗日方法的最优结果匹配（仅差对数因子）。在CUTEst测试问题上的确定性实验表明，相比非自适应惩罚参数和/或短对偶步长的增广拉格朗日方法，本方法具有明显的实践优势。在机器学习中的随机约束优化问题上的数值结果也进一步验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 通过自适应惩罚参数更新与完整对偶步长的结合，该算法在理论上保持了最优复杂度保证，同时在实践中显著优于现有增广拉格朗日方法，实现了理论保证与数值性能的良好平衡。

Abstract: We present an inexact Augmented Lagrangian algorithm for solving nonlinear, non-convex optimization problems. Unlike most recently proposed Augmented Lagrangian methods with worst-case complexity guarantees, we utilize adaptive penalty parameter updates and full dual stepsizes. We show that the method matches the best known worst-case complexity results for Augmented Lagrangian methods (up to logarithmic factors) when both the function and constraints are deterministic, when the function is stochastic and the constraints are deterministic, and when both are stochastic. Experiments on CUTEst test problems confirm the practical advantages of the proposed approach over Augmented Lagrangian methods with non-adaptive penalty parameters and/or short dual step sizes in the deterministic setting. Numerical results on stochastic constrained optimization problems in machine learning also confirm these findings.

</details>


### [129] [Solving Quasi-Variational Inequalities Using the Progressive Decoupling of Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27694)
*Manoel Jardim,Claudia Sagastizábal,Mikhail Solodov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文受优化和变分不等式领域中逐步解耦连接方法论的启发，提出了一种将拟变分不等式转化为一系列变分不等式序列来求解的算法。该方法在一定的正则性条件下局部收敛，当这些条件在整个定义域成立时全局收敛。此外，在另一类假设下，对具有"移动集合"特性的拟变分不等式建立了线性速率的全局收敛性。算法在大规模Walrasian均衡问题（广义Nash均衡的特例）及相关文献中的其他实例上展现出优越的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟变分不等式问题比标准变分不等式更为复杂，其可行域依赖于当前解。现有的求解方法在效率、收敛保证和大规模问题适用性方面存在局限。作者希望借鉴优化和变分不等式领域中已成熟的"逐步解耦连接"方法论，设计一种能够将困难的拟变分不等式问题分解为一系列较简单的变分不等式子问题来求解的算法，从而在理论上保证收敛性并在实践中提高计算效率。

Method: 算法核心思想是将拟变分不等式问题转化为一个变分不等式序列进行求解。具体而言，通过逐步解耦可行集与当前解之间的耦合连接，将原问题分解为一系列标准变分不等式子问题。理论分析方面：一是在某些正则性条件下建立局部收敛性，当条件在整个定义域成立时拓展为全局收敛；二是在不同类型假设下，针对具有"移动集合"（moving set）性质的拟变分不等式类，证明了线性速率的全局收敛性。

Result: 算法在多种正则性假设下实现了局部收敛，并且在条件全域成立时达到全局收敛；对于"移动集合"类的拟变分不等式，在另一组假设下获得了线性速率的全局收敛结果。数值实验表明，该方法在大规模Walrasian均衡问题（即广义Nash均衡的特例）以及文献中其他相关实例上表现出优越的计算性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于变分不等式序列的拟变分不等式求解算法，在收敛性理论和计算效率两方面均取得了显著成果。该算法不仅为拟变分不等式提供了新的求解途径，而且在大规模经济均衡问题（如Walrasian均衡）中展示了实际应用的潜力，为广义Nash均衡等相关领域的数值求解提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Inspired by the progressive decoupling of linkages methodology for optimization and variational inequalities, we propose an algorithm for solving quasi-variational inequalities as a sequence of variational inequalities. Our method is shown to converge locally under some regularity conditions and globally when such conditions hold throughout the entire domain. Separately, under other type of assumptions, global convergence with linear rate is also established for the class of quasi-variational inequalities said to have a moving set. Advantageous computational performance is shown for large-scale Walrasian equilibrium problems, a special case of generalized Nash equilibria, as well as for some other instances encountered in related literature.

</details>


### [130] [On the Computational and Statistical Efficiency of the Empirical Maximum Entropy on the Mean Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27705)
*Matthew King-Roskamp,Gabriel Rioux,Rustum Choksi,Tim Hoheisel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对经验最大熵（MEM）方法提出了一种O(n^{-1/2})的期望参数收敛率，显著改进此前King-Roskamp等人(2026)建立的O(n^{-1/4})界，并通过将MEM对偶问题重述为期望风险最小化问题，将其纳入现代随机优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEM方法在求解逆问题时具有灵活性和有效性，但实际中先验分布未知需从数据估计。此前经验MEM的收敛率仅为O(n^{-1/4})，该界不够理想，限制了其统计效率的理论保证。

Method: 通过原始和对偶优化问题在概率测度扰动下的新颖稳定性分析来推导收敛率；该分析仅依赖凸分析和概率论的基础工具。同时证明MEM对偶问题可等价重构为期望风险最小化问题，从而接入随机优化的现代框架。

Result: 建立了经验MEM方法O(n^{-1/2})的期望参数收敛率，从O(n^{-1/4})改进至O(n^{-1/2})；同时展示了MEM对偶问题的期望风险最小化重构，使其能够采用可扩展的随机梯度算法应用于大规模逆问题。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了经验MEM在统计和计算两个维度上都是高效的数据驱动逆问题求解方法，兼具更好的理论收敛保证与可扩展的计算算法。

Abstract: The Maximum Entropy on the Mean (MEM) method provides a flexible computational framework for solving inverse problems by combining data fidelity with entropy-based regularization. In practice, however, the prior distribution is typically unknown but can be estimated from data, giving rise to the empirical MEM method. We establish a parametric convergence rate of $O(n^{-1/2})$ in expectation for empirical MEM, improving upon the previously established $O(n^{-1/4})$ guarantee by King-Roskamp et al. (2026). Our proof is based on a novel stability analysis of the primal and dual optimization problems under perturbations of the underlying probability measure, relying only on foundational tools from convex analysis and probability. We further show that the MEM dual problem admits a reformulation as an expected risk minimization problem, thereby placing MEM within the modern framework of stochastic optimization and enabling scalable stochastic gradient algorithms for large-scale inverse problems. Together, these results place empirical MEM as a statistically and computationally efficient methodology for data-driven inverse problems.

</details>


### [131] [Solution Methods for Infinite-Dimensional Generalized Disjunctive Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27707)
*Daniel Nguyen,Joshua L. Pulsipher*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了无限维广义析取规划（InfiniteGDP）的四类解法推广，并引入基于高斯过程的MBM-GP方法，以更低的改革成本保持多Big-M的紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InfiniteGDP抽象将GDP从有限维扩展到连续域（时间、空间、不确定性），但此前仅有big-M和hull两种松弛端点被推广到无限维。为填补这一方法论空白，需要泛化更多求解方法到无限维优化。

Method: 将多Big-M改革、P-分裂改革、切割平面改革和基于逻辑的外近似算法四种GDP求解方法推广至无限维优化；提出MBM-GP，利用高斯过程从子问题求解的子集中学习大规模Big-M函数；在Julia包InfiniteDisjunctiveProgramming.jl中实现全部方法。

Result: 在动态与随机优化的案例研究中，泛化的求解方法在性能上优于big-M和hull；MBM-GP在保持多Big-M紧致性的同时，以远低的改革成本实现等效效果。

Conclusion: 这些方法填补了InfiniteGDP中间解法的空白，为无限维混合离散-连续决策问题提供了更丰富的求解谱系，MBM-GP在紧致性与计算成本之间实现了有效平衡。

Abstract: Generalized disjunctive programming (GDP) expresses mixed discrete-continuous decisions through Boolean indicators and disjunctions, and can be systematically solved via a library of methods proposed in the literature. The recent InfiniteGDP abstraction lifts this modeling layer to continuous domains such as time, space, and uncertainty, but only the big-M and hull reformulations, the two endpoints of the relaxation spectrum, have been generalized to the infinite setting. This work closes this gap by generalizing four other GDP solution methods to infinite-dimensional optimization: the multiple big-M reformulation, P-split reformulation, cutting plane reformulation, and the logic-based outer approximation algorithm. It further proposes MBM-GP, a novel Gaussian-process variant of multiple big-M that learns the big-M function over the infinite domain from a small subset of the subproblem solves. Moreover, these approaches are implemented in the Julia package InfiniteDisjunctiveProgramming.jl. The methods are benchmarked on case studies arising in dynamic and stochastic optimization. The results demonstrate how the generalized solution methods can outperform big-M and hull, with MBM-GP retaining the tightness of multiple big-M at a fraction of its reformulation cost.

</details>


### [132] [Beyond Procrustes distances: a multilinear Gromov-Wasserstein distance capturing chirality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27774)
*Clément Soubrier,Geoffrey Woollard,Andrew Warren,Khanh Dao Duc*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多线性推广的Gromov-Wasserstein目标，用于形状数据分析，能够区分形状与其镜像，从而解决现有形状分析度量无法处理手性（chirality）的问题。特别地，对于G=SO(d)的情况，引入了对手性敏感的Chiral Gromov-Wasserstein（CGW）距离，并开发了高效算法，包括局部和近似全局解，以及完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。数值实验验证了CGW作为手性物体形状度量的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状数据分析在许多科学领域至关重要，而手性是许多应用（尤其是分子科学）中的基本属性。然而，现有的形状分析度量无法区分一个形状与其镜像，这是一个关键缺陷。因此需要一种能够对手性敏感的新的形状度量方法。

Method: 本文提出了多线性推广的Gromov-Wasserstein目标函数。在温和假设下，该目标在形状（表示为按对称群G取商的概率分布）之间产生距离。对于G=SO(d)，引入了Chiral Gromov-Wasserstein（CGW）距离。同时，通过将耦合投影到低维空间来重新表述底层优化问题，开发了高效算法，包括局部解和近似全局解算法，并推导出完全多项式时间近似方案。

Result: 在温和假设下，多线性Gromov-Wasserstein目标在形状之间产生距离；CGW距离对手性敏感。算法方面，通过低维投影重新表述优化问题，获得了局部解和近似全局解的算法以及完全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS）。数值实验证明了CGW作为手性物体形状度量的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为手性形状分析提供了理论基础和高效算法。多线性Gromov-Wasserstein距离建立了稳健的鲁棒性性质，CGW距离能够有效区分镜像形状，且算法在计算效率上具有理论保证。该框架有望在手性分子科学等领域产生重要应用价值。

Abstract: Efficiently and robustly analyzing shape data is critical across many scientific disciplines. While chirality is a fundamental property in numerous applications - most notably in molecular science - existing shape analysis metrics fail to distinguish between a shape and its mirror image. To address this gap, we introduce a multilinear generalization of the Gromov-Wasserstein objective. Under mild assumptions, this objective yields a distance between shapes, represented as probability distributions quotiented by a symmetry group $G$. In particular, for $G = SO(d)$, we introduce the Chiral Gromov-Wasserstein ($\mathrm{CGW}$) distance, sensitive to chirality. We establish robustness properties for the multilinear Gromov-Wasserstein distances and develop efficient algorithms to compute them, reformulating the underlying optimization problem by projecting couplings onto a low-dimensional space. We derive algorithms for both local and approximate global solutions, yielding a fully polynomial-time approximation scheme for these problems. We validate the framework through numerical experiments that demonstrate the effectiveness of $\mathrm{CGW}$ as a shape metric for chiral objects.

</details>


### [133] [Optimal control of fractional diffusion with Dirac measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27776)
*Enrique Otarola,Abner J. Salgado*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由谱分数阶拉普拉斯算子控制的椭圆型PDE约束优化问题，其中强制项为狄拉克测度的线性组合，控制变量为这些奇异源的振幅。证明了最优解的存在唯一性，推导了一阶最优性条件，提出基于有限元的离散化方案，并给出了先验误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶拉普拉斯算子在反常扩散、非局部相互作用等领域有广泛应用，而奇异源（狄拉克测度）作为强制项在实际问题中（如点源定位、源强度控制）具有重要物理意义。本文旨在为这类带奇异源的分数阶PDE优化控制问题建立理论框架和数值方法。

Method: 1) 在变分框架下分析带狄拉克测度强制项的谱分数阶拉普拉斯方程的最优控制问题；2) 证明最优解的存在唯一性并推导一阶最优性条件（KKT条件）；3) 采用有限元方法离散化状态方程，由于控制空间是有限维的，控制变量无需离散化；4) 推导离散解的先验误差界。

Result: 证明了带狄拉克测度源的谱分数阶拉普拉斯PDE最优控制问题的最优解存在且唯一，获得了一阶最优性条件；建立了有限元离散化的先验误差估计。

Conclusion: 该研究为分数阶拉普拉斯方程中以奇异源振幅为控制变量的最优控制问题提供了完整的理论分析（适定性和最优性条件）与数值分析（有限元离散和误差界），为后续算法实现和更广泛的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study a PDE-constrained optimization problem for an elliptic equation with the spectral fractional Laplacian and a linear combination of Dirac measures as the forcing term; the controls are the amplitudes of these singular sources. We prove existence and uniqueness of an optimal solution and derive first-order optimality conditions. We then propose a discretization based on finite elements. Since the set of admissible controls is finite dimensional, the control variable itself does not require discretization. We conclude by deriving a priori error bounds

</details>


### [134] [Anchored Scenario Coverage for Failure-Aware First-Hit Batch Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27873)
*Chuhan Yang,Chenxi Wang,Linhan Wu,Yuyang Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ARC-SC（锚定风险约束情景覆盖）批量采集方法，用于失败频繁的闭环逆向设计，通过保留强边际候选作为锚点并用风险约束情景覆盖分配其余批次位置，在超导和材料性质基准上实现了统计显著的首次命中改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在失败频繁的闭环逆向设计中，早期发现至少一个有效满足目标需求的设计是核心目标。自然的批量基线是按乘积形式边际有效命中分数对候选排序，但独立选择最高分候选会在预测不确定性下产生冗余推荐，浪费实验预算。

Method: ARC-SC（Anchored Risk-Constrained Scenario Coverage）方法将强边际候选保留为锚点，并通过最大化预测目标情景上的互补覆盖度、同时受风险支持约束来分配其余批次位置。

Result: 在冻结预言机的闭环仿真中，基于超导和JARVIS材料性质基准，ARC-SC在首次命中发现上取得了统计支持的改进，并在更具挑战性的设计空间中保持了具有方向性有利的首次命中表现。

Conclusion: ARC-SC作为一种POF锚定的、情景感知的批量策略，能够在结构化实验失败下改进早期有效目标发现。

Abstract: Early discovery of at least one valid design satisfying a target requirement is a central objective in failure-prone closed-loop inverse design. A natural batch baseline ranks candidates by a product-form marginal valid-hit score, but selecting the highest-ranked candidates independently can produce redundant recommendations under predictive uncertainty and waste the experiment budget. We introduce ARC-SC(Anchored Risk-Constrained Scenario Coverage), a batch acquisition method that preserves strong marginal candidates as anchors and allocates the remaining batch positions by maximizing complementary coverage over predictive target scenarios under a risk-support constraint. In frozen-oracle closed-loop simulations on superconductivity and JARVIS materials-property benchmarks, ARC-SC yields a statistically supported improvement in first-hit discovery and remains competitive with directionally favorable first-hit performance on more challenging design space. These results establish ARC-SC as a POF-anchored, scenario-aware batch strategy for improving early valid-target discovery under structured experimental failure.

</details>


### [135] [On Solving Continuous-Discrete Projection Filters via Sum-of-Squares Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28002)
*Muhammad F. Emzir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续-离散投影滤波器在预测阶段因积分误差导致自然参数越界而产生的数值不稳定性问题，引入和平方（SOS）松弛来约束自然参数的演化始终保持在可行域内。通过使用log-Cholesky映射参数化底层SOS矩阵，推导出一种投影常微分方程（ODE），该方程固有地保持必要的正定性约束，无需此前半无限规划方法所需的昂贵在线优化检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续-离散投影滤波器是近似非线性状态估计问题求解的严格框架，但其预测阶段存在数值不稳定问题——当积分误差迫使自然参数超出其可行域时，会导致计算失败。现有基于半无限规划的约束方法需要昂贵的在线优化检查，计算开销大。

Method: 引入和平方（SOS）松弛来约束自然参数演化始终处于可行域内；利用log-Cholesky映射对底层SOS矩阵进行参数化；由此推导出能固有保持正定性约束的投影常微分方程（ODE），避免了在线优化检查。

Result: 提供了该保正性传播方案的理论推导，并给出了高斯情形下显式的SOS松弛演化方程。

Conclusion: 所提出的SOS松弛方法结合log-Cholesky参数化能够在不依赖昂贵在线优化检查的前提下，从根本上保证投影滤波器预测阶段中正定性约束的满足，从而提升数值稳定性。

Abstract: The continuous-discrete projection filter offers a rigorous framework to approximate the solution of the nonlinear state estimation problems. However, it suffers from numerical instability during the prediction phase when integration errors force the natural parameters outside their admissible domain. To address this issue, we introduce the sum-of-squares (SOS) relaxation to constrain the evolution of the natural parameters within the admissible domain. By parameterizing the underlying SOS matrix using the log-Cholesky map, we derive a projected ordinary differential equation (ODE) that inherently preserves the necessary positivity constraints without requiring the computationally expensive online optimization checks associated with previous semi-infinite programming approach. We provide a theoretical derivation of this positivity-preserving propagation scheme and present the explicit SOS-relaxed evolution equations for the Gaussian case.

</details>


### [136] [The AHI family of sum of squares polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28015)
*Shaon Naskar,Kanchan Rajwar,Sujeet Kumar Singh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于算术-调和不等式构造的新的非负多项式族（AHI多项式），证明了其非负锥与SOS锥重合，并研究了其凸性、与其他非负性证书（SONC、SDSOS）的包含关系。通过乘法封闭性构建了更大的锥Π_AHI，其成员资格存在无需求解半定规划的闭式证书。数值实验表明，利用AHI稀疏性可使计算速度提升300倍以上，并能处理标准方法无法应对的高次（最高40次）多项式优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的SOS方法虽然能提供非负性的充分条件，但求解半定规划的计算代价高昂，尤其在高维和高次多项式优化问题中面临严重的计算瓶颈。本文旨在构造一类既保留SOS等价性（即非负性可判定）又具备可高效验证结构的特殊多项式族，从而在不损失理论保证的前提下大幅提升多项式优化的计算效率。

Method: 通过算术-调和不等式构造AHI多项式族，给出其非负性的显式代数条件；利用代数几何和多项式优化理论证明其非负锥与SOS锥重合，并分析其凸性及单项式子结构的SOS凸性。将该族在乘法下封闭生成锥Π_AHI，推导其闭式成员资格证书（避免SDP求解）。最后设计基于AHI稀疏性的因子化层级算法，将大规模问题分解为独立小规模子问题。

Result: 证明了AHI多项式的非负锥与SOS锥重合，且每一个AHI多项式同时属于SOS和SONC的交叉锥，且包含关系严格。Π_AHI锥在SOS内但位于SONC锥和SDSOS锥之外，其成员资格可闭式验证。数值实验显示，利用AHI稀疏性比稠密SOS松弛快300倍以上，能够求解最高40次的多项式优化问题，且提出的因子化层级能分解出通用稀疏性技术无法检测的独立小规模子问题。

Conclusion: AHI多项式族为一类兼具理论优雅性与实际计算优势的非负多项式结构，其非负性判定等价于SOS但避免了对半定规划的依赖，为大规模高次多项式优化提供了可行路径。该工作拓展了非负多项式证书的谱系，并为稀疏多项式优化的算法设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We introduce a new family of non-negative polynomials, constructed via the arithmetic harmonic inequality, called AHI polynomials. We derive explicit algebraic conditions for this family and prove that, for AHI polynomials, the cone of non-negative polynomials coincides with the cone of sum of squares (SOS) polynomials. We then study their convexity, showing that although AHI polynomials are generally nonconvex, certain monomial substructures are SOS convex. We further locate the family precisely among the standard nonnegativities certificates; every AHI polynomial is simultaneously SOS and a sum of non negative circuit polynomials (SONC), and the containment in the intersection of these two cones is strict. By closing this family under multiplication, we obtain a cone Pi AHI that is, by construction, still SOS, yet we prove that it lies outside both the SONC cone and the smaller SDSOS cone. Moreover, membership in this cone admits a closed form certificate that does not require solving any semidefinite programs. Finally, we demonstrate the usefulness of these structures in optimization, numerical experiments indicate that exploiting AHI sparsity yields a computation time over 300 times faster than dense SOS relaxations and enables solving high degree polynomial optimization problems (up to degree 40) that standard methods cannot handle due to computational limits, and a factorized hierarchy for Pi AHI decomposes products into independent small subproblems that generic sparsity techniques do not detect.

</details>


### [137] [A Refined Parameter Condition in the Lyapunov Analysis of IGAHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28088)
*Samir Adly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过保留Lyapunov分析中先前被下界替换的两个系数，精化了原始IGAHD（带Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性梯度算法）的收敛性条件，给出了更宽松的充分条件0<β<2/(L√s)，使得对于固定的β>0和L>0，充分小的步长s也能被该条件覆盖，从而扩展了参数有效区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始IGAHD论文中Lyapunov分析得到的充分条件0≤β<2√s对于固定β>0排除了足够小的步长s>0的情况，这是一个明显的局限性。作者希望通过精化Lyapunov分析中的估计来放宽这一条件。

Method: （1）在原始Lyapunov分析中，作者保留了两个先前被替换为下界的系数，从而得到更精确的估计；（2）基于这一精化，推导出新的充分条件0<βL√s<1+√(1+sL(1-sL))，其中最简形式为0<β<2/(L√s)；（3）对于有限维凸二次函数，采用独立的谱分析方法，分析极限模态矩阵的Schur稳定性，给出参数区域的充要条件以及目标残差和梯度的几何衰减。

Result: 新的充分条件使得固定β>0和L>0时，充分小的步长s>0也能被覆盖，弥补了原始条件的缺陷。对于凸二次函数，谱分析给出了更大的参数区域，并获得了模态矩阵Schur稳定性的充要条件，以及目标残差和梯度的几何衰减。在此新的精化充分条件下，原论文的结论仍然成立。

Conclusion: 新的精化条件放宽了IGAHD算法的收敛性充分条件，扩展了参数可行域。对于非二次目标函数，是否可以通过选取不同的Lyapunov函数来恢复二次情形中谱分析得到的扩展区域，这是一个值得进一步探索的开放问题。

Abstract: In a recent paper, Attouch, Chbani, Fadili and Riahi introduced the inertial gradient algorithm with Hessian-driven daming, called (IGAHD). Under the condition $0\leqβ<2\sqrt{s}$, where $β$ is the Hessian driven damping parameter and $s>0$ is the gradient step size, their Lyapunov analysis shows an accelerated estimate of the objective function as well as a weighted summability of the gradient for the case $β>0$. For any fixed value of $β>0$, this condition excluded sufficiently small step sizes $s>0$. The present note refines one of the estimates in this Lyapunov analysis by retaining two coefficients that were previously replaced by lower bounds. This leads to the following less restrictive and sufficient condition: $$0<βL\sqrt{s}<1+\sqrt{1+sL(1-sL)},$$ where $L>0$ is the constant Lipschitz of the gradient objective function and $0<s\leq1/L$. In particular, the simplest condition $0<β<\frac{2}{L\sqrt{s}}$ is sufficient. For fixed values of $β>0$ and $L>0$, this new condition is satisfied for sufficiently small $s>0$. Consequently, under this new refined sufficient condition, the conclusions in the original paper stay valid. For convex quadratic functions in finite dimensions, a separate spectral analysis leads to a larger region parameters. It gives a necessary and sufficient condition for Schur stability of the limit modal matrices, as well as the geometrical decay of the objective residuals and the gradients. This modal analysis raises the question of whether a different Lyapunov function would allow us to recover part (or all) of this extended spectral domain for non-quadratic objective functions.

</details>


### [138] [First-order approximations of multifunctions defined implicitly by "split" feasibility problems with applications to optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28319)
*Amos Uderzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类与参数化"分裂"可行性问题相关的集值映射（解映射）的一阶微分性质，给出了其图（切）导数的内逼近与外逼近，并探讨了在"分裂"可行性约束数学规划最优性条件中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "分裂"可行性问题是凸可行性问题的一类特殊结构，在工程和系统生物学等多个领域具有成熟应用。理解与之相关的解映射的微分性质，对于进一步进行灵敏度分析和导出最优性条件具有重要意义。

Method: 利用集值分析（set-valued analysis）中的图导数（contingent/graphical derivative）工具，对由参数化"分裂"可行性问题定义的解映射进行一阶微分分析，通过问题数据的导数来刻画解映射导数（切向导数）的内逼近和外逼近。

Result: 获得了解映射图（切）导数的内逼近与外逼近结果，这些逼近以问题数据的导数形式表达。

Conclusion: 所得微分逼近结果可作为推导具有"分裂"可行性约束的数学规划最优性条件的理论基础，为该类规划问题提供新的研究视角。

Abstract: In the present paper, a study is made of first-order differential properties of certain multifunctions, which are defined as a solution map associated with a family of parameterized ``split" feasibility problems. The latter are a class of convex feasibility problems, whose specific structure makes them suitable for well-established applications in several areas of engineering and systems biology. As a result, inner and outer approximations of the graphical (contingent) derivative of the solution map are provided, which are expressed in terms of derivatives of the problem data. As an application, a perspective is also discussed for employing some of these achievements in the formulation of optimality conditions for mathematical programs with ``split" feasibility constraints.

</details>


### [139] [On Nonsmooth and Relatively Weakly Convex Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28530)
*Chen Jiang,Jiawen Bi,Shuzhong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个分量均为非光滑非凸的复合优化问题，提出了确定性Bregman近端次梯度方法（在次梯度上界条件下收敛，放宽了对正则项凸性的要求），并进一步扩展到随机情形，提出基于模型的最小化方法，在相对Lipschitz条件下建立O(ε^-4)的收敛速率，同时将框架推广到距离生成函数及其梯度仅能通过随机oracle访问的设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合优化在现代机器学习和信号处理中扮演核心角色，因为它能平衡数据保真度与结构性质。现有方法通常要求正则项为凸函数，这限制了其应用范围。本文旨在放宽这一要求，处理两个分量均为非光滑且非凸的广义复合优化问题，并适应随机环境以及oracle不可精确获得的实际情况。

Method: 首先提出确定性Bregman近端次梯度方法，在次梯度上界（subgradient upper-bound）条件下建立收敛性，放宽正则项凸性要求；其次发展基于模型的最小化（model-based minimization）方法处理随机情形，在相对Lipschitz（relative Lipschitz）条件下获得O(ε^-4)的收敛速率；最后进一步扩展框架，使得距离生成函数及其梯度通过随机oracle获得时仍保持收敛保证。

Result: 确定了Bregman近端次梯度方法在次梯度上界条件下的收敛性；建立了随机设置下基于模型的最小化方法在相对Lipschitz条件下的O(ε^-4)收敛速率；成功将收敛性保证扩展到随机oracle可访问距离生成函数及其梯度的情形。

Conclusion: 本文为双分量均非光滑非凸的复合优化问题提供了统一的确定性及随机求解框架，通过放宽凸性假设和适应随机oracle环境，显著拓宽了Bregman类算法的适用范围，为更广泛的机器学习与信号处理应用提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Composite optimization plays a central role in modern machine learning and signal processing, as it offers a natural balance between data fidelity and structural properties. In this paper, we study composite optimization in the setting where both components are nonsmooth and nonconvex. We start with a deterministic Bregman proximal subgradient method that converges under subgradient upper-bound conditions. This approach relaxes the standard requirement on the convexity of the regularization term, thus accommodating a broader range of applications. To extend this to the stochastic regime, we develop a model-based minimization method under a relative Lipschitz condition and establish a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-4})$. We also extend the framework with convergence guarantees to the setting where the distance generating function and its gradient are accessible only through a stochastic oracle.

</details>


### [140] [Some quartic control results for scalar-input systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28582)
*Karine Beauchard,Frédéric Marbach*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量输入系统小时间局部可控性中四次项的作用，证明了利用更多"好"四次李括号的充分条件，同时识别出一族基于第二类坐标正定泛函的四次可控性障碍，并表明两者互补性部分回答了Kawski 1987年关于构造区分好/坏四次李括号的Hall基的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量输入系统中四次李括号在小时间局部可控性中的作用，并回答Kawski 1987年提出的关于Hall基构造的开放问题。

Method: 本文采用三类方法：(1) 证明一个新的可控性充分条件，同时利用比之前结果更多的"好"四次李括号；(2) 基于第二类坐标为控制的正定泛函的李括号，识别一族四次可控性障碍；(3) 通过充分条件与障碍条件的互补性，回应Kawski关于构造分离好/坏四次李括号的Hall基的开放问题，并通过实例和评注说明这些概念的复杂性。

Result: 得到了一个更强的四次李括号可控性充分条件，发现了基于第二类坐标正定泛函的四次障碍条件族，并证明了充分条件与障碍条件的互补性可部分回答Kawski 1987年的开放问题。

Conclusion: 四次李括号在标量输入系统的局部可控性中起着关键的双重角色——既可作为可控性的充分条件来源，也可构成可控性障碍；这些结果的互补性为Kawski开放问题提供了部分解答，但其中仍存在众多微妙之处，体现在所举的实例和评注中。

Abstract: We investigate the role of quartic terms in the small-time local controllability of scalar-input systems. First, we prove a new sufficient condition for controllability, which exploits simultaneously more good quartic Lie brackets than previous results. Second, we identify a family of quartic obstructions to controllability, relying on Lie brackets whose coordinates of the second kind are positive-definite functionals of the control. Third, we show that the complementarity of these results can be seen as an answer to Kawski's 1987 open problem concerning the construction of a Hall basis which somehow separates good and bad quartic brackets. We give examples and remarks illustrating some of the intricacies of these notions.

</details>
