### [141] [High-Order CFD Modeling of Rotating Actuator Line Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27943)
*Abdullah Al Imran,Meilin Yu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种将高阶FR/CPR（通量重构/重建修正程序）格式与旋转致动线模型（ALM）相结合的计算框架，用于模拟旋转能量系统。通过时间相关源项在固定笛卡尔网格上施加叶片旋转与非定常气动力，无需显式叶片几何。采用准定常翼型数据计算气动载荷，并用高斯平滑分布到流场以维持数值稳定性。该框架在低叶尖速比下的两叶片垂直轴风力机（VAWT）上进行了测试，分析了相位平均叶片运动、整体载荷随方位角的变化、瞬时与平均流场、尾流结构与非定常涡脱落。通过与实验测量和叶片分辨大涡模拟（LES）对比归一化平均尾流速度剖面进行验证，结果表明该高阶虚拟体框架能够在强非定常条件下可靠捕捉主要叶片载荷趋势、流动组织和尾流特征，同时保持几何简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶CFD方法结合致动器叶片表示是模拟旋转能量系统的有吸引力方法，但现有的高阶框架与旋转致动线模型的耦合尚需发展。本文旨在开发一种无需显式叶片几何即可模拟完全旋转风轮机的高阶计算框架，兼顾计算效率、数值稳定性与物理保真度。

Method: 将FR/CPR高阶格式与旋转致动线模型（ALM）耦合；通过时间相关源项在固定笛卡尔网格上实现叶片旋转和非定常气动力；气动载荷基于准定常翼型数据计算，并经高斯平滑分布到流场中以维持高阶格式的数值稳定性。框架在低TSR下的两叶片VAWT上进行测试。

Result: 分析了相位平均叶片运动与整体载荷随方位角的变化规律；检查了瞬时和平均流场以描述尾流结构与非定常涡脱落；通过归一化平均尾流速度剖面与实验测量及叶片分辨LES文献数据对比完成验证。结果显示该高阶虚拟体框架在强非定常条件下能够可靠捕捉主要叶片载荷趋势、流动组织和尾流特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的高阶虚拟体框架在无需显式叶片几何的前提下，能够在强非定常流动条件下准确捕捉关键叶片载荷、流场组织和尾流特征，为旋转能量系统的高保真、高效模拟提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: High-order computational fluid dynamics (CFD) methods combined with actuator-based blade representations provide an attractive approach for simulating rotating energy systems. In this work, we develop a high-order computational framework that couples the flux reconstruction/correction procedure via reconstruction (FR/CPR) formulation with a rotating actuator line model (ALM). Blade rotation and unsteady aerodynamic forces are implemented through time-dependent source terms applied on a fixed cartesian grid. This approach allows simulations of a fully rotating turbine without using explicit blade geometry. Aerodynamic loads are computed using quasi-steady airfoil data and distributed into the flow field with Gaussian smoothing to maintain numerical stability in the high-order scheme. The framework is tested using a two-bladed vertical-axis wind turbine (VAWT) operating at a low tip speed ratio (TSR). The phase-averaged blade motion and overall load are analyzed to study variations with azimuthal angle. In addition, instantaneous and mean flow fields are examined to describe wake structure and unsteady vortex shedding. Normalized mean wake velocity profiles are used for validation through comparison with experimental measurements and blade-resolved large-eddy simulations (LES) reported in the literature. The results show that the proposed high-order virtual-body framework can reliably capture major blade-loading trends, flow organization, and wake features under strongly unsteady conditions, while maintaining geometric simplicity.

</details>


### [142] [Validation of quasi-two-dimensional model of convection in a transverse magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28023)
*Alexander Gelfgat*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文检验了准二维（Q2D）模型在横向磁场作用下侧向加热矩形腔体自然对流问题中的适用性。将Q2D模型结果与完全三维计算进行对比，涵盖方形截面腔体和长高比为8的水平拉长腔体，宽度比从1到10变化。研究表明，在特定条件（跨度方向边界完美热/电绝缘且关于跨度方向中面对称）下，Q2D模型能定量准确地预测三维结果；但对完美导电壁面腔体，Q2D模型（含修正版）均无法与三维渐近行为吻合。模型还可正确预测稳态-振荡转捩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向磁场作用下侧向加热矩形腔体的磁热对流问题在工程与物理中具有重要意义，全三维数值模拟计算成本高昂。准二维（Q2D）模型作为一种降维近似方法，其适用边界条件尚不明确，需要系统检验其在何种条件下能可靠替代三维计算。

Method: 将Q2D模型计算结果与完全三维数值模拟进行系统对比。测试两类几何：方形截面（宽高比1:1）和长高比为8的水平拉长腔体，宽度比从1变化到10。分别考察跨度方向边界为理想绝缘（热与电）和完美导电两种情况，并检验Q2D模型对稳态-振荡转捩的预测能力。

Result: 在满足跨度方向边界完美热/电绝缘及关于跨度方向中面对称的条件下，Q2D模型对方形截面和水平拉长腔体均能给出定量正确的三维结果，且与宽度比无关。对完美导电壁面腔体，三维结果的宽度比渐近行为与修正Q2D模型预测不一致。在适用范围内，Q2D模型可正确预测三维流动的稳态-振荡转捩。

Conclusion: Q2D模型在明确的物理条件下（跨度方向边界热/电绝缘且具备反射对称性）是有效的降维工具，但无法直接推广到完美导电边界情形，未来需要发展针对导电跨度方向边界的扩展Q2D模型。

Abstract: In this study we examine applicability of the quasi-two-dimensional (Q2D) model of natural convection in a laterally heated rectangular box subject to a transverse magnetic field. For this purpose, results obtained using the Q2D model are compared with fully three-dimensional calculations for rectangular boxes with a square cross-section and horizontally elongated ones with the length to height ratio 8. The width to height ratio is varied from 1 to 10. It is argued that the Q2D model is valid under certain conditions, which include perfect thermal and electrical insulation of the spanwise boundaries and reflection symmetry with respect to the spanwise midplane. It is shown that under these conditions, the Q2D model yields quantitatively correct results for boxes with square cross-section, as well as for horizontally elongated cavities, independently on the width to height ratio. Three-dimensional results obtained for boxes with perfectly conducting walls exhibit asymptotics at gradually increasing width ratio, which, however, does not agree with the predictions of an amended Q2D model. It is shown also that when is applicable, the Q2D model can correctly predict steady-oscillatory transition of three-dimensional flows. It is proposed to seek for extensions of the Q2D model for cavities with perfectly conducting spanwise boundaries.

</details>


### [143] [How Surface Viscoelasticity Eliminates Satellite Drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28071)
*Xiaocong Yang,Qingfei Fu,Lijun Yang,Bingqiang Ji*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文实验发现，在液丝断裂过程中，少量牛血清白蛋白引入的表面粘弹性可通过调控断点附近的时空拓扑结构，完全消除卫星液滴，表面剪切粘度在薄化后期平衡毛细力并将颈缩区域稳定锚定在两个初级液珠之间，从而从根本上阻止卫星液滴的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星液滴在液丝断裂过程中广泛形成，是众多工业技术中的有害副产物，但完全抑制卫星液滴一直是一个长期挑战，因此需要寻找有效的消除策略。

Method: 通过实验手段，在液丝中加入少量牛血清白蛋白以引入表面粘弹性，系统研究表面流变学对液丝断裂及卫星液滴形成的影响，并基于表面剪切粘度主导的薄化行为提出标度律。

Result: 表面剪切粘度能够完全消除卫星液滴，其机制是在薄化后期平衡毛细力，将颈缩区域稳定锚定在两个初级液珠中点，从而从机制上阻断卫星液滴的形成；同时提出了描述该自相似薄化行为的标度律。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了具有表面流变学的液丝的薄化动力学机制，为在实际工业应用中消除卫星液滴提供了具有前景的策略。

Abstract: Satellite drops form widely during the breakup of liquid threads and constitute deleterious byproducts across a broad range of industrial technologies, yet their complete suppression has remained a longstanding challenge. Here, we experimentally report that surface viscoelasticity introduced by a small amount of bovine serum albumin can fully eliminate satellite drops, via modulating the localized spatiotemporal topology near pinch-off singularity. During the late thinning stage, surface shear viscosity counterbalances the capillary and stably anchors the thinning neck at the midpoint between the two primary beads, fundamentally precluding satellite droplet formation. We further propose a scaling law to describe the unique self-similar thinning behaviors dictated by surface viscosity. These results shed light on the thinning dynamics of liquid threads with surface rheology and offer a promising strategy to eliminate satellite drops in practical applications.

</details>


### [144] [A high-order polynomial-corrected shifted boundary method for simulating fully nonlinear water waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28123)
*Jens Visbech,Allan P. Engsig-Karup,Harry B. Bingham,Mario Ricchiuto*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的非拟合（unfitted）计算框架，用于模拟基于完全非线性势流的水波。该方法采用高阶多项式修正的移位边界近似（shifted-boundary approximation），在简单的笛卡尔背景网格上无需重新网格化即可模拟由水深变化影响的弯曲、高度时变的自由表面运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统边界拟合网格方法在处理高度时变、弯曲的自由水面时需要频繁重新网格化，计算成本高昂且几何近似繁琐。作者旨在开发一种无需重新网格化、具备高阶收敛特性且能长期模拟高度非线性波的数值方案，以提升海洋波浪模拟及与海上结构物相互作用的计算效率和几何灵活性。

Method: 该方法结合了多项关键技术：（1）在非拟合谱单元上采用高阶多项式修正的移位边界近似，处理弯曲且时变的自由表面；（2）使用保持多项式特性的梯度恢复技术（polynomial-preserving gradient recovery）精确捕获垂直自由表面速度；（3）采用任意阶有限差分近似自由表面，并引入超粘性（hyperviscosity）保证数值稳定性。

Result: 在周期域和有限域中进行了验证与确认测试。重点验证了收敛性研究、高阶近似的必要性、最优梯度恢复的重要性、高度非线性波的长期模拟，以及非线性波与倾斜/弯曲水深变化和垂直壁面的相互作用，涵盖高度非线性流函数波和高振幅孤立子等案例。

Conclusion: 该计算模型提供了一个综合性的"一体化"框架，用于模拟海洋波浪及其与海上结构物的相互作用。其显著优势在于几何灵活性——自由表面等边界可在无需重新网格化的情况下随时间移动和变形，同时保持高阶收敛精度。

Abstract: We present a novel unfitted computational framework for simulating fully nonlinear potential flow-based water waves. Focusing on wave propagation, we describe the core methodology, which involves a high-order polynomial-corrected shifted-boundary approximation on unfitted spectral elements. This approach allows for the simulation of a curved, highly time-dependent (moving and deforming) free surface affected by bathymetric changes. All that on a simple Cartesian background mesh without re-meshing or further approximations of non-affine geometric features. In addition, we highlight the importance of proper gradient recovery using a polynomial-preserving technique to accurately capture the vertical free-surface velocity. Ultimately, the goal is to develop a high-order convergent numerical scheme capable of simulating highly nonlinear waves over long periods. This is achieved through an arbitrary-order finite-difference approximation of the free surface combined with added hyperviscosity for numerical stability. We present verification and validation test cases for wave propagation in both periodic and finite domains. Emphasis is placed on convergence studies, the justification for using high-order approximations, the importance of optimal gradient recovery, long-time simulation of highly nonlinear waves, and nonlinear wave interactions with both affine and curved bathymetry changes, as well as with vertical walls, for highly nonlinear stream function waves and high-amplitude solitons. The novel computational model offers a comprehensive, all-in-one framework for simulating ocean waves and their interactions with offshore structures. It provides significant geometric flexibility, enabling boundaries such as the free surface to move and deform over time without the need to re-mesh a boundary-fitted mesh.

</details>


### [145] [Inclination-Induced Crossover in the Velocity Scaling of Lubrication-Mediated Droplet Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28203)
*Haruka Hitomi,Ko Okumura*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究了浸没在黏性油中的水滴沿倾斜表面蠕滑的运动行为，发现速度-半径幂律指数随倾斜角从约3/2连续过渡到约9/4，揭示了润滑介导液滴运动的倾斜诱导尺度律交叉。尽管宏观液滴形状偏离煎饼形几何，但速度数据仍趋近煎饼尺度律，表明主导的润滑耗散机制发生了与整体形貌不直接对应的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解液体-液体系统中润滑介导液滴运动的物理机制，特别是倾斜角作为控制参数对液滴速度-尺寸尺度律的影响，以及速度尺度律交叉与宏观形态演变之间的关联与差异。

Method: 实验测量浸没于黏性油中的水滴在倾斜表面滑动的速度、宽度和高度，覆盖广泛的液滴尺寸和倾斜角范围；结合基于Landau-Levich-Derjaguin薄膜形成和动态弯月面黏性耗散的尺度分析，解释观测到的幂律指数变化。

Result: 液滴速度随半径按幂律增大，指数在小倾斜角时接近3/2（准球形尺度律），在大倾斜角时接近9/4（煎饼尺度律），呈现连续交叉。速度尺度律的演变比宏观液滴形状的演变更为显著——即使液滴全局尺寸远离煎饼极限几何，速度数据仍趋近煎饼尺度律。

Conclusion: 倾斜角是控制润滑介导液滴运动的关键参数；观测结果揭示了液滴下方主导润滑耗散机制存在交叉，而这种交叉并不直接反映在全局液滴形态上，突出了液-液系统中全局形状演变与局部耗散动力学之间的区别。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate the creeping motion of water droplets sliding along an inclined surface immersed in a viscous oil. The droplet velocity, width, and height are measured over a broad range of droplet sizes and inclination angles. The velocity increases with droplet radius according to a power law, but the corresponding exponent depends strongly on the inclination angle. At small inclinations, the exponent is close to 3/2, whereas at larger inclinations it progressively approaches 9/4. The results therefore reveal a continuous inclination-induced crossover in the velocity-size scaling of lubrication-mediated droplet motion. A scaling analysis based on Landau-Levich-Derjaguin film formation and viscous dissipation in the dynamic meniscus reproduces these limiting behaviors, which correspond to the quasi-spherical and pancake scaling regimes, respectively.
  Remarkably, the evolution of the velocity scaling is substantially more pronounced than the corresponding evolution of the macroscopic droplet shape. While the velocity data progressively approach the pancake scaling law at large inclinations, the global droplet dimensions evolve in a more complex manner than expected from a simple pancake-shape picture. In particular, the approach to pancake-like velocity scaling occurs even when the measured droplet dimensions remain far from the corresponding pancake-limit geometry.
  These observations suggest a crossover in the dominant lubrication-dissipation mechanism beneath the droplet that is not directly reflected in the global droplet morphology. The results identify inclination angle as a key control parameter governing lubrication-mediated droplet motion and highlight the distinction between global shape evolution and local dissipation dynamics in liquid-liquid systems.

</details>


### [146] [Unsteady airfoil aerodynamics in attached flow: From unsteady thin airfoil theory to wind turbine application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28280)
*Ang Li,Mac Gaunaa,Georg Raimund Pirrung*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统推导了风涡轮求解器中附着流非定常气动力的完整工程实现方法，将经典非定常薄翼理论中的环量和非环量载荷以力系数形式统一公式化，适用于BEM、升力线和致动线三类方法，并用两个转子级算例验证了各贡献项的工程必要性与正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态失速模型和风力机气动/气弹代码依赖附着流非定常空气动力学，但现有文献缺乏一套完整、内部自洽的附着流贡献实现路线，导致工程代码中容易遗漏关键项（如升力方向投影、中弦垂向加速度项等），从而引入功率和推力误差。

Method: 从经典非定常薄翼理论出发，将环量载荷与非环量载荷以升力、阻力和力矩系数的形式推导并给出系数表达式；采用下洗速度而非迎角作为气动状态变量来表述涡脱落记忆（尾迹记忆）；澄清了关键建模选择的含义；针对BEM、升力线（LL）和致动线（AL）三类方法统一公式化。通过两个算例进行验证：锥形直叶片基准算例（跨方法对比并量化遗漏三项贡献带来的推力/功率误差），以及零来流工况的垂直轴风力机（VAWT）算例（验证理想薄翼极限下所有环量与非环量贡献完全抵消、总转矩为零）。

Result: 锥形直叶片算例表明，升力方向的投影对功率影响最大，而遗漏中弦垂向加速度项会破坏非环量法向力抵消关系并产生推力误差；零来流VAWT算例验证了理想薄翼极限下所有环量与非环量贡献的完全抵消，转子总转矩为零，证明了公式体系内部一致性。

Conclusion: 本文为风涡轮BEM、LL、AL求解器提供了完整且内部一致的附着流非定常气动力实现路线，明确了各贡献项（尤其是容易被遗漏的非环量项和力投影项）的工程意义，为工程代码提升气动与气弹预测精度提供了理论基础和验证基准。

Abstract: Attached-flow unsteady aerodynamics underpin the dynamic stall models used in wind turbine aerodynamic and aeroelastic codes, particularly over the outboard blade region that dominates power production and loading. Here, angle of attack and relative velocity vary while the flow remains predominantly attached. A complete engineering model combines airfoil polar lookup, shed-wake memory, non-circulatory loads, consistent force definitions, and separated-flow dynamics. Although the underlying theory is classical, existing descriptions do not provide a complete and internally consistent implementation route for these attached-flow contributions in wind turbine solvers. This work formulates the attached-flow contributions as lift, drag, and moment coefficients for blade-element momentum (BEM), lifting-line (LL), and actuator-line (AL) methods, for use with 2-D airfoil polars from measurements or CFD. Starting from classical unsteady thin-airfoil theory, circulatory and non-circulatory loads are derived in coefficient form and key modeling choices are clarified. Shed-wake memory is formulated using downwash velocity rather than angle of attack as the aerodynamic state variable. Rotor-level implementation is verified using two cases. A coned straight-blade case provides a cross-method benchmark and quantifies thrust and power errors caused by omitting three required contributions. Lift-direction projection has the largest influence on power, while omitting the mid-chord heaving-acceleration term removes the non-circulatory normal-force cancellation and produces a thrust error. A zero-onset-flow vertical-axis wind turbine (VAWT) case verifies that, in the ideal thin-airfoil limit, all circulatory and non-circulatory contributions cancel, yielding zero total rotor torque.

</details>


### [147] [LES of iron-powder combustion in a jet-in-hot-coflow burner - Insights on flame structure and ignition characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28294)
*Shyam Hemamalini,XiaoCheng Mi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用LES和拉格朗日点粒子方法数值模拟了用于测试铁粉点火行为的射流-热共流（JHC）燃烧器，考察了开口火焰和封闭火焰两种模式以及两种铁氧化速率模型（一阶模型和氧化层模型）和两种辐射传热模型（Stafan-Boltzmann近似和P1模型）对点火行为的影响。研究发现点火发生在射流圆周区域，共流破碎促进点火；两种氧化模型所得的最小共流温度与实验结果不符；封闭火焰下氧化程度更高，P1模型辐射热损失更大导致氧化程度略低；大颗粒更易点火失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微米级铁粉作为一种新兴储能技术正快速发展，为改进实际铁粉燃烧器的设计，需要充分理解其在真实工况下的点火行为。此前已有JHC燃烧器的实验研究，但缺乏相应的数值模拟来深入理解点火机理与关键参数的影响。

Method: 采用LES（大涡模拟）与拉格朗日点粒子方法对Hameete等人(2024)设计的JHC燃烧器进行数值建模。模拟包含两种模式（开口火焰和封闭火焰），对比两种铁氧化速率模型（一阶模型和氧化层模型），并分别采用简化的Stefan-Boltzmann近似模型和P1模型处理两相间的辐射传热。

Result: 火焰结构分析表明点火发生在射流圆周区域，共流破碎有助于点火。较高的共流温度使点火起始和氧化完成提前。氧化层模型最小点火共流温度为1125K（完全氧化需1250K），一阶模型为800K（完全氧化需900K），两者均与Hameete的实验结果不符。封闭火焰中氧化程度更高；P1模型辐射热损失更大，导致氧化程度略低。大颗粒更易点火失败，且在足够高的位置上，整体氧化程度可反映颗粒点火概率。

Conclusion: 该数值研究揭示了JHC燃烧器中铁粉点火的关键机理（射流圆周点火、共流破碎的作用），但定量预测与实验仍有偏差，说明现有氧化模型对点火条件的预测还需改进。研究同时表明氧化程度分析可作为点火概率的统计指标，为铁粉燃烧器的实际设计提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: Micron-sized iron powders are a rapidly advancing novel energy storage technology. In order to improve the design of real-world iron-powder combustors, adequate understanding of the ignition behavior in such settings is necessary. In this work, a jet-in-hot-coflow (JHC) burner designed by Hameete et al. (2024) to test ignition of iron particles in lab-scale turbulent flames is modeled numerically using LES and Lagrangian point-particles. The JHC burner is simulated in two different modes--an open flame and an enclosed flame--similar to the experimental reference. Two iron-oxidation-rate models--the first-order model and oxide-layer model--are used to examine the effect on capturing the ignition behavior. For the radiative heat transfer between the two phases, a simplified Stefan-Boltzmann approximation model and the P1 model are considered, similar to Ramaekers et al. (2025). Analysis of flame structure indicates ignition in the circumference of the jet, aided by the break-up of the coflow. At higher $T_\mathrm{coflow}$, ignition onset and oxidation completion is earlier prior to jet break-up. Minimum coflow temperature for particle ignition with the oxide layer model is $1125\mathrm{K}$ with complete oxidation at $1250\mathrm{K}$, and for the first-order model at $800\mathrm{K}$ and $900\mathrm{K}$, respectively. Both of these results do not match the experimental results of Hameete. Oxidation degree is predictably higher for enclosed flames. For the chosen particle distribution, the P1 model exhibits higher radiative heat loss and results in a slightly lower oxidation degree. Analysis on particle ensembles with partial oxidation shows that the overall oxidation degree at a sufficient height above the nozzle reflects particle ignition probability. Further analysis in regards to particle size shows ignition failure is more prevalent in larger particles.

</details>


### [148] [A physics-constrained machine-learning sub-grid-scale modeling approach for turbulent premixed flames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28525)
*Seung Won Suh,Jonathan F. MacArt,Luke N. Olson,Jonathan B. Freund*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理嵌入的训练框架，用于封闭湍流预混火焰的亚格子尺度动力学。该模型与解析流场方程耦合，并以端到端方式优化，使其预测的流场与可信数据匹配。模型在湍流中自由传播的预混火焰（单组分、单步不可逆化学反应）上表现鲁棒有效，并通过守恒性、标量有界性和等变性等约束构建，同时基于解析方程残差进行尺度缩放以聚焦闭合需求区域。长期仿真中，该模型优于无模型、动态闭合以及直接用残差数据训练的机器学习模型，有效修正了湍流耗散和火焰运动学，且预测驱动的训练优于精确残差数据驱动的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流预混燃烧中，亚格子尺度动力学对解析流场的影响至关重要，但传统闭合模型（如动态闭合）和直接拟合法存在鲁棒性差或泛化能力不足的问题。作者旨在构建一种物理嵌入的训练框架，将机器学习模型直接集成到耦合求解过程中进行端到端优化，从而获得长期仿真中更鲁棒有效的亚格子闭合模型。

Method: 采用物理嵌入的训练框架：将可训练的机器学习模型嵌入到解析流场方程组中，以端到端方式同时优化耦合方程组和嵌入模型，使预测流场匹配可信数据。训练目标中内置了守恒性、标量有界性和等变性等重要约束；模型的作用范围根据解析流场方程的残差进行自适应缩放，使模型影响集中在需要闭合的区域。对比实验包括无模型、动态闭合以及直接拟合残差数据的同一机器学习模型。

Result: 在长期仿真中，所提出的物理嵌入模型优于无模型、动态闭合以及直接基于残差数据训练的机器学习模型。该模型能够有效修正湍流耗散和火焰运动学（如火焰传播动力学）。此外，结果表明基于预测的训练方式比基于精确残差数据的训练方式能为模型提供更有效的信息。

Conclusion: 物理嵌入、端到端优化的训练框架能够生成比传统闭合方案和纯数据驱动方案更鲁棒有效的亚格子闭合模型。预测驱动的训练优于残差驱动的训练，说明将模型置于耦合物理方程中进行优化比直接拟合残差数据更能学习到符合物理规律的行为。

Abstract: A physics-embedded training framework is used to close the sub-grid-scale dynamics of turbulent premixed flames. The trained model augments the resolved flow equations and is trained to match its predicted flow field to trusted data. An end-to-end optimization of the coupled resolved equations and embedded model leads to a model that is robust and effective for a freely propagating premixed flame in turbulence, modeled by a single-species, single-step, and irreversible chemical reaction. Important constraints are built into the training formulation for conservation, scalar boundedness, and equivariance. The model is scaled based on the residual of the resolved flow equations to focus its influence where the closure is needed. It outperforms other cases considered --- no-model, dynamic closure, and the same machine learning model trained directly to fit the residual data --- for a long-time simulation, correcting the turbulence dissipation and flame kinematics. Finally, it is shown how the prediction-based training better informs the model than the precise residual data.

</details>


### [149] [Compaction in a deformable porous cylinder with elastic boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28537)
*Richard Mcnair,Kerstin Schirrmann,Anne Juel,Igor L. Chernyavsky*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了由柔性弹性膜包裹的长细圆柱形多孔介质中流体驱动的压实问题。通过小宽高比渐近约化，得到孔隙率的主导阶非线性扩散方程并数值求解。研究发现柔性壁面相较于刚性边界只在中间阶段产生压实平台，当施加压力与膜刚度与初始孔隙率之积相当时，流动会突破平台。当膜刚度小于多孔介质时，流量可能超过刚性介质预期。论文还给出了参数空间图，区分平台区与突破区、亚达西（sub-Darcy）与超达西（super-Darcy）流动区，并提供了忽略重力时的渐近解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软材料（如生物组织、水凝胶）的渗透对器官功能和实验室系统（色谱柱、生物反应器）至关重要，理解流体驱动下多孔介质与弹性膜耦合的压实行为及流动规律与弹性参数的关系具有重要意义。

Method: 采用达西流动的拉格朗日表述，耦合小应变线性弹性理论，考虑孔隙率依赖的渗透率与弹性模量；在小宽高比极限下进行渐近约化，得到孔隙率的主导阶非线性扩散方程，并用数值方法求解。

Result: 刚性边界产生压实平台，而柔性壁面最多出现中间平台，一旦施加压力达到膜刚度与初始孔隙率乘积的量级，流动将突破平台。膜刚度小于多孔介质时流量可超过刚性介质预测。参数空间图区分了平台/突破域、亚达西/超达西域，并划定了小应变理论适用域。忽略重力时给出解析解，捕捉平台偏离并得到有效渗透率与流量的紧凑表达式。

Conclusion: 柔性膜壁显著改变了多孔介质的压实-流动行为，不同于刚性边界的经典结果；流量突破平台的条件由膜刚度与孔隙率共同决定，所得参数空间图和渐近表达式为设计软多孔系统（如生物反应器）提供了定量指导。

Abstract: Perfusion of soft materials such as biological tissue or hydrogels is essential for the functioning of organ and laboratory systems such as chromatographic columns and bioreactors. Inspired by these applications, we model fluid-driven compaction in a long, thin cylindrical porous medium bounded by an impermeable elastic membrane and study how flow regimes relate to elastic parameters. Using a Lagrangian formulation of Darcy flow coupled to small-strain linear elasticity with porosity dependent permeability and elastic moduli, we perform an asymptotic reduction in the small aspect ratio limit and obtain a leading-order nonlinear diffusion equation for the porosity, which we solve numerically. Whereas rigid boundaries produce a compaction plateau, compliant walls exhibit, at most, an intermediate plateau beyond which the flow increases once the imposed pressure becomes comparable to the product of membrane stiffness and initial porosity. When the membrane is less stiff than the porous medium, flow rate can exceed that expected for a rigid medium. A parameter space map distinguishes regimes where plateau and breakthrough occur, where the steady flow rate is below (sub-Darcy) or above (super-Darcy) the undeformable-medium prediction, and delineates the small-strain domain in which the theory applies. An asymptotic solution for negligible gravity captures the departure from the plateau and yields compact expressions for effective permeability and flow rate.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Numerical analysis of data assimilation for slightly compressible flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27647)
*Aytekin Çıbık,Rui Fang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对可微压缩流中同时同化速度与压力数据的连续数据同化模型，对其及其有限元离散格式进行了数值分析，建立半离散和全离散（线性化后向欧拉）格式的稳定性与误差估计，证明无穷可预测时域、初始误差指数衰减，模型误差关于观测分辨率H为一阶、关于压力nudging参数μ₁为μ₁^{-1/2}阶，选取μ₁=O(H^{-2})获得最优收敛率，数值实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可微压缩流中，仅同化速度数据的nudging方法存在局限。已有模型通过同时同化速度和压力数据并将两者nudging进不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程，展示了连续时间误差估计和初步实验的有效性。为进一步从数值分析层面严格验证该方法的稳定性与收敛性，本文对其及有限元离散方案进行系统分析。

Method: 对同时同化速度和压力数据的连续数据同化模型进行数值分析：(1) 建立半离散有限元格式的稳定性与误差估计；(2) 建立全离散线性化后向欧拉格式的稳定性与误差估计；(3) 通过权衡观测分辨率H与压力nudging参数μ₁引入的误差项，选取μ₁=O(H^{-2})以平衡两项误差、获得最优收敛率。

Result: 分析表明该模型具有无穷可预测时域：初始误差随时间指数衰减；模型误差关于观测分辨率H为一阶（O(H)），关于压力nudging参数μ₁为O(μ₁^{-1/2})。通过平衡两项误差选取μ₁=O(H^{-2})可获得最优收敛率，数值实验证实了所有理论预测的收敛阶。

Conclusion: 联合速度和压力nudging的连续数据同化模型在半离散和全离散有限元框架下均具有良好的数值稳定性与收敛性，无穷可预测时域和最优收敛率的确立为该模型的实际应用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Continuous data assimilation improves flow predictions by continually nudging a model toward available observational data. For slightly compressible flow, a recent model addresses the limitations of velocity-only nudging by assimilating both velocity and pressure data and nudging both quantities into the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations [5]; continuous-in-time error estimates and preliminary experiments show that this joint nudging is effective and substantially reduces the model error relative to velocity-only nudging. Motivated by these results, we carry out the numerical analysis of the model and its finite element discretizations. We establish stability and error estimates for the semi-discrete scheme and for the fully discrete, linearized backward Euler scheme. The analysis shows an infinite predictability horizon: the effect of the initial error decays exponentially in time, and the model error is first order in the observation resolution H and of order $μ_1^{-1/2}$ in the pressure nudging parameter $μ_1$. Balancing these two error terms, we choose $μ_1=\mathcal{O}(H^{-2})$, which yields the optimal convergence rate. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted rates.

</details>


### [151] [The singular Zienkiewicz tetrahedron: Definition and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27741)
*Johannes Storn*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文将二维奇异Zienkiewicz元推广到三维，构建了基于有理形函数的四面体网格上的H²协调有限元，并发展了精确迭代积分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将二维奇异Zienkiewicz元扩展至三维，以在四面体网格上构造新的H²协调有限元，填补三维高阶协调有限元构造的空白。

Method: 通过有理形函数构造三维H²协调有限元，建立相容性分析，并开发针对该有理函数类的精确迭代积分程序。

Result: 得到精确积分公式，可对基函数、其导数及有限元组装中出现的乘积项进行精确积分。

Conclusion: 该三维奇异Zienkiewicz元为四面体网格提供了新的H²协调有限元构造，且配套的精确积分方法保证了实际计算中的可行性。

Abstract: We extend the two-dimensional singular Zienkiewicz element to three dimensions, leading to a novel $H^2$-conforming finite element on tetrahedral meshes based on rational shape functions. Besides the finite element construction and its conformity analysis, we develop an exact iterative integration procedure for the associated class of rational functions. The resulting formulae allow for the exact integration of the basis functions, their derivatives, and the products occurring in finite element assembly.

</details>


### [152] [High-order robust basis-update & Galerkin integrators for dynamical low-rank approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27749)
*Cory D. Hauck,Jonas Kusch,Steffen Schotthöfer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖显式时间积分方案的一般阶数BUG积分器，通过增广和并行两种变体实现高阶精度，并证明了其一般阶数误差界，在多个刚性/非刚性数值基准上显著优于先前版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态低秩近似在多个学科中被广泛使用，其低秩分解的演化会导致高度刚性的动力学。现有BUG积分器仅限二阶精度，而一般阶数的BUG积分器被设计为显式时间积分方法的投影，严重限制了其应用。

Method: 提出不依赖显式时间积分方案的一般阶数增广(augmented)和并行(parallel)BUG积分器；证明了一般阶数误差界；通过一系列刚性和非刚性数值基准测试验证性能。

Result: 所提出的增广和并行BUG积分器在刚性和非刚性数值基准测试中均显著优于之前的BUG版本，且能以更少的基函数数量实现高阶精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法克服了传统BUG积分器在阶数上受限的局限，实现了不依赖显式时间积分的一般高阶精度，同时保持结构的隐式时间积分特性，扩大了BUG积分器的适用范围。

Abstract: Dynamical low-rank approximation has become a widely used numerical method in diverse disciplines. Its main idea is to represent the matrix or tensor-valued solution to a time-dependent differential equation as a low-rank factorization. The evolution of the factorization leads to highly stiff dynamics and requires the derivation of novel time integration methods that are not prone to this stiffness. A promising family of integrators are basis-update \& Galerkin (BUG) integrators as they enable implicit time integration and structure--preservation. However, current BUG integrators are limited to second--order accuracy while general-order BUG integrators are designed as projections of explicit time integration methods, thus severely limiting their use.
  In this work, we propose general-order BUG integrators that do not rely on an explicit time integration scheme while requiring a smaller number of basis functions to achieve high-order accuracy. We prove a general order error bound for the proposed augmented and parallel BUG integrators and demonstrate their behaviour for a series of stiff and non-stiff numerical benchmarks in which they significantly outperform previous BUG versions.

</details>


### [153] [A Massively Parallel Hybridizable Discontinuous Galerkin Solver for Direct Numerical Simulation of Compressible Flows on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27863)
*Andrew Welter,Thea Collin,Ngoc Cuong Nguyen,Jaime Peraire*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于GPU加速高性能计算系统的、面向可压缩Navier-Stokes方程直接数值模拟（DNS）的大规模并行混合间断伽辽金（HDG）求解器。该求解器结合高阶HDG离散、鲁棒激波捕捉、对角隐式龙格-库塔（DIRK）时间积分以及由加性Schwarz预条件和缩减基近似加速的Newton-GMRES求解策略，并通过GPU感知MPI、Kokkos和CUDA/HIP实现异构平台上的可扩展计算。在亚声速Eppler 387翼型过渡流、超声速Taylor-Green涡和超声速边界层过渡三个基准问题上验证了求解器的能力，数值结果与实验测量和已发表DNS数据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩过渡流和湍流的DNS需要兼具高阶精度、鲁棒性和计算效率的数值方法，以解析宽范围的空间和时间尺度。传统方法在面对宽马赫数范围的复杂流场（包括层流-湍流过渡、强压缩效应、激波结构等）时面临精度、稳定性与计算成本之间的严峻权衡，亟需一种能够在异构高性能计算平台上高效并行的高阶求解器。

Method: 该求解器的核心方法包括：(1) 高阶HDG空间离散，兼具高精度与紧凑的单元局部性问题；(2) 鲁棒激波捕捉策略以处理强间断；(3) DIRK时间积分，具有良好的非线性稳定性；(4) Newton-GMRES求解策略，配合加性Schwarz预条件和缩减基近似加速收敛；(5) 基于GPU感知MPI、Kokkos和CUDA/HIP库的分布式实现，实现异构计算平台上的可扩展执行。

Result: 在三个覆盖宽马赫数范围的典型基准问题上进行了验证：亚声速Eppler 387翼型过渡流、超声速Taylor-Green涡和超声速边界层过渡。数值结果与实验测量及已发表的DNS数据在多个不同流动机制下均表现出良好的一致性，证明了求解器能够解析层流-湍流过渡、强压缩效应、激波相关流动结构以及完全三维湍流动力学。

Conclusion: 所提出的GPU加速HDG求解器成功展示了在异构高性能计算平台上进行大规模可压缩DNS的能力，在宽马赫数范围内均能获得与实验和文献DNS数据一致的高保真结果，为可压缩复杂流动的高分辨数值模拟提供了可靠且可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Direct numerical simulation (DNS) of compressible transitional and turbulent flows requires numerical methods that combine high-order accuracy, robustness, and computational efficiency to resolve a broad range of spatial and temporal scales. This paper presents a massively parallel hybridizable discontinuous Galerkin (HDG) solver for DNS of the compressible Navier-Stokes equations on GPU-accelerated high-performance computing systems. The proposed solver combines high-order HDG discretization with robust shock capturing, diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta (DIRK) time integration, and an efficient Newton-GMRES solution strategy accelerated by additive Schwarz preconditioning and reduced-basis approximation. A distributed implementation of these methods based on GPU-aware MPI, Kokkos, and CUDA/HIP libraries enables scalable execution on heterogeneous computing platforms. The solver is demonstrated on three canonical benchmark problems covering a wide range of Mach-number flow regimes: subsonic transitional flow over the Eppler 387 airfoil, the supersonic Taylor-Green vortex, and hypersonic boundary-layer transition. Numerical results are compared with available experimental measurements and published DNS data, showing good agreement across distinct flow regimes. The results demonstrate the ability of the proposed solver to resolve laminar-turbulent transition, strong compressibility effects, shock-associated flow structures, and fully three-dimensional turbulent dynamics.

</details>


### [154] [Optimal error estimates for the half-way bounce-back lattice Boltzmann method for the Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27926)
*Kai Koike*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对平坦通道中不可压Stokes方程的D2Q9 BGK格子玻尔兹曼方法（采用半步反弹边界规则）给出了最优收敛速率的严格数学证明：速度达到二阶收敛，压力达到一阶收敛（当网格间距h趋于零时）。这一结果优于以往严格收敛定理仅能给出的速度误差O(h^{1/2})上界，与形式分析和数值实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于格子玻尔兹曼方法（LBM）的严格收敛分析只能给出速度误差O(h^{1/2})的次优界，这与实践中观察到的二阶速度收敛和一阶压力收敛不符。为了弥合理论与数值实践之间的差距，需要对边界一致性误差进行更精细的处理，以获得最优收敛速率的严格证明。

Method: 本文将边界一致性误差分解为宏观（Stokes）分量和动理学（Knudsen层）分量。前者通过构造恰当的Stokes修正项吸收，后者通过离散Knudsen层修正项吸收。将这些修正项纳入先前严格分析中使用的预测函数，构建出一个具有足够高阶一致性误差的精细化预测函数；再结合已知的加权L^2稳定性估计，最终推导出最优收敛速率。

Result: 对不可压Stokes方程，D2Q9 BGK格子玻尔兹曼方法配合半步反弹边界规则，在平坦通道中速度收敛达到二阶精度，压力收敛达到一阶精度，从数学上严格证明了最优收敛速率。

Conclusion: 通过将边界一致性误差分解为宏观与动理学分量并分别构造修正项，成功突破了以往严格分析只能获得O(h^{1/2})次优界的瓶颈，给出了与数值实验和形式分析一致的最优收敛速率的严格数学证明。

Abstract: We give a mathematical proof of the optimal convergence rates for the D2Q9 BGK lattice Boltzmann method with the half-way bounce-back rule for the incompressible Stokes equations in a flat channel. The convergence rates are second-order for the velocity and first-order for the pressure as the lattice spacing $h$ tends to zero, in agreement with formal analyses and numerical experiments, whereas the available rigorous convergence theorems only yield an $O(h^{1/2})$ bound for the velocity error. A key step in the proof is a decomposition of the leading boundary consistency error into macroscopic and kinetic components. These components are absorbed by suitably constructed Stokes and discrete Knudsen layer correctors, respectively. Incorporating these correctors into the prediction function used in previous rigorous analyses, we obtain a refined prediction function with consistency errors of sufficiently high order. Combined with the known weighted $L^2$-stability estimate, this gives the optimal convergence rates.

</details>


### [155] [A Two-Level Preconditioner Based on Dominant Components for Time-Dependent Multiscale High-Contrast Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27958)
*Yating Wang,Yibao Li,Wing Tat Leung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对高对比度多尺度介质中的时间依赖问题，开发了一种基于多变尺度方法（特别是松弛非局部多连续介质方法，NLMC）的两级重叠预条件子。粗空间被划分为高渗透率分量和低渗透率背景分量。对于小时间步长，仅需高渗透率分量即可实现有效的全局粗校正；对于一般时间步长，需使用完整NLMC空间或用标准多尺度空间增强高渗透率分量。该方法降低了计算成本，同时保持了对系数对比度和细尺度分辨率的鲁棒性，并通过数值实验证明了其鲁棒性、效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间依赖问题在高对比度多尺度介质中的数值求解具有挑战性，特别是需要构造鲁棒且高效的预条件子以处理对比度相关的全局模态。现有的粗空间构造往往计算成本高或对标量对比度不鲁棒，因此需要一种既能降低计算成本又能保持鲁棒性的两级预条件子粗空间构造方法。

Method: 提出基于NLMC方法的两级重叠预条件子。NLMC空间被分解为高渗透率区域分量和低渗透率背景分量。理论分析表明完整NLMC空间可有效预条件非均质刚度算子，高渗透率分量可捕获对比度相关的全局模态。针对不同时间步长选择不同粗校正策略：小时间步长仅用高渗透率分量，一般时间步长使用完整NLMC空间或用标准多尺度空间增强高渗透率分量。多尺度基函数通过迭代求解松弛能量最小化公式构造。

Result: 数值实验证明了该方法的鲁棒性、效率和可扩展性。所提出的粗空间构造在降低计算成本的同时，保持了相对于系数对比度和细尺度分辨率的鲁棒性。对于合适的小时间步长，仅用高渗透率分量即可维持预条件性能；对于一般时间步长，通过使用完整NLMC空间或增强的粗空间维持性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为高对比度多尺度时间依赖问题提供了一种计算高效且鲁棒的两级预条件子，通过合理选择粗空间分量（高渗透率分量或完整NLMC空间）适应不同时间步长需求，兼顾了计算成本与求解性能。迭代求解松弛能量最小化公式进一步提升了基函数构造效率。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a two-level overlapping preconditioner for time-dependent problems in high-contrast multiscale media. We present a coarse-space construction based on multiscale methods, with emphasis on the relaxed nonlocal multicontinuum (NLMC) method. The NLMC space can be separated into components representing the high-permeability regions and the low-permeability background. We show that the complete NLMC space effectively preconditions the heterogeneous stiffness operator, whereas the high-permeability component alone captures the contrast-dependent global modes. For suitable small time-step sizes, the mass matrix controls the low-permeability contribution and only the high-permeability component in NLMC space is required for the global coarse correction in the two-level preconditioner. For general time-step sizes, performance can be maintained by using the full NLMC space or augmenting the high-permeability component with a standard multiscale space. The proposed coarse-space construction lowers the computational cost while preserving robustness with respect to coefficient contrast and fine-scale resolution. To further improve efficiency, the multiscale basis functions can be constructed by iteratively solving the relaxed energy-minimizing formulation. We demonstrate the robustness, efficiency and scalability of the proposed method through several numerical experiments.

</details>


### [156] [Quantile Randomized Kaczmarz for Streaming Linear Systems with Massart Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27968)
*Emeric Battaglia,Jian-Feng Cai,Junren Chen,Anna Ma,Deanna Needell,Tong Wu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究采用分位数随机Kaczmarz（QRK）方法求解含Massart噪声的流式线性系统。不同于以往工作中隐藏常数和模糊的容忍界，本文通过流式设置中样本的独立性，给出了可显式计算的最优批次大小 D=O(log T)，建立了可容忍约7%腐败水平的线性收敛性，并讨论了在盲目噪声下常数的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Cai等人已证明O(log T/log(1/β))的样本量对于QRK在T次迭代内线性收敛是充要条件，但可容忍的腐败水平β究竟能多大，以及所需子样本量D如何显式计算（不含隐藏常数），仍然是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 利用流式线性系统中各次迭代样本之间的独立性（不同于以往设置中迭代间样本的依赖），对QRK算法进行更精细的分析，从而导出关于可容忍腐败水平和所需子样本量的显式、可计算的界。每次更新采用阶最优的批次大小 D=O(log T)。

Result: 建立了腐败水平最高约7%时的线性收敛性，并给出了显式的批次大小计算公式；在盲目（oblivious）噪声条件下，相关常数可进一步改进。

Conclusion: 流式设置下的独立性假设使得QRK分析更为尖锐，能够显式量化可容忍的腐败水平与所需子样本量，克服了以往工作中隐藏常数的局限。

Abstract: Quantile randomized Kaczmarz (QRK) has proven to be an efficient solver for corrupted linear systems and has received much attention. It was recently shown by Cai et al. (SIAM J. Matrix Anal. Appl. 47(2):802-823, 2026) that using $O(\log T/\log(1/β))$ samples for computing the quantile is necessary and sufficient for QRK to converge linearly over $T$ iterations when solving linear systems with a $β$-fraction of arbitrary corruptions, as long as $β$ is small enough. However, it remains unclear how large the corruption level $β$ can be, and how to compute the required subsample size $D$ explicitly, without hidden constants. This paper studies streaming linear systems with Massart noise via QRK using an order-optimal batch size $D=O(\log T)$ in each update. The independence of samples from previous iterations in the streaming setting enables a sharper analysis, yielding explicit, computable bounds on both the tolerable corruption level and the required subsample size. In particular, we establish linear convergence for corruption levels of up to approximately 7%. We also discuss how the constants improve under oblivious noise.

</details>


### [157] [Transport-Matched Penalties for Diffusion Synthetic Acceleration of Polytopic Discontinuous Galerkin Discretisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28022)
*Ansar Calloo,Matthew Evans,Francois Madiot,Tristan Pryer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对多面体网格上高阶迎风间断伽辽金离散纵标输运，提出了一种输运匹配的修正内罚（MIP）扩散合成加速方法，证明了精确标量修正的正定性和自伴性，推导了源迭代收敛因子的精确表达式，并在光学厚极限下证明了该方法的严格加速性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散合成加速（DSA）的有效性取决于其扩散修正是否准确反映产生迭代误差的输运离散格式。现有方法在多面体网格上的高阶迎风DG离散中缺乏与离散格式精确匹配的扩散修正。

Method: 从离散输运扫描中推导出精确的标量修正，证明其响应算子为正且自伴；以此精确修正为参考，构造输运匹配的修正内罚（MIP）修正，其边界项直接源自均匀真空流入条件，并推导源迭代收敛因子的精确表达式。

Result: 在光学厚区域，MIP形式的相对误差与有效单元努森数成正比；实现了源迭代的严格加速，且加速界对网格尺寸、多项式阶数和单元面数一致；笛卡尔和质心Voronoi网格上的数值实验验证了收敛预测和修正算子标度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多面体网格上高阶DG输运离散的输运匹配DSA理论框架，证明所构造的MIP修正能严格加速源迭代，其收敛界对各类网格参数具有一致性。

Abstract: Diffusion synthetic acceleration is most effective when its diffusion correction reflects the transport discretisation that generates the iteration error. We develop this principle for high-order upwind discontinuous Galerkin discretisations of discrete-ordinates transport on polytopic meshes. From the discrete transport sweep, we derive the exact scalar correction that removes the source-iteration scalar error in one step. We prove that the associated scalar response is positive and self-adjoint, obtain an exact expression for the source-iteration convergence factor, and quantify the additional damping produced by vacuum leakage.
  Using the exact correction as a reference, we construct a transport-matched modified interior penalty correction whose boundary terms are inherited directly from homogeneous vacuum inflow. In the optically thick regime, the resulting MIP form approximates the exact correction with relative error proportional to the effective cell Knudsen number. This gives a strict acceleration of source iteration, with bounds uniform in mesh size, polynomial degree, and element face count for admissible polytopic meshes. Numerical experiments on Cartesian and centroidal Voronoi meshes confirm the predicted convergence and correction-operator scaling.

</details>


### [158] [Conditioning and interpolation error bounds for second-order Stiefel retractions with closed-form inverses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28073)
*Rasmus Jensen,Ralf Zimmermann*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Stiefel流形上的Cayley回缩和polar-light回缩在插值问题中的性质，证明了这两类回缩映射条件良好并推导了显式的插值误差界，同时讨论了逆回缩的局部存在性及其通过等距群作用实现稳定计算的方法，最后通过Hermite插值数值实验展示了基于回缩的插值方法与传统黎曼正规坐标方法的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流形上执行数据插值任务时，黎曼指数映射和对数映射计算成本高昂，而回缩（retraction）提供了计算高效的替代方案。在Stiefel流形上，仅有两类具有闭式逆的二阶回缩（Cayley回缩和polar-light回缩），但它们的插值性质尚不清楚，需要系统研究其条件数与误差界，以验证其在实际插值应用中的可行性。

Method: 本文首先分析Cayley回缩和polar-light回缩及其逆映射的条件数，推导插值误差界；然后通过等距群作用将数据映射到适当的邻域以确保逆回缩计算的稳定性；构造任意点周围的法向邻域以保证逆映射的存在性；最后将回缩应用于Hermite插值问题，同时再现函数值和导数信息。

Result: 证明了Cayley回缩和polar-light回缩是良条件的（well-conditioned），推导出与经典欧氏插值类似的显式插值误差界。逆回缩在一般情况下不是良条件的，但可通过等距群作用保证稳定计算。在紧致流形上，两类逆回缩仅在局部存在，文章构造了保证逆映射可计算的法向邻域。数值实验表明基于回缩的Hermite插值与基于黎曼正规坐标的经典方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 回缩方法为Stiefel流形上的插值问题提供了计算高效且数值稳定的替代方案，具有与经典方法可比的精度，尤其适用于需要同时插值函数值和导数信息的Hermite插值场景。

Abstract: Retractions provide a computationally efficient alternative to the Riemannian exponential and logarithm maps for practical data-processing tasks on manifolds. In particular, second-order retractions with closed-form inverse are well-suited for interpolation problems on manifolds. On the Stiefel manifold of orthogonal frames, there are only two retractions of this type: the Cayley retraction, which is second-order accurate under the canonical metric, and the recently proposed polar-light retraction, which is second-order accurate under the Euclidean metric.
  In this paper, we study the properties of these maps in the context of interpolation on the Stiefel manifold. To obtain explicit interpolation error bounds, we examine the conditioning of the retraction maps and their inverses. We show that the retractions are well-conditioned, and we derive interpolation error bounds similar to those of classical Euclidean interpolation. The inverse retractions are not well-conditioned in general, and we discuss how data can be mapped via an isometric group action to ensure stable computations. As with all retractions on compact manifolds, the inverse canonical Cayley retraction and the inverse polar-light retraction exist only locally, and we construct normal neighborhoods around any point in which either the inverse Cayley retraction or the invese polar-light retraction are guaranteed to be computable.
  As an application of the retraction maps, we consider Hermite interpolation, where the objective is to reproduce both sampled function values and derivative information. A numerical example demonstrates that retraction-based interpolation is competitive with classical methods based on Riemannian normal coordinates.

</details>


### [159] [A Local Discontinuous Galerkin method for the modified Camassa--Holm equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28077)
*Xiang-Ke Chang,Yong Liu,Qi Tao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对修正Camassa-Holm（mCH）方程的局部间断伽辽金（LDG）方法，该方程包含三次非线性和高阶导数项。通过使用守恒通量和耗散通量分别获得了能量守恒和稳定性，并建立了半离散格式的误差估计。数值实验验证了理论结果的锐利性，并表明该方法能够捕捉mCH方程的peakon解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mCH方程含有三次非线性项和高阶导数项，数值求解具有挑战性，需要设计既能保持能量守恒又能保证数值稳定性的高精度数值方法。

Method: 采用局部间断伽辽金（LDG）方法离散mCH方程，选取守恒通量实现能量守恒性质，选取耗散通量保证数值稳定性，并对半离散格式进行误差分析。

Result: 理论证明了LDG方法在守恒通量下具有能量守恒性、在耗散通量下具有稳定性，并获得了半离散格式的误差估计。数值实验表明理论结果是最优（sharp）的，方法能有效捕捉mCH方程的尖峰孤子（peakon）解。

Conclusion: 提出的LDG方法在理论上具备能量守恒和稳定性保证，误差估计成立，在数值上能够准确捕捉mCH方程的peakon解，验证了该方法的有效性和理论结果的锐利性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a local discontinuous Galerkin (LDG) method for the modified Camassa-Holm (mCH) equation that contains cubic nonlinearity and high-order derivative terms. Energy conservation and stability are obtained using the conservative and dissipative fluxes, respectively. The error estimate of the semi-discrete scheme is also established. Numerical examples verify that our theoretical findings are sharp and the proposed LDG method is capable of capturing the peakon solution of the mCH equation.

</details>


### [160] [Inverse Source Problem for a Time-Fractional Diffusion-Wave Equation with a Singular Inverse-Square Potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28103)
*Zewen Wang,Bin Wu,Yikan Liu,Liangwei Jin,Shufang Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究含奇异反平方势的时间分数阶扩散-波动方程的反源问题。在适当的能量框架下建立了正问题适定性和正则性，证明了终端观测算子的紧性及空间源项的唯一性，并提出了基于伴随问题的Tikhonov正则化共轭梯度重建方法，在一维和二维数值实验中验证了算法的有效性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分数阶扩散-波动方程在反常扩散和粘弹性等物理过程中有广泛应用，而带奇异反平方势的此类方程在量子力学和临界现象建模中具有重要意义。从终端观测数据反演未知空间源项属于不适定反问题，需要严格的数学分析和稳定的数值算法，但目前对该类含奇异势的反源问题研究尚不充分。

Method: 首先利用Hardy型不等式和奇异椭圆算子的谱性质，在合适的能量框架下建立正问题的适定性和正则性。随后证明终端观测算子的紧性，并在时间因子的非退化条件下建立空间源项的唯一性。针对不适定反问题引入Tikhonov正则化，通过含右端分数阶导数的伴随问题推导正则化泛函的梯度，进而采用伴随型共轭梯度法配合精确线搜索进行数值求解。

Result: 在一维和二维空间域上，分别使用精确和含噪声的终端数据进行数值实验，结果验证了所提出的源重建方法在恢复未知空间源项方面的有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究为带奇异反平方势的时间分数阶扩散-波动方程反源问题提供了完整的理论分析框架（适定性、唯一性与紧性）和高效的数值算法，所提出的伴随共轭梯度方法在多种数值场景下均表现出良好的重建性能。

Abstract: This paper investigates an inverse source problem for a time-fractional diffusion-wave equation with a singular inverse-square potential. The source term is assumed to consist of a known temporal factor and an unknown spatial component, which is to be recovered from terminal-state measurements. The well-posedness and regularity of the forward problem are established within an appropriate energy framework by exploiting Hardy-type inequalities and the spectral properties of the associated singular elliptic operator. The terminal observation operator is then shown to be compact, and uniqueness of the spatial source is established under a suitable nondegeneracy condition on the temporal factor. To stabilize the resulting ill-posed inverse problem, a Tikhonov regularization approach is introduced. The gradient of the regularized functional is derived through an adjoint problem involving a right-sided fractional derivative, leading to an adjoint-based conjugate gradient method with an exact line search for the numerical reconstruction of the unknown source. Numerical experiments are conducted on both one-and two-dimensional spatial domains, using both exact and noisy terminal data, to demonstrate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed source reconstruction method.

</details>


### [161] [Stochastic trace estimation for positive trace-class operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28135)
*Zvonimir Bujanović,Luka Grubišić,Daniel Kressner,Hrvoje Olić*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文发展了正定迹类算子（可分Hilbert空间上的无限维算子）的隐式迹估计新方法，提出了理想化的 Girard-Hutchinson 和 Hutch++ 无限维类比估计器，证明了高概率误差界，并引入了截断实现用于实际计算。理论结果表明 infHutch++ 达到 O(ε^{-1}) 样本复杂度，数值实验在积分算子、态密度近似和 Dirac 算子谱滤波中表现优于 ContHutch++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的迹估计理论主要集中在有限维矩阵情形（如 Girard-Hutchinson 和 Hutch++），但许多实际问题涉及无限维空间上的线性算子（如积分算子、微分算子的谱滤波等）。需要将有限维的方差缩减迹估计方法推广到无限维希尔伯特空间上的迹类算子，同时保证理论保证和实际可计算性。

Method: (1) 提出理想化无限维估计器：利用协方差由目标算子确定的高斯随机元，构造无偏的 Girard-Hutchinson 和 Hutch++ 无限维类比（infHutch++）；(2) 证明与有限维矩阵情形类似的高概率误差界；(3) 引入截断实现：将随机样本限制在有限维子空间，并证明在样本预算固定时，截断 infHutch++ 随截断维度趋于无穷在分布上收敛到理想版本；(4) 通过数值实验验证方法在积分算子、态密度近似和径向 Dirac 算子谱滤波中的应用。

Result: 理论上证明了理想化 infHutch++ 达到 O(ε^{-1}) 的样本复杂度（与有限维 Hutch++ 一致）；截断实现保持分布收敛性。数值实验表明所提截断估计器在使用更低阶函数表示和更小内部离散化（chebfun）的条件下，可达与 ContHutch++ 相当的精度。

Conclusion: 该工作将方差缩减迹估计从有限维矩阵成功推广到无限维希尔伯特空间的迹类算子，提供了完整的理论保证和实用的截断实现路径，为函数空间中的算子迹估计提供了新工具，在数值分析和谱计算领域有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Implicit trace estimation aims to approximate the trace of a matrix or linear operator accessible only through matrix-vector or operator-vector products. In the matrix setting, the Girard-Hutchinson estimator typically requires $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ products to achieve accuracy $\varepsilon$, while the variance-reduced Hutch++ algorithm reduces this sample complexity
  to $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1})$ for positive semidefinite matrices. We develop infinite-dimensional analogues of these estimators for positive trace-class operators on separable Hilbert spaces. The idealized estimators use Gaussian random elements whose covariance is determined by the target operator, leading to unbiased operator versions of Girard-Hutchinson and Hutch++. We prove high-probability error bounds analogous to the finite-dimensional matrix results; in particular, idealized infHutch++ achieves $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1})$ sample complexity.
  For practical computation, we introduce truncated implementations that restrict the random samples to finite-dimensional subspaces; for fixed sample budget, we show that truncated infHutch++ converges in distribution to its idealized counterpart as the truncation dimension tends to infinity. Numerical experiments with integral operators, density-of-states approximations, and spectral filtering for a radial Dirac operator show that these truncated estimators can achieve accuracy comparable to the ContHutch++ algorithm by Zvonek, Horning & Townsend while using lower-degree function representations and smaller internal discretizations in chebfun.

</details>


### [162] [Conformal Uncertainty Quantification Guarantees for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28515)
*Tom Stent,Nicolas Boullé*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分裂共形框架，用于为神经算子预测提供带不确定性量化的点带，保证在评估域上至少1-γ比例的区域内包含真实解，且置信度至少为1-α。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为函数空间之间的快速代理模型，其预测往往缺乏不确定性量化，这在科学计算等安全关键应用中是不可接受的。现有的校正方法要么覆盖性无法保证，要么带过宽、过于保守。

Method: 将归一化残差场降至其空间(1-γ)分位数，并利用独立的校准数据集计算缩放因子。在任意概率空间上定义的可测残差场上证明边际覆盖保证，覆盖连续域和固定离散化情形。

Result: 在温和假设下，校准集上的覆盖条件符合Beta分布。在Darcy流和Navier-Stokes方程的数值实验验证中，该方法得到的带比现有校正方法更紧凑，同时保持目标覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该共形校准方法为神经算子提供了有理论保证且更紧致的不确定性带，兼顾了统计覆盖保证与实用带宽效率。

Abstract: Neural operators provide fast surrogate models for approximating operators between function spaces, but their predictions often lack uncertainty quantification. We develop a split conformal framework to guarantee that a calibrated pointwise band around the neural operator output contains the true solution on at least a $1-γ$ fraction of the evaluation domain, with probability at least $1-α$ over test and calibration inputs, where $α,γ\in(0,1)$. Our method reduces a normalized residual field to its spatial $(1-γ)$-quantile and computes a scaling factor using a held-out calibration dataset. We prove marginal coverage guarantees for measurable residual fields defined on arbitrary probability spaces, covering both continuum domains and fixed discretizations. Under mild assumptions on the data distribution, we show that the coverage conditional on the calibration set follows a Beta distribution, which we verify with numerical experiments on Darcy flow and Navier--Stokes equations, where our calibration yields bands consistently tighter than existing corrections while retaining the target coverage.

</details>


### [163] [Closest Normal Matrix Found Again Using Riemannian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28545)
*Vanni Noferini,Matvei Zhukov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于黎曼优化的方法，计算给定矩阵的最近正规矩阵。问题可表述为酉群U(n)或旗流形U(n)/U(1)^n上的光滑函数最小化。作者在旗流形上刻画了目标函数的全局最大值，并证明对一般输入，局部极小值点有限且孤立，从而意味着原始最近正规矩阵问题一般具有有限个特征值互异的局部极小值点。同时开发了黎曼信赖域方法，显著优于经典算法，可处理更大规模矩阵，并给出计算最近实正规矩阵的变体，配以大量数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近正规矩阵问题在数值线性代数中具有重要地位，但经典算法效率有限且难以处理大规模矩阵。现有方法缺乏对问题解的全局与局部结构的严格理论分析，需要提出更好的优化框架并建立收敛性质的理论保证。

Method: 将最近正规矩阵问题分别转化为酉流形U(n)与旗流形U(n)/U(1)^n上的光滑最小化问题；在旗流形上进行理论分析，刻画全局最大值并证明一般输入下局部极小值点有限且孤立；开发基于黎曼信赖域（Riemannian trust-region）的数值算法，并扩展至实正规矩阵情形。

Result: 理论方面：在旗流形上找到了目标函数的全局最大值并证明了局部极小值点的有限性与孤立性；对一般输入，最近正规矩阵问题存在有限个局部极小值点且其矩阵特征值互异。数值方面：黎曼信赖域方法相比经典算法有实质性提升，能够处理显著更大的矩阵规模。

Conclusion: 黎曼优化框架为最近正规矩阵问题提供了既具理论深度又具实用效率的求解方案，旗流形上的几何分析揭示了问题的全局结构，而黎曼信赖域算法在效率和可扩展性上优于经典方法。

Abstract: We propose an approach based on Riemannian optimization to compute a nearest normal matrix to a given one. The problem can be formulated as the minimization of a smooth function either on the manifold $U(n)$ of unitary matrices of size n or on the flag manifold $U (n)/U (1)^n$. The flag manifold is particularly suitable for theoretical analysis; we characterize the global maximum of the objective function and prove that, for generic inputs, its local minimizers are finitely many and isolated; in turn, this implies the original nearest normal matrix problem generically has finitely many local minimizers, all with distinct eigenvalues. We also develop a Riemannian trust-region method that improves substantially on classical algorithms and can handle considerably larger matrices, as well as a variant for computing the nearest real normal matrix. The paper is complemented by extensive numerical experiments.

</details>
